<h1><a href='../../../translations.html'>The Great Parinirvāṇa</a></h1>
<h2>An Excerpt from<br/>the <em>Kṣudrakavastu</em> of the <em>Mūlasarvāstivāda Vinaya</em><br/>(fascicles 35-39)</h2>

<hr/>

<table class="trans">
  <th class='ch'></th>
  <th class='en'></th>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382b29'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>1. Varṣākāra’s Visit</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382b29'>緣在王舍城，住鷲峯山。 時，摩揭陀主、未生怨王與佛栗氏國共相違逆。 未生怨王於大眾中告諸人曰： 「安隱、豐樂，與我相違。 我欲興兵而往討罰。 皆令破散。」</td>
    <td id='p1'>1. It happened that the Buddha was staying on Mount Gṛdhrakūṭa of Rājagṛha.<sup id="ref1"><a href="#n1">1</a></sup> Then, the Lord of Magadha King Ajātaśatru was in conflict with the Buddhist country of Vṛji. King Ajātaśatru told everyone in a large gathering: “They are secure and prosperous, and they oppose me. I’m going to raise an army and send it to punish them. They’ll be shattered.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c3'>王告大臣行雨婆羅門言： 「卿往佛所頂禮佛足。 為我問訊： 『起居輕利？少病少惱？氣力安不？』 次復白言： 『大德，未生怨王對諸眾前作如是語： 「彼國豐樂與我相違。 我欲興兵而往討罰。 皆令破散。」 世尊許不？』 如世尊記，皆當領受還來報我。 何以故？ 如來、應、正遍知者言無虛妄。」</td>
    <td id='p2'>2. The King told his prime minister, the brahmin Varṣākāra, “You will go to the Buddha and bow your head at his feet. Ask him how he is doing for me: ‘Is it easy for you to get up and around? Are you seldom ill and have few troubles? Are you healthy, strong, and comfortable?’ Next, say this to him, ‘Venerable, King Ajātaśatru has said this in front of an assembly: “That country is prosperous and opposes me. I will raise an army and send it to punish them. They will be shattered.” Does the Bhagavān approve of this?’ Whatever the Bhagavān predicts, remember what he says and report it back to me. Why is that? The words of the Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One are never false.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c9'>是時行雨奉王教已，乘白馬車執持金杖，掛以金瓶。 出王舍城，往詣佛所。 至下車處，足步而行登鷲峰山。 至世尊所歡顏敬問。 在一面坐白言： 「世尊，摩揭陀主未生怨王，頂禮世尊足下。 敬問起居輕利，少病少惱，氣力安不？」</td>
    <td id='p3'>3. After he accepted the King’s instructions, Varṣākāra rode a carriage with white horses and carried a golden staff with a golden jar hanging from it. He left Rājagṛha and went to the Buddha. Once he arrived, he dismounted from his carriage and climbed Mount Gṛdhrakūṭa on foot. When he reached the Bhagavān, he respectfully made inquiries with a glad expression. He then sat to one side and said, “Bhagavān, the Lord of Magadha King Ajātaśatru bows his head at the Bhagavān’s feet. He respectfully asks if it’s easy for you to get up and around? Are you seldom ill and have few troubles? Are you healthy, strong, and comfortable?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c14'>作是語已，佛告婆羅門： 「願王及汝無病安樂。」</td>
    <td>After he said this, the Buddha told the brahmin, “I hope the king and you are healthy and happy.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c15'>時婆羅門即以王語次第，白佛廣陳其事： 「未審世尊作何垂誨？」</td>
    <td id='p4'>4. The brahmin then told the Buddha about the matter the king said next in detail. [Then he asked,] “Would the Bhagavān have any instruction to offer about this?”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c16'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>2. Seven Ways the Vṛji Will Not Retreat</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c16'>佛告婆羅門： 「我不多時在佛栗氏國，曾於三月坐夏之時於彼而住。 我時為眾宣說七種不退轉法。 婆羅門，彼國諸人護持七種不退法時，國界人民日見增長，善法無損。」</td>
    <td id='p5'>5. The Buddha told the brahmin,<sup id="ref2"><a href="#n2">2</a></sup> “I haven’t been in the Buddhist country of Vṛji many times, but I did stay there during the last summer retreat for three months. While I was there, I lectured on the seven ways to not retreat for that community.<sup id="ref3"><a href="#n3">3</a></sup> Brahmin, when the people of that country take care to remember those seven ways to not retreat, their population will increase daily, and they won’t lose any of the good Dharma.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c20'>婆羅門言： 「我未能解大德所陳要妙之義。 唯願慈悲，廣為我說，令得開解。」</td>
    <td>The brahmin said, “I’ve yet to understand the essential meaning of the venerable’s statement. Please take pity on me and explain it fully so that I might understand it.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c23'>爾時具壽阿難陀在佛後立執扇招涼。 佛告阿難陀： 「汝頗聞知佛栗氏國所有人民，數多聚集評論法義不？」</td>
    <td id='p6'>6. (1) The elder Ānanda was standing behind the Buddha at the time, fanning him to keep him cool. The Buddha addressed Ānanda, “Have you heard that the people of the Buddhist country of Vṛji hold frequent meetings to discuss the Dharma and its meaning?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c25'>「大德，我聞彼國人多聚集評論法義。」</td>
    <td>“Venerable, I’ve heard that the people of that country hold many meetings to discuss the Dharma and its meaning.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c26'>佛告婆羅門： 「若彼國中人多聚集評論法義，應知彼國日見增長，善法無損(一了)。</td>
    <td>The Buddha told the brahmin, “If the people in that country hold many meetings to discuss the Dharma and its meaning, you should know that they will grow daily, and they won’t lose any of the good Dharma.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c28'>「阿難陀，汝頗聞知佛栗氏國，人多和合，同起同坐，評論國事？」</td>
    <td id='p7'>7. (2) “Ānanda, have you heard that the people of the Buddhist country of Vṛji often come together, rising and sitting the same and discussing the affairs of state?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c29'>答言： 「我聞⋯」</td>
    <td>He answered, “I have heard that …”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.382c29'>廣說如上，佛告婆羅門亦具如上說，乃至⋯ 「善法無損」(二了)。</td>
    <td><em>Recited in full as above, the Buddha telling the brahmin the same as above up to</em> … “and they won’t lose any of the good Dharma.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a1'>「阿難陀，汝頗聞知彼國人眾，不應求事而不求之，所應得事不令斷絕，國之教令常樂奉行？」</td>
    <td id='p8'>8. (3) “Ānanda, have you heard or know that the people of that country don’t pursue what they shouldn’t pursue, that they don’t bring an end to something that ought to be done, and that they are always happy with and approving of the country’s teachings?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a4'>答言： 「我聞⋯」</td>
    <td>He answered, “I have heard that …”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a4'>廣說如上，佛告婆羅門亦具如上說，乃至⋯ 「善法無損」(三了)。</td>
    <td><em>Recited in full as above, the Buddha telling the brahmin the same as above up to</em> … “and they won’t lose any of the good Dharma.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a5'>「阿難陀，汝頗聞知彼國女人及童女類，或是母護、父護、兄弟、姊妹、姑嫜、親族，而相擁護有過訓罰？ 是他妻妾乃至授花許為其婦，不共倉卒行非法事？」</td>
    <td id='p9'>9. (4) “Ānanda, have you heard or know that women and girls of that country, whether they are under the protection of their mothers, fathers, brothers, sisters, aunts, uncles, or other relatives, are safeguarded and their violators punished? That they don’t make the wives of others, concubines … <em>up to</em> … or women who are betrothed<sup id="ref4"><a href="#n4">4</a></sup> become their wives by force or immoral means?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a8'>答言： 「我聞⋯」</td>
    <td>He answered, “I have heard that …”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a9'>廣說如上，佛告婆羅門亦具如上說，乃至⋯ 「善法無損」(四了)。</td>
    <td><em>Recited in full as above, the Buddha telling the brahmin the same as above up to</em> … “and they won’t lose any of the good Dharma.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a10'>「阿難陀，汝頗聞知彼國人眾，於其父母師長之處，恭敬、供養、隨順言教，情無違惱？」</td>
    <td id='p10'>10. (5) “Ānanda, have you heard or know that the people of that country respect, support, and comply with the instructions of their parents and teachers with whom they live, without any disobedience?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a12'>答言：「我聞⋯」</td>
    <td>He answered, “I have heard that …”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a12'>廣說如上。佛告婆羅門亦具如上說，乃至⋯ 「善法無損」(五了)。</td>
    <td><em>Recited in full as above, the Buddha telling the brahmin the same as above up to</em> … “and they won’t lose any of the good Dharma.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a13'>「阿難陀，汝頗聞知彼國人眾，於制底處常修供養所有古舊，恭敬法式不令虧廢？」</td>
    <td id='p11'>11. (6) “Ānanda, have you heard or know that the people of that country always give offerings at shrines as they’ve done in ancient times and that they respect the law and prevent it from falling into disuse?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a15'>廣說乃至⋯ 「善法無損」(六了)。</td>
    <td><em>Recited in full up to</em> … “and they won’t lose any of the good Dharma.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a15'>「阿難陀，汝頗聞知彼國人眾，於阿羅漢敬心、慇重、常生正念？ 其未來者願皆來此，其已來者得安隱住？ 衣服、飲食、臥具、醫藥所須資具皆悉給與無有乏少？」</td>
    <td id='p12'>12. (7) “Ānanda, have you heard or know that the people of that country esteem, honor, and are always properly mindful of the <em>arhat</em>s? Do they hope that the <em>arhat</em>s will go there in the future and that those who are there now live in safety? Do they provide all the clothing, meals, lodging, and medicines that the <em>arhat</em>s require so that they haven’t the slightest want?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a19'>廣說乃至⋯ 「善法無損」(七了)。</td>
    <td><em>Recited in full up to</em> … “and they won’t lose any of the good Dharma.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a19'>佛告婆羅門： 「但令彼國所有人眾，於斯七種不退轉法修行之時，當知彼國常得增長，無有損失，善法隆盛。」</td>
    <td id='p13'>13. The Buddha told the brahmin, “As long as the people of that country put these seven ways to not retreat into practice, you should know that their country can always grow without any decline, and the good Dharma will prosper.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a22'>婆羅門言： 「大德，彼國人眾於七法中隨行其一，未生怨王不應興罰。 何況七法具足奉行。」</td>
    <td id='p14'>14. The brahmin said, “Venerable, should the people of that country follow one of these seven things, King Ajātaśatru shouldn’t raise [an army] to punish them. How would it be if they put all seven things into practice?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a24'>婆羅門曰： 「大德喬答摩，我有多緣，且欲辭去。」</td>
    <td id='p15'>15. The brahmin said, “Venerable Gautama, I have many duties, so I would like to take my leave.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a25'>佛言： 「隨意。」</td>
    <td>The Buddha said, “Do what you will.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a25'>時，婆羅門聞佛所說歡喜，奉行。</td>
    <td id='p16'>16. When the brahmin heard what the Buddha taught, he rejoiced and approved.<sup id="ref5"><a href="#n5">5</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a27'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>3. The Ways to Prevent the Decline of the Dharma</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a27'>時婆羅門辭佛去後，佛告阿難陀： 「汝可遍告鷲峯山處所有苾芻，皆令集在供侍堂中。」</td>
    <td id='p17'>17. After that brahmin had departed from his audience with the Buddha, the Buddha told Ānanda, “You can announce to all the monks who are staying on Mount Gṛdhrakūṭa that they are to assemble in the temple hall.”<sup id="ref6"><a href="#n6">6</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383a28'>時阿難陀即便，遍告諸苾芻眾盡集堂已，還至佛所，在一面立白言： 「世尊，苾芻盡集。 願佛知時。」</td>
    <td id='p18'>18. Once he had announced to the community of monks that they were to assemble in the hall, Ānanda returned to the Buddha, stood to one side, and said, “Bhagavān, all the monks have assembled. The Buddha may go when he decides it’s time.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383b2'>佛至堂所就座坐已，告諸苾芻： 「我今為汝說七不虧損法。 汝等諦聽極善作意。 云何為七？ 汝等苾芻數多集會評論法義，應知苾芻福德增長，善法無損(一了)。</td>
    <td id='p19'>19. Once he had arrived at the hall, the Buddha prepared a seat, sat down, and then told the monks, “Now, I will teach you the seven ways to not decline. All of you, listen closely and think on it well. What are the seven? (1) When all of you monks hold frequent meetings to discuss the meaning of the Dharma, you should know that the monks’ virtues will grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.<sup id="ref7"><a href="#n7">7</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383b5'>「汝等苾芻，若和合同集，同起、同坐、同作法事，應知福德增長，善法無損(二了)。</td>
    <td id='p20'>20. “(2) When all of you monks come together and unite, rising the same, sitting the same, and performing the same Dharma duties, you should know that your virtues will grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383b7'>「汝等苾芻不應求者而勿苦求，所應得者不令斷絕，所有正教常樂奉行。 如是當知福德增長，善法無損(三了)。</td>
    <td id='p21'>21. “(3) Suppose all of you monks don’t take pains to seek what shouldn’t be sought, don’t allow what you should obtain to end, and always delight in and approve of the correct teaching that you have. In this way, you should know that your virtues will grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383b9'>「汝等苾芻，所有愛著與貪俱生喜，願未來諸有相續。 由此輪轉。 此若除者，如是當知得安樂住。 令諸苾芻福德增長，善法無損(四了)。</td>
    <td id='p22'>22. “(4) You monks, when someone has delights that arise from craving existence together with attachment and greed, they will aspire to continue to future existences. The wheel [of rebirth] turns on account of this. You should know that someone who eliminates this will live happily. This will make the monks’ virtues grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383b12'>「汝等苾芻，若有苾芻久事出家，修淨梵行滿二十夏。 耆年宿德大師所讚，為同梵行者之所識知。 眾皆恭敬殷重供養，所說言教樂共聽聞。 如是當知福德增長，善法無損(五了)。</td>
    <td id='p23'>23. “(5) You monks, suppose there are monks who left home a long time ago, who have cultivated the religious practice for a full twenty summers. They are praised by senior and virtuous great teachers, and they are well-known to those of the same religious practice. The community respects, honors, and supports them, and they are delighted to listen to what those senior monks say and teach. In this way, you should know that their virtues will grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383b16'>「汝等苾芻，若有苾芻居阿蘭若，受下臥具生喜足心。 如是當知福德增長，善法無損(六了)。</td>
    <td id='p24'>24. “(6) You monks, suppose there are monks who live in the forest and are satisfied with finding a place to sleep there. You should know in this way that their virtues will grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383b18'>「汝等苾芻，若有苾芻於同梵行者殷重用心，常存正念。 欲令不來同梵行者而來至此，既來至已作安樂住心不生厭。 於新衣服飲食臥具醫藥所須資具，皆悉給與勿令少乏⋯廣說乃至⋯ 善法無損(七了)。</td>
    <td id='p25'>25. “(7) You monks, suppose a monk who is equal in the religious practice seriously applies his mind and continuously in right mindfulness. He would like those of the same religious practice to come who haven’t yet and to ensure those who have come will be happy and not get tired of being there. He wants them to be provided with provisions of new robes, meals, bedding, and medicines that they may need so that they have not the slightest want … <em>recited in full up to</em> … and the good Dharma won’t diminish.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383b22'>「汝等苾芻，能行如是七種法時，當知苾芻所有善法常得增長。 無有虧損安樂而住。</td>
    <td id='p26'>26. “You monks, when you practice in these seven ways, you should know that the good Dharma possessed by the monks will always grow. Nothing will ruin their happiness.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383b25'>「汝等苾芻，復有七種不虧損法。 汝等應聽。 云何為七？ 若諸苾芻於大師處恭敬、供養、尊重、讚歎，如是作時得安樂住。 令諸苾芻眾得增長，善法無損(一了)。 如是應知，於法⋯於戒⋯於教授事⋯不放逸事⋯於臥具事⋯於修定事。 生殷重心、恭敬、供養，如是作時得安樂住。 令諸苾芻眾得增長，善法無損(七了)。</td>
    <td id='p27'>27. “You monks, there are another seven ways to not decline.<sup id="ref8"><a href="#n8">8</a></sup> All of you should listen. What are the seven? (1) If the monks are respectful, offer support, honor, and praise a great teacher while they are at his abode, they will live happily while doing so. They will allow the community of monks to grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish. In this way, you should know (2) the Dharma … (3) the precepts … (4) matters of instruction … (5) matters of carefulness … (6) matters of sleeping quarters … (7) matters of cultivating <em>samādhi</em>. Becoming fervent, they pay their respects and offer support, and they will live happily while doing so. This will allow the community of monks to grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383c3'>「汝等苾芻，復有七種不虧損法。 汝等應聽。 云何為七？ 若諸苾芻不愛作業⋯不愛言談⋯不著睡眠⋯不樂聚集及近惡友⋯不貪名利參問他人⋯常修於定，於增上證不生喜足、無退屈心⋯乃至⋯證得真實諦來無暫休息。 如是作時得安樂住。 令諸苾芻眾得增長，善法無損(七了)。</td>
    <td id='p28'>28. “You monks, there are another seven ways to not decline. All of you should listen. What are the seven? (1) If the monks don’t crave to work … (2) don’t crave conversation … (3) aren’t attached to sleep … (4) don’t enjoy gatherings or (5) being close to bad friends … (6) don’t covet fame or benefits from questioning other people … (7) regularly cultivate <em>samādhi</em>, they won’t become satisfied or have thoughts of giving up regarding the higher realization … <em>up to</em> … realization of the real truth comes without break or pause. In this way, they will live happily while doing so. This will allow the community of monks to grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383c8'>「汝等苾芻，復有七種不虧損法。 汝等應聽。 云何為七？ 若有苾芻有淨信心⋯有慚⋯有愧⋯具大精勤⋯有念⋯定⋯慧，如是作時得安樂住。 令諸苾芻眾得增長，善法無損(七了)。</td>
    <td id='p29'>29. “You monks, there are another seven ways to not decline. All of you should listen. What are the seven? (1) If there are monks with pure faith … (2) modesty … (3) conscience … (4) great diligence … (5) mindfulness … (6) <em>samādhi</em> … (7) wisdom, they will live happily while doing so. This will allow the community of monks to grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383c12'>「汝等苾芻，復有七種不虧損法。 汝等應聽。 云何為七？ 知法、知義、知時、知量、知自身、知門徒、知他人行。 如是作時得安樂住，令諸苾芻眾得增長，善法無損(七了)。</td>
    <td id='p30'>30. “You monks, there are another seven ways to not decline. All of you should listen. What are the seven? (1) Knowing the Dharma, (2) knowing the meaning, (3) knowing the time, (4) knowing limits, (5) knowing oneself, (6) knowing disciples, and (7) knowing the practice of other people. They will live happily while doing these things. This will allow the community of monks to grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383c15'>「汝等苾芻，復有七法。 云何為七？ 若有苾芻修念覺分觀時，依空閑處依止離欲、依止寂滅遠離災難⋯ 如是法、勤、喜、安、定、捨⋯ 修觀之時依空閑處、依止離欲、依止寂滅遠離災難。 如是作時得安樂住。 令諸苾芻眾得增長，善法無損。</td>
    <td id='p31'>31. “You monks, there are another seven things. What are the seven? Suppose there are monks who contemplate while cultivating the awakening factor of mindfulness supported by an empty and secluded place, supported by seclusion from desires, and supported by the tranquility of being far away from troubles … <em>and so on for teachings, diligence, joy, calm, </em>samādhi<em>, and equanimity</em> … who contemplate while cultivating [the awakening factor of equanimity] supported by an empty and secluded place, supported by seclusion from desires, and supported by the tranquility of being far away from troubles. They will live happily while doing so. This will allow the community of monks to grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383c20'>「汝等苾芻，是謂七法，無有退轉應常修習。 汝等一心慇懃守護。 令諸苾芻眾得增長，善法無損。</td>
    <td id='p32'>32. “You monks, these are the seven things that should always be cultivated without turning back. All of you, be diligent and protect them wholeheartedly. This will allow the community of monks to grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.383c23'>「汝等苾芻，復有六法令他歡喜。 汝應諦聽，我當為說。 云何為六？ 一者： 『我今應以身業行慈。 謂於大師所及諸賢聖同梵行處，起慈善心以身禮敬。 灑掃塗拭作曼荼羅，布列眾華，燒香供養。 或復為其按摩手足，若見病苦隨時供給。』 如是作時令他歡喜。 愛念敬重共相親附。 和合，攝受，無諸違諍。 一心同事如水乳合。</td>
    <td id='p33'>33. “You monks, there are another six things that bring joy to others.<sup id="ref9"><a href="#n9">9</a></sup> You should listen closely, and I will teach them for you. What are the six? The first is this: ‘Now, I should be kind in my physical conduct. That is to say, I will produce kind and skillful thoughts about the great teacher and the nobles who live in the same religious practice by venerating them with my body. I will clean and wash a circular area,<sup id="ref10"><a href="#n10">10</a></sup> scattering many flowers and burning incense as offerings there. I might also support them by rubbing their hands and feet at appropriate times if I see them suffering an illness.’ This will bring joy to others when one does so. You’ll have mutual affection, mindfulness, and respect for those close to you. You’ll be aligned, cooperative, and have no conflicts. You’ll be wholehearted in performing the same duties like water and milk combined.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384a1'>「二者： 『我今應以語業行慈。 謂於大師所及諸賢聖同梵行處，起慈善心以語讚歎。 彰其實德，他不聞者令其普知。 讀誦經典，晝夜無歇。』 如是作時令他歡喜。 愛念敬重共相親附。 和合攝受無諸違諍。 一心同事如水乳合。</td>
    <td id='p34'>34. “Second: ‘Now, I should act kindly with my verbal conduct. That is to say, I will produce kind and skillful thoughts about the great teacher and the nobles who live in the same religious practice by praising them with my speech. I will make their genuine virtues known so that people everywhere who haven’t heard about them will know. I will read and recite the <em>sūtra</em> canon<sup id="ref11"><a href="#n11">11</a></sup> day and night without stopping.’ This will bring joy to others when one does so. You’ll have mutual affection, mindfulness, and respect for those close to you. You’ll be aligned, cooperative, and have no conflicts. You’ll be wholehearted in performing the same duties like water and milk combined.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384a6'>「三者： 『我今應以意業行慈。 謂於賢聖同梵行處起慈善心。 不生妬害慳嫉之想，於身語業所有行慈。 繫念思惟無令斷絕。 設在危難，亦不暫停。 況復平居而乖正念？ 於諸含識起悲愍心，不斷其命不行楚苦。 遠離煩惱至解脫處。』 如是作時令他歡喜。 愛念敬重共相親附。 和合攝受無諸違諍。 一心同事如水乳合。</td>
    <td id='p35'>35. “Third: ‘Now, I should act kindly with my mental conduct. That is to say, I will produce kind and skillful thoughts about [the great teacher and] the nobles who live in the same religious practice. Not giving rise to ideas that are envious, harmful, stingy, or jealous, I will act with kindness regarding my bodily and verbal deeds. I will tie my thoughts to contemplation, and I won’t allow them to stop. Even if some disaster happens, I would not stop temporarily. How could I go against right mindfulness while in a peaceful place? I will produce sympathetic thoughts about sentient beings, and I won’t end their lives or do painful things to them. I will stay far away from mental affliction and reach the place of liberation.’ This will bring joy to others when one does so. You’ll have mutual affection, mindfulness, and respect for those close to you. You’ll be aligned, cooperative, and have no conflicts. You’ll be wholehearted in performing the same duties like water and milk combined.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384a13'>「四者： 諸有所得如法利養，乃至鉢中獲少飲食，悉皆歡喜共他受用不屏處食。 於同梵行者情無彼此。 如是作時令他歡喜。 愛念敬重共相親附。 和合攝受無諸違諍。 一心同事如水乳合。</td>
    <td id='p36'>36. “Fourth: Everyone gladly shares with others whatever benefits that have been gained in accord with the Dharma, even if it’s a small amount of food from one’s bowl, without excluding anyone from making use of alms that are received. Those of the same religious practice will feel there is no here or there. This will bring joy to others when one does so. You’ll have mutual affection, mindfulness, and respect for those close to you. You’ll be aligned, cooperative, and have no conflicts. You’ll be wholehearted in performing the same duties like water and milk combined.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384a18'>「五者： 於所受戒，不破不穴不雜不垢不穢，初後淨持智人所讚。 同梵行者不生輕鄙，共持淨戒法食俱同。 如是作時令他歡喜⋯廣說乃至⋯ 如水乳合。</td>
    <td id='p37'>37. “Fifth: Not breaking, not making holes in, not adulterating, not defiling, and not polluting the precepts that have been accepted, wise people commend it when they are purely observed from beginning to end. Those of the same religious practice won’t become mean but equal in their observance of the pure precepts, Dharma, and alms. This will bring joy to others when one does so … <em>recited in full up to</em> … like water and milk combined.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384a22'>「六者： 能生正見無有疑惑是聖出離無能破壞，速盡苦邊，與同梵行者共同此見。 如是作時令他歡喜⋯廣說乃至⋯ 如水乳合。</td>
    <td id='p38'>38. “Sixth: Producing right view without any uncertainty about this noble escape which cannot be broken, one will quickly reach the end of suffering, and those of the same religious practice will share this same view. This will bring joy to others when one does so … <em>recited in full up to</em> … like water and milk combined.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384a25'>「汝等苾芻，是謂六種歡喜之法。 應常修習慇懃守護。 令諸苾芻眾得增長，善法無損。」</td>
    <td id='p39'>39. “You monks, these are the six joyful things. They should always be cultivated and diligently protected. This will allow the community of monks to grow, and the good Dharma won’t diminish.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384a27'>時諸眾聞佛說已，皆悉歡喜、信受、奉行。</td>
    <td id='p40'>40. When the assembly heard what the Buddha taught, all of them were delighted by, believed, and approved it.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384a28'>根本說一切有部毘奈耶 雜事 卷第三十五</td>
    <td class="fasc_heading"></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384b2'>根本說一切有部毘奈耶 雜事 卷第三十六</td>
    <td class="fasc_heading"></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384b5'>三藏法師義淨奉　制譯</td>
    <td class="taisho_byline"></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384b6'>第八門第十子攝頌之二</td>
    <td class="ch_heading"></td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384b7'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>4. Teaching the Laity at Pāṭali about Carefulness</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384b7'>內攝頌曰：</td>
    <td>The inner summary verse says:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.384b8'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>眾集敬大師，<br/>
    聞法生正信。<br/>
    自述年衰老，<br/>
    說行雨因緣。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>They gathered to respect the great teacher,<br/>
    And hearing the Dharma produced right faith.<br/>
    Describing his own weakness and old age,<br/>
    Varṣākāra was taught causes and conditions.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384b10'>爾時，世尊告具壽阿難陀曰： 「我今欲往波吒離邑。」 阿難陀言：「如是，世尊，」即與諸苾芻隨從世尊，發摩揭陀國。 漸次遊行，至波吒離邑，住制底邊。</td>
    <td id='p41'>41. It was then that the Bhagavān addressed Venerable Ānanda, “Now, I will go to the town of Pāṭali.”<sup id="ref12"><a href="#n12">12</a></sup> Ānanda replied, “Yes, Bhagavān,” and then the monks followed the Bhagavān as he set out from Magadha. They made their way to the town of Pāṭali and stayed on the outskirts.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384b13'>時，彼邑人聞佛來至，悉皆聚會至制底處。 詣世尊所頂禮雙足退坐一面。</td>
    <td id='p42'>42. Then, the people of that town heard that the Buddha had arrived, and they all assembled together and went to that place on the outskirts. When they arrived, they bowed their heads to the Buddha’s feet and withdrew to sit to one side.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384b14'>爾時，世尊告諸婆羅門、長者、居士曰： 「汝等應知放逸之事有五過失。 云何為五？ 一者若婆羅門等為放逸時，以此因緣所有財寶受用之物悉皆散失。 二者若放逸人，以此因緣，凡所趣向眾會之處，情生愧𧹞又懷怯懼。 三者若放逸人，以此因緣，有惡名稱流遍四方。 四者若放逸人，以此因緣，臨命終時心生悔恨。 五者若放逸人，以此因緣，命終之後墮於地獄、餓鬼、傍生。 是謂五種放逸之過。</td>
    <td id='p43'>43. It was then that the Bhagavān told those brahmins, prominent men, and householders, “All of you should know that being negligent in your duties has five faults.<sup id="ref13"><a href="#n13">13</a></sup> What are the five? First, when brahmins and others are negligent, it will be a cause for the wealth they possess and possessions they use to be lost. Second, if people are negligent, it will be a cause for often feeling ashamed and apprehensive when they go to an assembly. Third, if people are negligent, it will be a cause for having a bad reputation that spreads everywhere in the four directions. Fourth, if people are negligent, it will be a cause for having bitter regrets when they are about to die. Fifth, if people are negligent, it will be a cause for falling to hell, hungry ghosts, or animals after one dies. These are said to be the five faults of negligence.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384b24'>「復次，若婆羅門等行不放逸時，有五勝利。 云何為五？ 一者所有財寶受用之物皆不散失。 二者凡所趣向眾會之處，情無愧𧹞亦無怯懼。 三者有善名稱流遍四方。 四者臨命終時不生悔恨。 五者命終之後生於天上長受安樂。 是謂五種行不放逸利益之事。」</td>
    <td id='p44'>44. “Furthermore, when brahmins and others take care in their conduct, there are five successes. What are the five? First, what wealth they possess and possessions they use won’t be lost. Second, they will usually feel no shame or apprehension when they go to an assembly. Third, they will have a good reputation that spreads everywhere in the four directions. Fourth, they won’t have bitter regrets when they are about to die. Fifth, they will be born up in heaven and experience happiness for a long time after they die. These are said to be the five successes of taking care in one’s conduct.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384b29'>爾時世尊為波吒離邑諸婆羅門等，演說法要示教利喜已，默然而住。</td>
    <td id='p45'>45. After he gave a talk on the essentials of the Dharma for the brahmins and others of Pāṭali, having taught it plainly, benefited, and gladdened them, he fell silent and waited.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384c2'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>5. The Spirits Settling in Pāṭali</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384c2'>諸婆羅門等即從坐起，偏袒右肩右膝著地。 合掌向佛白言： 「世尊，願佛慈悲哀受我等，晝日遊從閑靜房舍。」</td>
    <td id='p46'>46. The brahmins and others then rose from their seats, adjusted their robes to bare their right shoulders, and knelt their right knees to the ground. They then said to the Buddha with their palms together, “Bhagavān, please let the Buddha have mercy and accept our [invitation to stay] at our quiet building during the day while he tours.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384c4'>爾時，世尊默然為受。 諸婆羅門等知佛受已，頂禮佛足奉辭而去。 諸人去後佛即詣彼閑靜住處。 既至彼已，即於房外洗足已，入室宴坐。</td>
    <td id='p47'>47. The Bhagavān then silently accepted [their invitation]. Once they knew the Buddha had accepted, the brahmins and others bowed their heads at his feet, paid their respects, and departed. After they had left, the Buddha went to that quiet residence. Once he was there, he washed his feet outside the hut and went inside to sit in repose.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384c7'>時摩揭陀國行雨大臣，便於波吒離邑，四邊量度廣立封疆，欲造城隍將罰佛栗氏國。 時此邑中，有大勢力威德天神，各求住處。</td>
    <td id='p48'>48. The prime minister of Magadha Varṣākāra was assessing the perimeter of Pāṭali on all four sides while planning to build a city ditch for an attack on the Buddhist country of Vṛji. There were then powerful and dignified heavenly spirits in this town, each of whom was looking for a residence.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384c10'>爾時世尊於宴坐處，即以天眼過於人天，觀彼天神各求住處。 乃於晡時從宴坐起，詣清涼處坐告阿難陀曰： 「汝豈不聞量度城邑。」</td>
    <td id='p49'>49. At the time, the Bhagavān was sitting in repose and observed those heavenly spirits each looking for a residence using his heavenly eye that surpasses that of humans and gods. He got up from his seat of repose in the afternoon and went to a cool place to sit, where he addressed Ānanda, “Have you heard that the city is being assessed?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384c13'>白言： 「我聞行雨大臣欲置城邑以自牢固將伐北城。」</td>
    <td id='p50'>50. He replied, “I have heard that Prime Minister Varṣākāra is establishing defenses in the town to defend against an attack from the north city.”<sup id="ref14"><a href="#n14">14</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384c15'>佛言： 「阿難陀，善哉！ 行雨大臣有大智慧欲置城邑，即與三十三天形狀相似。 我於住處以天眼觀見諸大天神各求住處。 阿難陀，但是勢力諸天欲住之處，於此城內福德大人亦於其中而求住處。 但是處中諸天欲住之處，其處中人及餘諸類亦於此住。 阿難陀，於其城邑有勝人住止。 有勝人言議，有勝商人來共交易往還無滯者，謂即是此波吒離城。 然有三災禍城當損壞：所謂水、火及內反逆。」</td>
    <td id='p51'>51. The Buddha said, “Ānanda, that’s good! That Prime Minister Varṣākāra has much wisdom in establishing defenses in the town, for then it will look like the Trāyastriṃśa Heaven. I observed great heavenly spirits each looking for a place to live here with my heavenly eye. Ānanda, the powerful gods only want to live in places such as this city where great people of virtue are looking for a place to live, too. The middling gods only want to live where middling people and other sorts are living. Ānanda, there are superior people who stay in this city. There are superior people who debate and superior merchants who go long distances to do business with each other and return without obstacles here in Pāṭali. Still, there are three disasters that will destroy the city, which are fire, water, and internal rebellion.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384c24'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>6. The Brahmin Varṣākāra Provides a Meal for the Buddha</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384c24'>時行雨大臣聞佛、世尊從摩揭陀漸漸遊行至波吒離邑住制底處，為諸人眾之所恭敬。 聞已，尋往至世尊所。 修敬已畢，共相慰問，退坐一面。 佛為說法示教利喜已，默然而住。</td>
    <td id='p52'>52. That Prime Minister Varṣākāra then heard that the Buddha, the Bhagavān, had gradually made his way from Magadha to the outskirts of Pāṭali, where respects were paid to him by an assembly of people. Having heard that, he immediately went to visit the Bhagavān. After he was done paying his respects, he exchanged greetings with the Buddha and withdrew to sit to one side. After teaching the Dharma plainly, benefiting and gladdening him, the Buddha fell silent and waited.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.384c29'>爾時大臣即從座起，偏露一肩，右膝著地。 合掌恭敬白言： 「喬答摩，唯願明日及苾芻僧，就我宅中為受微供。」 佛默然受。 是時大臣知佛受已，從座而去。</td>
    <td id='p53'>53. The prime minister when rose from his seat, bared one shoulder, and knelt his right knee to the ground. He then saluted the Buddha with his palms together and said, “Gautama, please accept my invitation to provide some meager offerings to the assembly of monks tomorrow at my home.” The Buddha silently accepted. When that prime minister knew that the Buddha had accepted, he rose from his seat and departed.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385a3'>時行雨大臣既至宅中，告諸大小。 即於其夜備辦種種上妙飲食。 食既辦已，至明清旦，敷設座席安淨水盆，澡豆齒木嚴辦既周。 即令使人往白： 「時至！飲食具備。 願佛知時。」</td>
    <td id='p54'>54. Varṣākāra then went to his home and told the young and old there about it. Then, they prepared a variety of wonderful foods and drinks during the night. Once the meals were prepared, he went in the morning to arrange the seating with cushions, basins of clean water, soap, and chewing sticks set out all around. He then sent a messenger out to say, “The day has arrived! The meals are ready. The Buddha may come when he decides it’s time.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385a7'>世尊即於小食時，執持衣鉢，將諸僧眾詣大臣家。 至設食處就座而坐。 行雨大臣見佛大眾次第坐已，自手奉持種種上妙飲食。 供養佛僧皆令飽足，嚼齒木澡漱已收鉢訖。 行雨大臣即以金瓶注水在佛前立，發是願言： 「我此施供所有勝善等流之業當獲樂報。 以斯福力願此城內舊住天神，於長夜中受勝利樂。 願稱彼名而為呪願。」</td>
    <td id='p55'>55. When it was time for a small meal, the Bhagavān took his robe and bowl and led the assembly of monks to that prime minister’s house. They prepared their seats and sat down in the area for the meal. Once he saw the Buddha and the great assembly sitting in their order, Varṣākāra served them a variety of wonderful foods and drinks with his own hands. Once the Buddha and the assembly were satisfied with their alms, they put away their bowls after using chewing teeth sticks and washing up. Varṣākāra then poured water from a gold jar while standing in front of the Buddha and made this vow: “This offering of mine is a deed that naturally leads to greater virtue, and it will bring happy rewards. May the power of these merits cause the heavenly spirits, who have lived here in this city since antiquity, receive excellent rewards of happiness during the long night. May their names be praised as invocations.”<sup id="ref15"><a href="#n15">15</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385a14'>爾時世尊於彼大臣所設供養，為隨喜故而說頌言：</td>
    <td id='p56'>56. The Bhagavān was happy with the support that the prime minister had provided and spoke these verses:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.385a16'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「若人能有淨信心，<br/>
    恭敬供養於大眾；<br/>
    常依大師真實語，<br/>
    則為諸佛所稱揚。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“If someone can have pure belief,<br/>
    Revere and support the assembly,<br/>
    And always rely on the great teacher’s true words,<br/>
    Then they will be praised by the <em>buddha</em>s.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.385a18'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>若有聰明智慧人，<br/>
    卜居於此勝妙處；<br/>
    供養持戒淨行者，<br/>
    復為宣說願伽陀。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>If an intelligent and wise person<br/>
    Chooses to reside in this wonderful place<br/>
    And supports precept-keepers of pure conduct,<br/>
    They also are proclaimed in invocations.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.385a20'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>若合恭敬布施者，<br/>
    應可殷心修供養。<br/>
    由是天眾起恩慈，<br/>
    猶如父母憐赤子。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>To unite with respectful and generous people,<br/>
    They must have sympathy and work to support them.<br/>
    The gods will feel affection for them,<br/>
    Just as parents have sympathy for their baby.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.385a22'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>既蒙諸天所守護，<br/>
    常得安然受勝樂；<br/>
    生生恒遇於善人，<br/>
    究竟當至無為處。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Having received the protection of the gods,<br/>
    They are always at peace and exceptionally happy.<br/>
    From birth to birth, they always meet with good people<br/>
    And arrive at the uncreated place in the end.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385a24'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>7. Fording the Gaṅgā River</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385a24'>是時世尊為彼大臣示教利喜。 說妙法已，從座而去。 時彼大臣了知世法終歸棄捨。 即整衣服隨世尊後作如是念： 「世尊喬答摩從城出處，我當於彼起大門樓，渡弶伽河為作津濟。」</td>
    <td id='p57'>57. At this point, the Bhagavān taught that prime minister plainly, benefiting and gladdening him. Once he was taught the wonderful Dharma, the Buddha rose from his seat and departed. That prime minister then understood that worldly ways had to be discarded in the end. He adjusted his clothes and followed after the Bhagavān with this thought, “The place where the Bhagavān Gautama exits the city, I will erect a great gate and tower there, and I will make river crossing where he fords the Gaṅgā River.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385a28'>時佛世尊知彼念已，於城中道西趣郭門。 北面而行向河欲過。 時彼河中諸人欲渡。 或將草、木、瓠、及浮囊憑而渡水。 往還不絕數有億千。 世尊見已作如是念： 「我今為當安步中流水上而去？ 為以神力從此岸沒於彼岸出？」</td>
    <td id='p58'>58. Once he knew this thought, the Buddha, the Bhagavān, took the road west through the city as he headed for the gate. He then turned north as he was about the cross the river. At the time, there were people crossing the river. Some crossed the river on grass, wood, gourds, or flotation bags. Those crossing in either direction were unending in the thousands and millions. Having seen this, the Bhagavān thought, “Now, shall I walk across the river on the surface of the water and depart? Or will I use my miraculous powers to disappear from this side and reappear on the other side of it?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385b4'>即入勝定隨其所念，并諸苾芻此沒彼出。 有一苾芻即於是時說伽陀曰：</td>
    <td id='p59'>59. He then entered a superior <em>samādhi</em> that followed what he thought, and he and the monks disappeared from this side and appeared on the other side. One of the monks then spoke these verses:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.385b6'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「諸人求渡者，<br/>
    往來非一數。<br/>
    浮囊及草木，<br/>
    欲越弶伽津。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“People who sought the crossing<br/>
    Came and went without number.<br/>
    Floating on bags, grass, and wood,<br/>
    They wanted to traverse Gaṅgā Ford.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.385b8'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>世尊以神力，<br/>
    并及於僧眾<br/>
    從此至彼岸，<br/>
    不復起疲勞。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>With miraculous powers, the Bhagavān<br/>
    Together with the assembly of monks<br/>
    Went from this side to the other<br/>
    And suffered no more exhaustion.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.385b10'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>平川水流溢，<br/>
    穿井復何為？<br/>
    心根煩惱除，<br/>
    豈更求餘物？」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The river’s flow is a level surface,<br/>
    How could a well hole be drilled into it?<br/>
    The mind faculty without afflictions,<br/>
    How could one seek anything more than that?”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385b12'>時行雨大臣於佛出城處，為造門樓，名曰喬答摩門。 河津階道名喬答摩路。</td>
    <td id='p60'>60. Then, Varṣākāra had a gate and tower built where the Buddha had exited the city and called it Gautama’s Gate. He called the stairway where he crossed the river Gautama’s Path.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385b13'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>8. The Teaching at Kuṭigrāmaka</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385b13'>爾時世尊既至北岸，告阿難陀曰： 「我今欲往小舍村、北升攝波林。」 佛行至彼既安坐已，告諸苾芻曰： 「此是尸羅，此是三摩地，此是般若。 由持戒力，定能安穩、久住、不退。 由修定故，智慧得生。 由慧力故，於染瞋、癡心得解脫。 如是，諸苾芻，心善解脫。 得正解了： 『我生已盡，梵行已立，不受後有。 所作已辦。』 如實而知。」</td>
    <td id='p61'>61. The Bhagavān then went to the north side [of the river] and told Ānanda, “Now, I am going to visit the rosewood forest north of the village of Kuṭi.”<sup id="ref16"><a href="#n16">16</a></sup> After he had traveled there and found a peaceful place to sit, the Buddha told the monks, “This is moral conduct, this is <em>samādhi</em>, and this is wisdom.<sup id="ref17"><a href="#n17">17</a></sup> <em>Samādhi</em> can be stable for a long time without receding as a result of the power of observing precepts. Wisdom can arise as a result of cultivating <em>samādhi</em>. One gains liberation from defiled thoughts of hate and delusion as a result of wisdom’s power. In this way, monks, one’s mind is well liberated. One obtains this right understanding: ‘My births have been ended, the religious practice has been established, and I won’t be subject to a later existence. The task has been accomplished.’ It being true, one will know it.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385b20'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>9. The Teaching at the Village of the Reed Traders</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385b20'>世尊復告阿難陀曰： 「我今欲往販葦聚落村外林中。」 白言： 「世尊，如是，應去。」 既至彼已，時彼聚落人遭疫癘。 有一淨信鄔波索迦因茲命過。 復有善賢名稱等諸近事男亦皆命過。</td>
    <td id='p62'>62. The Bhagavān again told Ānanda, “Now, I am going to visit the forest outside of the village of Nādikā.”<sup id="ref18"><a href="#n18">18</a></sup> He replied, “Yes, Bhagavān, we should go.” Once they had gone there, the people of that village were suffering from an epidemic. There was a pure layman who passed away as a result. There were also other laymen of virtuous reputation who passed away.<sup id="ref19"><a href="#n19">19</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385b24'>時諸苾芻於小食時執持衣鉢，入聚落中次行乞食。 聞此村中多有諸人遭疫而死。 既得食已，各還本處。 飯食訖、收衣鉢、洗足已，俱詣佛所。 禮佛足已，在一面坐白言： 「世尊，我等入村行乞食時，聞有眾多鄔波索迦悉皆命過。 未知彼等當生何處？」</td>
    <td id='p63'>63. When it was time for a small meal, the monks took their robes and bowls and walked the alms round in the village. They heard that many people had died from the epidemic in that village. Once they had obtained their alms, they each returned home. When they were finished eating, putting away their robes and bowls, and washing their feet, they all went to the Buddha. After bowing at the Buddha’s feet, they sat to one side and said, “Bhagavān, when we went to the village to walk the alms round, we heard that there were many laymen who had passed away. Do we know yet where they were born [after dying]?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385c1'>佛言： 「苾芻，於此村中有二百五十諸鄔波索迦，斷五下分結。 從此命過得化生身。 於彼涅槃更不退轉。 證不還果不復更來。</td>
    <td id='p64'>64. The Buddha said, “Monks, there were two hundred and fifty laymen in this town who had abandoned the five lower bonds. They obtained a body born through transformation upon passing away. That they will <em>nirvāṇa</em> there is irreversible. They realized the fruit of non-returning and won’t come here again.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385c3'>「汝等苾芻，復有三百餘人鄔波索迦從此命過，薄斷染瞋、癡。 得一來果，暫來人間當盡苦際。</td>
    <td id='p65'>65. “You monks, there were another three hundred other laymen who passed away from here having weakened or abandoned the defilements of hate and delusion. Having attained the fruit of once-returning, they will reach the end of suffering when they come back among humans.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385c5'>「汝諸苾芻，於此村中有五百人並已命過，能斷三結。 得預流果不復退轉，於七有生人天還往當盡苦際。</td>
    <td id='p66'>66. “You monks, there were another five hundred laymen in this town who all have passed away who could abandon the three bonds. That they will attain the fruit of stream-entry is irreversible, and they will reach the end of suffering after seven rebirths as humans or gods.<sup id="ref20"><a href="#n20">20</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385c8'>「汝等苾芻，何煩致問作斯擾惱？ 生者必死，此為常事，若佛出世及不出世。 生死之法如來悉知，為諸有情分別演說，開示十二緣生法門。 所謂此有故彼有，此生故彼生。 即是無明緣行，行緣識，識緣名色，名色緣六處，六處緣觸，觸緣受，受緣愛，愛緣取，取緣有，有緣生，生緣老、死、憂、悲、苦、惱。</td>
    <td id='p67'>67. “You monks, how vexing would it be to ask about these irritations? Anyone who is born will surely die. This is always the case, whether a <em>buddha</em> arises in the world or not. The Tathāgata completely knows the law of birth and death, which he discerns and expounds upon for sentient beings, revealing the Dharma subject of the twelve [steps of] dependent origination. That is to say, that exists because this exists, and that arose because this arose. Just so, ignorance is the condition for actions, actions are the condition for awareness, awareness is the condition for name and form, name and form are the conditions for the six senses, the six senses are the conditions for contact, contact is the condition for feeling, feeling is the condition for craving, craving is the condition for grasping, grasping is the condition for existence, existence is the condition for birth, and birth is the condition for old age, death, sorrow, lamentation, pain, and trouble.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385c14'>「此無故彼無，此滅故彼滅。 所謂無明滅則行滅，行滅則識滅，識滅則名色滅，名色滅則六處滅，六處滅則觸滅，觸滅則受滅，受滅則愛滅，愛滅則取滅，取滅則有滅，有滅則生滅，生滅則老、死、憂、悲、苦、惱滅。 如是廣大苦蘊悉皆除滅。</td>
    <td id='p68'>68. “That doesn’t exist because this doesn’t exist, and that ceased because this ceased. That is to say, actions cease when ignorance ceases, awareness ceases when actions cease, name and form cease when awareness ceases, the six senses cease when name and form cease, contact ceases when the six senses cease, feeling ceases when contact ceases, craving ceases when feeling ceases, grasping ceases when craving ceases, existence ceases when grasping ceases, birth ceases when existence ceases, old age, death, sorrow, lamentation, pain, and trouble cease when birth ceases. In this way, the vast mass of suffering is completely ceased.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385c19'>「我今復為汝等說法鏡經。 應可諦聽，善思念之。 云何法鏡？ 謂佛、法、僧、聖清淨戒，汝等於此深生尊重、恭敬、供養、禮拜、讚歎，正信正念常不斷絕。 是名法鏡。 如是應持。」</td>
    <td id='p69'>69. “I will now teach all of you the Dharma mirror <em>sūtra</em> again. You should listen closely and consider it well. What is the Dharma mirror? When all of you honor, respect, support, revere, and praise the Buddha, Dharma, Saṅgha, and noble, pure precepts, which all of you deeply honor, respect, give support, revere, and praise, then proper faith and mindfulness with be constant and unending. This is called the Dharma mirror. You should remember it in this way.”<sup id="ref21"><a href="#n21">21</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385c23'>時諸苾芻聞佛所說依教，奉行。</td>
    <td id='p70'>70. The monks who heard what the Buddha taught relied on his teachings and approved of them.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385c24'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>10. The Woman Āmra’s Invitation</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385c24'>佛告具壽阿難陀曰： 「我今欲往廣嚴城。 汝可告諸大眾。」 時阿難陀言：「如是。世尊，」 佛及僧眾漸至城所住菴沒羅林。</td>
    <td id='p71'>71. The Buddha told Venerable Ānanda, “Now, I am going to visit Vaiśālī.<sup id="ref22"><a href="#n22">22</a></sup> You can inform the great assembly.” Ānanda replied, “Yes, Bhagavān.” The Buddha and the assembly made their way to that city and stayed in the mango forest there.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.385c26'>時此城中有一女人(舊云「奈女」者，非)，顏容端正眾所知識。 名菴沒羅，是此林主。 聞世尊至住我林中，著妙衣瓔而自莊飾。 命諸女屬共相隨從。 乘駕寶車詣世尊處。 既至林所便即下車徒步而進。</td>
    <td id='p72'>72. There was a woman in the city at the time (<em>the old version says “mango woman,” which is incorrect</em>), whose visage was handsome and known to many people. Her name was Āmra, and she was the owner of the forest. Hearing that the Bhagavān had arrived to stay in her forest, she put on fine clothes and jewelry to decorate herself. She then ordered her retinue of women to follow along with her. They rode in precious chariots to where the Bhagavān was staying. Upon arriving at the forest, they dismounted from their chariots and proceeded on foot.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386a2'>爾時世尊於無量百千苾芻眾中而為說法。 于時世尊遙見女已，告諸苾芻： 「彼諸女眾欲來至此。 汝等應當繫念思惟。 勿生異想，聽我所說。</td>
    <td id='p73'>73. At the time, the Bhagavān was teaching Dharma to an assembly of measureless hundreds of thousands of monks. When he saw this woman from a distance, the Bhagavān told the monks, “There is an assembly of women that are coming here. You all must fix your attention and contemplate. Don’t have perceptions of anything other than listening to what I am teaching.<sup id="ref23"><a href="#n23">23</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386a5'>「汝等苾芻，云何名為繫念思惟？ 若有苾芻起罪惡念、不善心時，當即除遣。 應生正信，發起精勤，攝心，令住正念、不散。 使善法生、惡念止息，正智熏習圓滿增廣。 正勤相續勿為異念，苾芻如是繫念思惟。</td>
    <td id='p74'>74. “All of you monks, what is called fixing one’s attention and contemplating? When a blameworthy and wicked thought or unskillful feeling occurs to a monk, he should immediately eliminate it. He should produce correct faith, initiate the effort to gather his mind, and make it stay with proper mindfulness and no distraction. Making good qualities arise and bad thoughts stop, correct knowledge will suffuse you completely and grow larger. Applying this effort continuously and not having any other thoughts, a monk fixes his attention and contemplates in this way.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386a9'>「汝等復聽！勿生異想。苾芻應知，往來所趣當善觀察屈、申，俯仰，著僧伽胝、執持衣鉢，行、住、坐、臥，語、默，睡眠、惛沈、起時。 為對治法正念而住。</td>
    <td id='p75'>75. “All of you, listen to this, too! Don’t produce perceptions of anything else. Monks, you should know, wherever you may go, you should be watchful when bending, stretching, looking up and down, putting on your robes, carrying your robe and bowl, walking, standing, sitting, lying down, speaking, being quiet, sleepy, lethargic, and waking up. Abide with correct mindfulness as a counteractive.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386a12'>「云何苾芻正念而住？ 汝今當知，謂觀內身。 策起正勤，應善調伏，於諸世間知是憂苦。 次觀外身⋯內外身⋯內受⋯外受⋯內外受⋯內心⋯外心⋯內外心⋯內法⋯外法⋯內外法。 於此諸法繫念觀察攝心令住。 策起正勤、勇猛、不息，應善調伏，於諸世間知是憂苦。 苾芻如是繫念思惟。 是故汝等正念而住。 由彼女眾欲來至此，是我殷勤之所教誨。」</td>
    <td id='p76'>76. “How does a monk abide with correct mindfulness? Now, you should know, this refers to observing the internal body. To rouse proper effort, one should be well disciplined and aware of the sorrows and pains of worldly people. Next, observe the external body … both the internal and external body … internal feeling … external feeling … both internal and external feeling … internal mind … external mind … both internal and external mind … internal principles … external principles … both internal and external principles. Regarding these principles, one fixes their attention and observes them while collecting their mind and keeping it there. To rouse the proper effort, vigor, and not stop, one should be well disciplined and know the sorrows and pains of worldly people. A monk fixes his attention and contemplates in this way. Therefore, all of you abide with correct mindfulness. These are my instructions to take care because those women are about to arrive here.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386a20'>是時女眾來詣佛所，頂禮雙足，退坐一面。 爾時世尊為說妙法，示教利、喜，默然而住。</td>
    <td id='p77'>77. It was then that the assembly of women came to the Buddha, bowed their heads at his two feet, and sat to one side. Thereupon, Buddha taught them the fine Dharma plainly, benefiting and gladdening them, and then fell silent.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386a22'>時菴沒羅女從座而起合掌恭敬，白佛言： 「世尊，唯願哀愍，與諸苾芻明日就宅，受我微供。」世尊默然。 知佛受已，頂禮雙足，奉辭而去。</td>
    <td id='p78'>78. The woman Āmra then rose from her seat, saluted the Buddha with her palms together, and said to him, “Bhagavān, please take pity on us and accompany the monks tomorrow to my house to receive my meager offering!” The Buddha remained silent. Once she knew the Buddha had accepted, she bowed her head at his two feet, paid her respects, and departed.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386a24'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>11. The Licchavi Men’s Invitation</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386a24'>時廣嚴城諸栗㚲毘子聞佛世尊遊行人間，住菴沒羅林。 各嚴種種駟馬寶車。 馭青馬駕、青車、青轡勒，執青鞭，戴青帽，擎青蓋。 帶青刀，捉青拂，著青衣。 瓔珞、塗香悉皆青色，并諸從者皆服青衣。 復有栗㚲毘與諸從者別為一隊，車、馬、衣、瓔悉為黃色。 復有一隊悉為赤色。 復有一隊悉為白色。 如是，各別前後隊仗，聲螺擊鼓，出廣嚴城。 皆欲親覲如來，頂禮，恭敬。</td>
    <td id='p79'>79. The Licchavi sons of Vaiśālī heard that the Buddha Bhagavān was touring among the people and staying in the mango forest. They each prepared a variety of horse-drawn precious chariots. They drove blue chariots with blue horse teams wearing blue spurs and bridles while holding blue whips and carrying blue caps and having blue parasols in hand. They had blue swords in their belts, grasped blue fly whisks, and wore blue clothes. Their jewelry and rubbed incense were both blue, and their followers were wearing blue clothing, too. There was also a group of Licchavis and their followers whose carts, horses, clothing, and jewelry were all yellow. There was also a group who were all red. There was another group who were all white. In this way, they each had a group of armed troops in front and rear who blew conches and beat drums as they left Vaiśālī. They wanted to see the Tathāgata up close, bow their heads, and reverence him.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386b4'>世尊知彼欲來，告諸苾芻： 「汝等未見三十三天遊觀芳園者，今可觀此廣嚴城中諸栗㚲毘子。 由其威德莊飾巧妙，與三十三天出遊芳園等無有異。」</td>
    <td id='p80'>80. The Bhagavān noticed they were about to arrive and told the monks, “All of you who have yet to see the thirty-three gods when they go sightseeing in their parks, now you can look at those young Licchavis from this city of Vaiśālī. There’s no difference between their dignity, adornments, and skill and the thirty-three gods sightseeing in their parks.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386b7'>諸栗㚲毘子既至林所，便即下車，徒步而進。 詣世尊所，頂禮雙足，退坐一面，欲聽妙法。 世尊為說示教利喜，各令慶悅。</td>
    <td id='p81'>81. Once they had reached the forest, the Licchavi men dismounted from their chariots and proceeded on foot. When they reached the Bhagavān, they bowed their heads at his two feet and withdrew to sit to one side hoping to listen to the marvelous Dharma. The Bhagavān then taught them Dharma plainly, benefiting and gladdening them, which delighted each of them.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386b10'>爾時會中有一婆羅門名曰黃髮摩納婆，從座而起整衣合掌，白佛言：「世尊，我今樂欲隨喜讚歎。」</td>
    <td id='p82'>82. There was a brahmin in the congregation at the time named Paiṅgika Māṇava.<sup id="ref24"><a href="#n24">24</a></sup> He rose from his seat, adjusted his robe, and said to the Buddha with his palms together, “Bhagavān, I would now like to gladly praise you!”<sup id="ref25"><a href="#n25">25</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386b12'>佛告摩納婆： 「隨汝意說。」 既蒙佛許，即說頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p83'>83. The Buddha told Māṇava, “You can go ahead and do as you like.” Having received the Buddha’s consent, he then spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.386b14'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「大王身持寶裝甲，<br/>
    今為國主獲善利。<br/>
    有佛現生於此處，<br/>
    名稱高遠若須彌。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“The great king is clad in jeweled armor,<br/>
    And today’s rulers have won good profits<br/>
    A <em>buddha</em> has appeared here in this place<br/>
    Whose fame is as lofty as Sumeru.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.386b16'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>如白蓮華處池中，<br/>
    於夜開敷散芬馥；<br/>
    如日流暉照空界，<br/>
    光明遍滿於世間。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>He’s like a white lotus flower in a pond<br/>
    That opens at night to spread its fragrance;<br/>
    Like the sun streaming its rays through the sky<br/>
    Illuminating everything in the world.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.386b18'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>當觀如來智慧力，<br/>
    如大明炬照昏冥。<br/>
    常為人天作智眼，<br/>
    諸來見者皆調伏。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>We should look on the Tathāgata’s wisdom<br/>
    As a brilliant torch shining in the dark.<br/>
    He made his eye of knowledge for humans and gods,<br/>
    To tame all those who come to see him.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386b20'>時諸栗㚲毘聞是說已，同聲讚言： 「大摩納婆，善說斯語。」 是時會中有五百栗㚲毘子，各脫上衣持施黃髮。 世尊復為大眾說法示教利喜默然而住。</td>
    <td id='p87'>87. Once they had heard this, the Licchavis praised him with the same voice, “Great Māṇava, these words are well spoken!” Thereupon, five hundred Licchavi men in the congregation each took off their upper robe and gave them to Paiṅgika. The Bhagavān again taught the Dharma for that great assembly plainly, benefiting and gladdening them, and then fell silent.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386b23'>時諸栗㚲毘子各從座起，整衣合掌而白佛言： 「唯願，世尊哀愍我等，與諸苾芻明日城內，受我微供。」</td>
    <td id='p88'>88. Those Licchavi men each rose from their seats, adjusted their robe, and said to the Buddha with their palms together, “Please, Bhagavān, take pity on us and accompany the monks tomorrow to the city to receive our meager offerings.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386b25'>佛言： 「我與苾芻已許菴沒羅女明日就食。」</td>
    <td id='p89'>89. The Buddha said, “I and the monks have already agreed to a meal tomorrow with the woman Āmra.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386b26'>白言： 「大德，我有所失，不如彼女。 彼有智慧先請世尊。 我等不能及時親覲恭敬禮拜，我於後時當興供養。」</td>
    <td id='p90'>90. They replied, “Venerable, we’ve lost something, not being the equal of that woman. She had the wisdom to invite the Bhagavān first. Given that we can’t meet you personally, pay our respects, and honor you then, we will provide some support at a later time.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386b28'>佛言：「甚善！」 聞佛讚已情懷歡喜。 頂禮佛足奉辭而去。</td>
    <td id='p91'>91. The Buddha said, “Very good!” Upon hearing the Buddha’s praise, they felt glad. They bowed their heads at the Buddha’s feet, paid their respects, and departed.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386c1'>時摩納婆見彼諸人辭佛去後，少時而住。 即從座起整衣合掌白佛言： 「大德，彼五百人聞我讚佛同聲慶喜。 為妙語故，各持一衣來施於我。 我持奉佛。 唯願慈悲哀愍納受。」</td>
    <td id='p92'>92. Māṇava watched those men pay their respects to the Buddha and waited a little while after they had left. He then rose from his seat, adjusted his robe, and said to the Buddha with his palms together, “Venerable, those five hundred men heard my praise of the Buddha and appreciated it with the same voice. They each took a robe off and gave it to me because of my fine words. I present them to the Buddha. Please have mercy and pity by accepting them.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386c5'>世尊為受，告言： 「摩納婆，若如來、應、正等覺出現世間，有五希有事亦現於世。 云何為五？ 謂於世間若有大師，如來、應、正等覺、明行圓滿、善逝、世間解、無上士、調御丈夫、天人師、佛、世尊出現於世。 凡所說法初、中、後善，文、義巧妙、純一、圓滿，清淨鮮白梵行之相。 當知此是如來、應、正等覺出現世間第一希有。</td>
    <td id='p93'>93. The Bhagavān accepted them and said, “Māṇava, when a Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One appears in the world, there are five extraordinary things that also appear in the world. What are the five? Suppose there is a great teacher in the world or a Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One who is complete in insight and conduct, well gone, an understander of the world, an unsurpassed man, a trainer of men, a teacher of gods and humans, a Buddha and a Bhagavān who appears in the world. They all teach the Dharma that’s good in the beginning, middle, and end, that’s skillful, consistent, and complete in content and meaning, and that signifies the pure and clean religious practice. You should know that this is the first extraordinary thing about a Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One who arises in the world.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386c12'>「復次，若有聽聞如是妙法，能善作意，一心審諦，攝斂諸根思念觀察。 當知此是如來、應、正等覺出現世間第二希有。</td>
    <td id='p94'>94. “Furthermore, if someone hears such a fine Dharma, they can think well on it wholeheartedly and sincerely, bringing together their faculties to carefully consider and investigate it. You should know that this is the second extraordinary thing about a Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One who arises in the world.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386c15'>「復次，其聞法者，情生喜悅，獲大善利，於世俗事生厭離心⋯此是如來、應、正等覺出現世間第三希有。</td>
    <td id='p95'>95. “Furthermore, those who hear the Dharma feel joyful, obtain a great and good benefits, and grow tired of worldly affairs … this is the third extraordinary thing about a Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One who arises in the world.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386c18'>「復次，若有展轉聽聞法者，皆亦漸漸依教奉持⋯此是如來、應、正等覺出現世間第四希有。</td>
    <td id='p96'>96. “Furthermore, if someone coverts upon listening to the Dharma, they will gradually rely on the teaching, accepting and keeping it, too … this is the fourth extraordinary thing about a Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One who arises in the world.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386c20'>「復次，諸聞法者繫念思惟，即能通達甚深妙慧⋯此是如來、應、正等覺出現世間第五希有。</td>
    <td id='p97'>97. “Furthermore, those who hear the Dharma fix their attention and contemplate it, and then they can master its profound and wonderful wisdom … this is the fifth extraordinary thing about a Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One who arises in the world.<sup id="ref26"><a href="#n26">26</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386c22'>「復次摩納婆，知恩報恩名大善士，少尚不忘，何況多恩。 是故汝今應勤修學。」 摩納婆聞佛說已，歡喜信受，頂禮雙足，辭佛而去。</td>
    <td id='p98'>98. “Furthermore, Māṇava, one who knows their favors and repays those favors is called a great and good man who does not forget even a minor request. How would it be with these many favors? Therefore, you should apply yourself to your training now.” Once he had heard what the Buddha said, Māṇava rejoiced and believed it. He bowed his head at the Buddha’s feet, paid his respects, and then departed.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386c24'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>12. The Woman Āmra’s Gifts</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386c24'>時菴沒羅女即於其夜，備辦種種上妙飲食。 至明清旦，敷設床席，置淨水盆、齒木、及⋯屑。 遣使白佛： 「飲食已辦。 願佛知時。」</td>
    <td id='p99'>99. The woman Āmra then prepared a various of wonderful dishes and drinks during the night. When morning came and the sun rose, she laid out seats and couches and put out a basin of clean water, chewing sticks, [soap, cow dung, and fine earth]. Then, she sent a messenger to tell the Buddha, “The meal is ready. The Buddha may come when he decides it’s time.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.386c27'>爾時，世尊著衣持鉢，與苾芻眾詣彼食處。 佛及大眾次第坐已。 時菴沒羅女見佛大眾悉安坐已，手自奉行種種上妙飲食，普令飽滿。 飯食訖，次授澡豆及以齒木。 澡漱已，收鉢竟，遂取卑席於佛前坐，攝心聽法。</td>
    <td id='p100'>100. At that time, the Bhagavān put on his robe, took his bowl, and went to her place to eat with the assembly of monks. Once there, the Buddha and the great assembly were seated in their order. When she saw that the Buddha and the great assembly were seated, Āmra then served them a variety of wonderful dishes and drinks with her own hands until they were all full. When they were done eating, she next gave them [water basins], soap, and chewing sticks. Once they had washed up and were done putting away their bowls, she fetched a small stool, sat in front of the Buddha, and collected her mind to listen to Dharma.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387a3'>爾時世尊即為其女說施伽他曰：</td>
    <td id='p101'>101. It was then that the Bhagavān spoke verses as a gift to her:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.387a4'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「若人不慳能施與<br/>
    見者愛敬咸親近，<br/>
    入眾會中無畏懼<br/>
    得大利益具名聞。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“When someone isn’t stingy and giving<br/>
    And treat friends with affection and respect,<br/>
    They enter their communities without fear,<br/>
    And they obtain great benefits and fame.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.387a6'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>是故智人常惠施，<br/>
    能令長夜福增長。<br/>
    漸除煩惱破慳貪，<br/>
    三十三天受歡樂。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The wise therefore always focus on giving,<br/>
    Which cause one’s merits to grow in the long night.<br/>
    Gradually removing afflictions and destroying greediness,<br/>
    One experiences the joy of the Trāyastriṃśa Heaven.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.387a8'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>修諸善業營功德，<br/>
    命終之後得生天，<br/>
    與諸女眾戲芳園，<br/>
    為佛弟子常安樂。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Cultivating good deeds and building virtue,<br/>
    One will be born in heaven after they die.<br/>
    Frolicking in pleasure parks with a host of women,<br/>
    The Buddha’s disciples are always happy there.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387a10'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>13. The Monks Disperse and the Buddha Falls Ill</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387a10'>爾時世尊復為菴沒羅女隨機，說法示教利喜已，從座而去。 還至住處，告阿難陀曰： 「我今欲往竹林中。 汝可告諸大眾。」 時阿難陀如佛所教，即與大眾隨佛至竹林。 北住升攝波林。</td>
    <td id='p102'>102. The Bhagavān further motivated the woman Āmra, teaching her the Dharma plainly, benefiting and gladdening her. Afterward, she rose from her seat and departed. They returned to their abode, and the Buddha told Ānanda, “I’m going into Veṇuvana now.<sup id="ref28"><a href="#n28">28</a></sup> You can inform the great assembly.” Ānanda did as the Buddha instructed and followed the Buddha with the great assembly to Veṇuvana. They stayed in a rosewood grove to the north.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387a14'>時屬飢儉乞求難得。 佛告諸苾芻： 「今時飢儉。 汝等宜可求同意者於薜舍離諸方聚落隨便安居。 我與阿難陀於此處住。 若不如是，求乞難得。」</td>
    <td id='p103'>103. There was a famine at the time that made getting alms difficult. The Buddha told the monks, “Now this is a time of famine. All of you ought to seek out like-minded people in Vaiśālī and the surrounding villages to find shelter with them. Ānanda and I will stay here. If we don’t do this, alms will be difficult to find.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387a17'>時諸苾芻聞佛教已，各依善友隨處安居。 唯阿難陀獨留侍佛，在於樹下而作安居。 佛於夏內身嬰病苦。 受諸痛惱幾將命沒。 作如是念： 「我身有疾，不久遷謝，然諸苾芻散在餘處。 我今不應離諸大眾而般涅槃。 應以無相三昧觀察自身，令苦停息。」 作是念已，即入勝定，所受諸苦如念皆除，安隱而住。</td>
    <td id='p104'>104. Having heard the Buddha’s instruction, the monks each relied on good friends for their residence. Only Ānanda stayed to attend to the Buddha, and they made a home under the trees. During the summer, the Buddha suffered from illness and pain inside his body. He experienced so much agony that his life was about to end. He thought, “Having this illness, it won’t be long before I die, but the monks are scattered in other places. I shouldn’t leave the great assembly now and <em>parinirvāṇa</em>. I should observe my own body with the signless <em>samādhi</em> to make the pain go away.” Having had that thought, he then entered the higher <em>samādhi</em>, which made the pains that he felt go away as he thought, and he abided peacefully.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387a24'>時具壽阿難陀於日晡時從定而起，往詣佛所頂禮佛足在一面立。 合掌白言： 「大德世尊，我於向者身心迷悶，莫辯好惡，所聞之法不能誦持。 由見世尊受諸病苦，恐將寂滅。 今聞世尊未般涅槃，少得醒悟。 又言： 『若諸苾芻不總集者我不涅槃。』 以此惟忖故知更說希有之法。」</td>
    <td id='p105'>105. Then, the elder Ānanda rose from his repose in the afternoon and went to the Buddha, bowed his head at the Buddha’s feet, and stood to one side. With his palms together, he said, “Venerable Bhagavān, my body and mind were depressed a while ago, and I couldn’t tell good from bad, nor could I recite or remember the Dharma I had learned. It was because I had seen the Bhagavān experiencing painful ailments and was afraid he was going to be peacefully extinguished.<sup id="ref29"><a href="#n29">29</a></sup> Now that I’ve heard that the Bhagavān has yet to <em>parinirvāṇa</em>, I’m a little more awake. He also said, ‘If the monks haven’t all assembled, then I won’t <em>nirvāṇa</em>.’ I know he will teach the extraordinary Dharma further because I’ve surmised this.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387b2'>佛告阿難陀： 「汝作是意，謂我教導諸苾芻故不涅槃者，無有是處。 何以故？ 豈可我今更欲示諸苾芻希有之法？ 阿難陀，我所應說皆已說竟，悉令解了內外諸法，所謂四念住、四正勤、四神足、五根、五力、七覺分、八聖道。 阿難陀，諸佛如來常以此法分明為說。 無有祕悋覆藏之心。</td>
    <td id='p106'>106. The Buddha told Ānanda, “That thought you had that I won’t <em>nirvāṇa</em> because I will teach the monks isn’t a possibility. Why is that? Why would I want to show the extraordinary Dharma to the monks now? Ānanda, I have already taught what I should teach, and everyone understands the inner and outer teachings, which are the four abodes of mindfulness, four right efforts, four miraculous abilities, five faculties, five powers, seven factors of awakening, and eightfold noble path. Ānanda, <em>buddha</em>s and <em>tathāgata</em>s always clearly discern these teachings. He has nothing secret or hidden in his thoughts.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387b7'>「然阿難陀，我身有疾將欲涅槃，便作是念： 『吾今病苦必定命終。』 諸苾芻等各在餘處，我念：『不應離斯大眾而般涅槃。 宜自用意以無相三昧觀察其身痛惱令息。』即便入定，所受諸苦悉皆除愈，得安隱住。</td>
    <td id='p107'>107. “Still, Ānanda, my body is ailing and is going to <em>nirvāṇa</em>, so I thought this: ‘Now, I am ailing and in pain and my life will surely end.’ The monks are each living elsewhere, and I thought, ‘I shouldn’t abandon this great assembly and <em>parinirvāṇa</em>. I ought to apply my mind to the signless <em>samādhi</em> and watch this body’s pain and trouble to make it stop.’ Then I entered that <em>samādhi</em> and eliminated all of the pains I was feeling so that I could have some peace.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387b12'>「阿難陀，我今衰邁身力羸弱年將八十。 唯依二事而得存住，如朽破車亦依二事。 以是義故，汝今不應憂愁苦惱。 但諸世間有為之法從因緣生，而不滅壞得常住者，無有是處。 我先為汝常說是事。 一切世間樂欲光華。 愛念可意悉皆散壞。 恩愛別離無留住者。 是故當知，於我現在及我滅後，汝等自為洲渚，自為歸依。 法為洲渚，法為歸依。 無別洲渚，無別歸依。 何以故？ 若我現在及我滅度，若依法者、樂持戒者於我聲聞弟子最為第一。</td>
    <td id='p108'>108. “Ānanda, I am getting old now, my body is weak, and I’ve reached eighty years. It’s only by relying on two things that I have been able to stay alive, like a decrepit cart that also relies on two things. For this reason, you shouldn’t be sad or troubled now. It’s simply impossible for worldly things that are produced from causes and conditions to not be destroyed or last forever. I’ve taught you this often in the past. All the pleasant desires of the world are like shining flowers. Everything that’s cherished and agreeable will break up and be destroyed. Loved ones will be separated until no one remains. Therefore, you should know, while I am here and after I have perished, all of you will be your own islands and your own refuges. The Dharma is the island, and the Dharma is the refuge. There is no other island and no other refuge. And why is that? Whether I am present or after my extinguishment, those who rely on the Dharma and who like observing precepts will be the best of my voice-hearer disciples.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387b21'>「云何苾芻自為洲渚、自為歸依，無別洲渚、無別歸依？ 阿難陀，若諸苾芻能於內身善知身相，繫念觀察攝心，令住發起勇猛，降伏貪、瞋、及諸憂惱。 如是外身⋯內身⋯內外身⋯內受⋯外受⋯內外受⋯內心⋯外心⋯內外心⋯內法⋯外法⋯內外法⋯於如是處，繫念觀察攝心，令住發起勇猛，降伏貪、瞋、及諸憂惱。 苾芻若作如是觀者，此則名為自為洲渚自為歸依順法而住。」</td>
    <td id='p109'>109. “How will a monk be his own island and his own refuge, having no other island and no other refuge? Ānanda, if the monks are able to know aspects of the body well, fixing their attention and observing them with a collected mind, keeping it there and generating vigor, they will defeat greed, hate, and troubling sorrows. In this way, they fix their attention and observe the external body … internal and external bodies … internal feelings … external feelings … internal and external feelings … internal mind … external mind … internal and external minds … internal principles … external principles … internal and external principles … with a collected mind, keeping it there and generating vigor, they will defeat greed, hate, and troubling sorrows. If a monk performs these contemplations, this is called being his own island and his own refuge by following the Dharma and abiding in it.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387c1'>內攝頌曰：</td>
    <td>The inner summary verse says:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.387c2'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>行雨竹林內，<br/>
    修理波吒邑，<br/>
    渡河詣小村，<br/>
    漸向涅槃等。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>They spent the rainy season in a bamboo grove,<br/>
    Practiced the principle in Pāṭali,<br/>
    Forded the river and went to a small village,<br/>
    Slowly headed toward <em>nirvāṇa</em>.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387c4'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>14. Ānanda Misses the Hint and Māra Visits the Buddha</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387c4'>爾時，世尊告具壽阿難陀曰： 「我今欲往廣嚴城。」 時阿難陀聞佛教已，即隨佛。 後至廣嚴城，住重閣堂。 於小食時著衣持鉢入城乞食，時阿難陀隨佛而去。 次第乞已還至本處。</td>
    <td id='p110'>110. It was then that the Bhagavān told the elder Ānanda, “Now, I’m going to Vaiśālī.”<sup id="ref30"><a href="#n30">30</a></sup> Once he had heard the his instruction, and Ānanda followed the Buddha. Once they arrived in Vaiśālī, they stayed in the second-floor hall there.<sup id="ref31"><a href="#n31">31</a></sup> When it was time for a meal, he put on his robe and took his bowl into the city to solicit alms. Ānanda then followed the Buddha as he departed. After they had walked the alms round, they returned to their abode.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387c7'>飯食訖、收衣鉢、澡漱畢、洗足已，佛即往詣取弓制底樹下而坐。 告阿難陀曰： 「此廣嚴城物產、華麗、芳林、果樹在處敷榮。 塔廟清池甚可愛樂。 贍部洲內此最希奇。 阿難陀，若有能於四神足修習、多修習，欲住一劫若過一劫，悉皆隨意。 阿難陀，如來已於四神足已多修習。 欲住一劫若過一劫，悉皆自在。」</td>
    <td id='p111'>111.  After they were finished with their meal, had put away their robe and bowl, cleaned themselves up, and washed their feet, the Buddha then went under a tree at Cāpāla Shrine and sat there.<sup id="ref32"><a href="#n32">32</a></sup> He told Ānanda, “This city of Vaiśālī is teeming with life, the flowers are elegant, the forest is sweet-smelling, and the fruit trees here are flourishing. The shrines and clear ponds are very lovely. It’s the most extraordinary place in Jambudvīpa. Ānanda, anyone who can cultivate and cultivate often the four miraculous abilities could live for an eon or more as they like. Ānanda, the Tathāgata has cultivated the four miraculous abilities often. He is entirely free to live for an eon or more if he wants.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387c14'>時阿難陀默然無語。 如是世尊三唱前事⋯乃至⋯悉皆自在。 阿難陀亦皆無語。 佛作是念： 「今阿難陀被魔所惑，身心迷亂。 我已再三分明告示，竟無言說，能為啟請。 由是定知被魔所惑。」 即便告曰： 「汝可依一樹下宴坐而住。 不應與汝雜亂同居。」</td>
    <td id='p112'>112. Then, Ānanda was silent and said nothing. <em>The Bhagavān declared this three times as above … up to …</em> “He is entirely free [to do that].” Ānanda also said nothing each time. The Buddha then thought, “Ānanda is under the influence of Māra now, his body and mind being depressed and distracted. I clearly stated this three times, and he said nothing nor made a request. Surely this is because he is under the influence of Māra.” Then he said, “You can go sit in repose under a tree. You shouldn’t remain here with me while you’re feeling muddled.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387c19'>時阿難陀聞佛教已，即往晝日宴坐之處，住一樹下。</td>
    <td>Once he had heard the Buddha’s instruction, Ānanda went to spend the day sitting in repose under a tree.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387c21'>爾時惡魔波卑來詣佛所，頂禮佛足，在一面立。 合掌恭敬白言： 「世尊，涅槃時至。 唯願善逝入般涅槃。」</td>
    <td id='p113'>113. Then, Māra the Wicked One came to the Buddha, bowed his head at the Buddha’s feet, and stood to one side. He then saluted with his palms together and said, “Bhagavān, the time for <em>nirvāṇa</em> has arrived. May the Well Gone One please enter <em>parinirvāṇa</em>.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387c23'>佛告魔曰： 「汝今何故，云：『涅槃時至』，請我涅槃？」</td>
    <td id='p114'>114. The Buddha asked Māra, “Why do you now say, ‘The time for <em>nirvāṇa</em> has arrived,’ and request that I <em>nirvāṇa</em>?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387c24'>魔言： 「大德，往者一時，佛於尼連河側菩提樹下。 成佛未久時，我詣彼白言： 『世尊當知涅槃時至。 唯願善逝入般涅槃。』</td>
    <td id='p115'>115. Māra said, “Venerable, there was once a time when the Buddha was under the <em>bodhi</em> tree on the bank of the Nairañjanā River. Not long after he became a <em>buddha</em>, I went to him and said, ‘Bhagavān, you should know that the time for <em>nirvāṇa</em> has arrived. May the Well Gone One please enter <em>parinirvāṇa</em>.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.387c27'>「佛告我言： 『若我聖眾聲聞弟子，未有智慧通達聰明辯了，以正法言摧伏邪論，顯揚聖教能流通者。 又諸苾芻、苾芻尼、鄔波索迦、鄔波斯迦亦未能得堅修戒品，令我梵行得廣流布，利益多人及諸天眾者。 我今無宜入大涅槃。』</td>
    <td id='p116'>116. “The Buddha told me, ‘My noble assembly of direct disciples haven’t yet the wisdom to fully master, learn, and be eloquent in the proper Dharma to defeat incorrect theories and extol the noble teaching so that they can be promulgated. Also, the monks, nuns, laymen, and laywomen have yet to become firm in their cultivation of the precepts, ensure that my religious practice becomes widespread, and benefit many people and the host of gods. It wouldn’t be appropriate for me to enter that great <em>nirvāṇa</em>.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388a3'>「大德世尊，今聲聞眾有大智慧。 具足通達辯才無礙，以正法言摧伏邪論，顯揚聖教能使流通。 又諸苾芻、苾芻尼、鄔波索迦、鄔波斯迦，能令梵行得廣流布，利益多人及諸天眾諸事圓滿。 是故我今白世尊言： 『涅槃時至。 唯願善逝，入般涅槃。』」</td>
    <td id='p117'>117. “Venerable Bhagavān, the assembly of disciples has great wisdom now. They have fully mastered eloquence and are unobstructed, their words defeat incorrect theories with the proper Dharma, and they extol the noble teaching so that it can be promulgated. Also, the monks, nuns, laymen, and laywomen are able to make the religious practice widespread, and they have fulfilled the benefits to many people and the host of gods. These things have been fulfilled, therefore, I say to the Bhagavān now, ‘The time for <em>nirvāṇa</em> has arrived. May the Well Gone One please enter <em>parinirvāṇa</em>.’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388a8'>佛告魔曰： 「汝且少待。 如來不久却。 後三月，入無餘依大涅槃界。」</td>
    <td id='p118'>118. The Buddha told Māra, “You just wait a while. It won’t be long before the Tathāgata will be leaving. After three months, he will enter the great realm of <em>nirvāṇa</em> that has no remainder.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
