    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388a9'>時魔作念： 「沙門喬答摩，出言無二。 定般涅槃。」 情生歡喜，忽然隱沒。</td>
    <td>Then Māra thought, “The ascetic Gautama does not speak duplicitously. Surely, he will <em>parinirvāṇa</em>!” Feeling joy, he instantly disappeared.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388a11'>佛作是念： 「我今宜可入如是定，隨彼定力，留其命行、捨其壽行。」 作是念已，便即入定，留命行、捨壽行。 于時，大地悉皆振動，四方熾然星光墮落，於虛空中天鼓自鳴。 佛從定出，說伽他曰：</td>
    <td id='p119'>119. The Buddha thought, “It would be fitting now for me to enter such a <em>samādhi</em> the power of which will bring an end to my life and discard my life span.” After he had this thought, he entered <em>samādhi</em>, ended his life, and discarded his life span. Thereupon, the whole Earth shook, fiery meteors fell down in all directions, and a heavenly drum sounded in the sky. The Buddha then emerged from that <em>samādhi</em> and spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.388a16'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「諸有等不等，<br/>
    牟尼悉已除，<br/>
    由得內證定，<br/>
    如鳥破於㲉。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“Those who are equals to the unequaled,<br/>
    Those sages have all been eliminated.<br/>
    Because of that <em>samādhi</em> of inner realization,<br/>
    He was like a bird breaking out of its egg.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388a18'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>15. The Causes of Earthquakes</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388a18'>時具壽阿難陀於日晡時從宴坐起，便詣佛所。 頂禮佛足，在一面立白言： 「世尊，何因緣故大地振動？」</td>
    <td id='p120'>120. Then, the elder Ānanda rose from his seat of repose in the afternoon and went to the Buddha. He bowed his head at the Buddha’s feet, stood to one side, and said, “Bhagavān, what was the cause and condition for that great earthquake?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388a20'>佛告阿難陀： 「有八因緣大地振動。 云何為八？ 今此大地依水而住。 水依風住、風依空住。 阿難陀，有時空中現大猛風水即波動。 水若搖動地即振動。 阿難陀，此是初因緣大地振動。</td>
    <td id='p121'>121. The Buddha told Ānanda, “There are eight causes and conditions for great earthquakes. What are the eight? Now, this entire Earth rests on water and abides there. The water rests on air and abides there, and the air rests on space and abides there. Ānanda, there are times when a fierce gale arises in space that creates waves in the water. If the water is agitated, then the Earth shakes. Ānanda, this is the first cause and condition for a great earthquake.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388a25'>「復次阿難陀，苾芻有大威德具大功用。 以神通力令此大地，為小塵想入無邊水想，欲令大地悉皆振動。 若苾芻尼及諸天眾大威德者，若作此想亦使大地悉皆振動。 阿難陀，此是第二因緣大地振動。</td>
    <td id='p122'>122. “Furthermore, Ānanda, a monk makes use of the great dignity and great merits he possesses. With the power of his miraculous abilities, he causes this whole Earth to be perceived as a small particle entering limitless water in order to cause the whole Earth to be shook. Or a nun or one of the host of gods with great dignity might make use of this perception to cause the whole Earth to shake. Ānanda, this is the second cause and condition for a great earthquake.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388b1'>「復次阿難陀，若大菩薩從覩史多天下降母胎時，大地振動，諸世界中光明晃耀，倍勝天光。 世間所有極幽闇處，假使日月具大威光而不能照，菩薩現生母腹之時，光明赫奕悉皆普照。 諸有情類從生以來，欲見自手尚不能覩，因光照了互得相見。 知餘有情亦生於此。 阿難陀，此是第三因緣大地振動。</td>
    <td id='p123'>123. “Furthermore, Ānanda, when a great <em>bodhisattva</em> descends from the Tuṣita Heaven into his mother’s womb, there is a great earthquake, and a brilliant light twice as bright as the heavens shines throughout the worlds. In the darkest places that exist in the world where even the great radiance of the sun and moon aren’t able to shine, the light is dazzling and illuminates everything when the <em>bodhisattva</em> is born from his mother’s side. The sentient beings [in those dark places] from their birth to the present can’t see their own hands when they want to look at them, but they can see each other’s form due to that illumination. They realize then that there are other sentient beings who were born there. Ānanda, this is the third cause and condition for a great earthquake.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388b8'>「復次阿難陀，若大菩薩初生之時，大地振動⋯廣如上說⋯ 此是第四因緣大地振動。</td>
    <td id='p124'>124. “Furthermore, Ānanda, there is a great earthquake when a great <em>bodhisattva</em> is first born … <em>and so on as above</em> … This is the fourth cause and condition for a great earthquake.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388b10'>「復次阿難陀，若菩薩成正等覺時，大地振動⋯廣如上說⋯ 此是第五因緣大地振動。</td>
    <td id='p125'>125. “Furthermore, Ānanda, when a <em>bodhisattva</em> achieves complete awakening, there is a great earthquake … <em>and so on as above</em> … This is the fifth cause and condition for a great earthquake.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388b12'>「復次阿難陀，若如來三轉法輪時，大地振動⋯亦如上說⋯ 此是第六因緣大地振動。</td>
    <td id='p126'>126. “Furthermore, Ānanda, when a <em>tathāgata</em> turns the Dharma wheel three times, there is a great earthquake … <em>and so on as above</em> … This is the sixth cause and condition for a great earthquake.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388b14'>「復次阿難陀，若如來留命行捨壽行時，大地振動。 四面熾然流光赫奕，於虛空中天鼓自鳴。 此是第七因緣大地振動。</td>
    <td id='p127'>127. “Furthermore, Ānanda, when a <em>tathāgata</em> stops his life force and discards his life span, there is a great earthquake. A blazing light flows in four directions that’s brilliant, and heavenly drums sound in the sky. This is the seventh cause and condition for a great earthquake.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388b17'>「復次阿難陀，如來不久却後三月，入無餘依妙涅槃界。 於此時中大地振動，四維上下朗然明照，於虛空中諸天叫聲猶如擊鼓。 阿難陀，此是第八因緣大地振動。」</td>
    <td id='p128'>128. “Furthermore, Ānanda, not long after three months, a <em>tathāgata</em> enters the wonderful realm of <em>nirvāṇa</em> that has no more basis. Thereupon, there is a great earthquake, a clear radiance in the four intercardinals and up and down, and a roaring cry from the gods in the sky like someone beating a drum. Ānanda, this is the eighth cause and condition for a great earthquake.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388b21'>爾時具壽阿難陀白佛言： 「世尊，我觀如來所說之事，為留命行捨壽行，因此大地悉皆振動。」</td>
    <td id='p129'>129. The elder Ānanda then said to the Buddha, “Bhagavān, examining these things taught by the Tathāgata, it was the stopping of his life force and discarding of his life span that caused this great earthquake.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388b23'>佛告阿難陀：「如是，如是！我留命行、捨壽行。」</td>
    <td>The Buddha told Ānanda, “So it is, so it is! I stopped my life force and discarded my life span.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388b24'>阿難陀言： 「大德，我親聞佛作如是說： 『若有能於四神足修習多修習者，欲住一劫、若過一劫，皆得自在。』 大德世尊於四神足已修習、多修習。 唯願世尊，住世一劫！ 唯願善逝，住過一劫！」</td>
    <td id='p130'>130. Ānanda said, “Venerable, I personally heard the Buddha say this: ‘If someone cultivates the four miraculous abilities and cultivates them often, they can live for an eon or more if they want. They have that freedom.’ The Venerable Bhagavān has cultivated the four miraculous abilities and cultivated them often. Please, Bhagavān, remain in the world for an eon! Please, Well Gone One, remain for more than an eon!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388b28'>佛告阿難陀： 「是汝之過作斯非理。 我已再三分明告汝，汝自不能知其意趣。 由魔波卑惑亂汝心。 阿難陀，汝意云何？ 諸佛如來言有二不？」</td>
    <td id='p131'>131. The Buddha told Ānanda, “It was your mistake to act senselessly. I clearly told you this three times, and you couldn’t understand my point. Māra the Wicked One had confused your mind. Ānanda, what do you think? Do <em>buddha</em>s or <em>tathāgata</em>s speak duplicitously?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388c2'>白言： 「不爾。」</td>
    <td>He answered, “They do not.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388c2'>佛言：「善哉！善哉！阿難陀，如來大師出二言者，無有是處。 我已許魔，汝無宜請。 阿難陀，汝今可往取弓塔邊側近苾芻，皆令普集常食堂中。」</td>
    <td id='p132'>132. The Buddha said, “Good, good! Ānanda, it’s impossible for the Tathāgata, the great teacher, to speak duplicitously. I gave my assent to Māra [to enter <em>nirvāṇa</em>] when you made no request of me. Ānanda, you may go over by Cāpāla Shrine and have the monks all assemble at the hall of regular alms.”<sup id="ref33"><a href="#n33">33</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388c5'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>16. The Buddha’s Last Look at Vaiśālī</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388c5'>時阿難陀即往遍告眾。 既集已，詣世尊所，頂禮佛足合掌白言： 「大德世尊，諸苾芻眾咸悉來集常食堂所。 願佛知時。」</td>
    <td id='p133'>133. Ānanda then went around telling the community. Once they had gathered there, he went to the Buddha, bowed his head at the Buddha’s feet, and said with his palms together, “Venerable Bhagavān, the assembly of monks have all come for a meeting at the hall of regular alms. The Buddha may go when he decides it’s time.” </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388c8'>佛從座起，至其堂內，就座而坐。 告諸苾芻：「汝等觀察，諸行無常，是變易法，不可委信。 深可厭捨而求解脫。 汝等當知，有勝妙法能於現世得利樂住，未來世中亦復利樂。 汝等苾芻宜於此法受持讀誦、善解其義、謹慎奉行。 能令梵行久住不滅。 如是之法便得弘廣利益有情，哀愍一切安樂人天。</td>
    <td id='p134'>134. The Buddha rose from his seat, went into the hall, prepared a seat, and sat down. He then told the monks, “All of you observe how actions are impermanent, things liable to change, and untrustworthy. Be deeply averse to them and pursue liberation. All of you should know, there is a superior Dharma that can bring you happiness in the present world and also happiness in the future world. All of you monks ought to maintain and recite that Dharma, understand its meaning well, and take care in putting it into practice. It ensures that the religious practice will last a long time and not disappear. Such a Dharma brings broad benefits to sentient beings and pity to all humans and gods for their well-being.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388c14'>「云何勝法能得現世利樂及後世利樂？ 若諸苾芻受持讀誦、善解其義、謹慎奉行，能令梵行久住不滅。 如是之法便得弘廣，利益有情哀愍一切安樂人天。 所謂四念處、四正勤、四神足、五根、五力、七覺分、八聖道。 當知此是現法利樂及後世利樂。 應當讀誦，受持勿忘。」</td>
    <td id='p135'>135. “How does this superior Dharma bring happiness in the present world and happiness in the later world? If monks maintain and recite it, understand its meaning well, and take care to put it into practice, it ensures the religious practice will last a long time and not disappear. Such a Dharma brings broad benefits to sentient beings and pity to all humans and gods for their well-being. That is to say, the four abodes of mindfulness, four right efforts, four miraculous abilities, five faculties, five powers, seven factors of awakening, and the noble eightfold path. You should know that these are the benefits and happiness in the present and the benefits and happiness in the later world. One should recite and maintain it without forgetting it.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388c20'>佛告阿難陀： 「我今欲往重患村中。」</td>
    <td id='p136'>136. The Buddha told Ānanda, “Now, I am going to the village Kuṣṭha.”<sup id="ref34"><a href="#n34">34</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388c21'>時阿難陀聞佛教已，即隨佛後。 世尊行至廣嚴城西北園林之界。 如大象王全身右顧望廣嚴城。 (躬行此處，親為敬禮。 願於像末，聖教流通。)</td>
    <td id='p137'>137. Once he had heard the Buddha’s instruction, Ānanda then followed after the Buddha. The Bhagavān walked to a grove on the northwest border of Vaiśālī. There, he turned his body to the right and looked back at Vaiśālī as a great elephant king would. (He walked to this place where his friends had paid their respects to him. He wanted a last image of the noble teaching being promulgated.)</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388c23'>時阿難陀白言： 「世尊，如來右旋徘徊周望城郭，非無因緣。 唯願為說。」</td>
    <td id='p138'>138. Ānanda then said, “Bhagavān, the Tathāgata doesn’t turn to the right to look back at a city for no reason. Please tell me what it is!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388c25'>佛告阿難陀： 「我今右旋顧視，如汝所言，非無因緣。 阿難陀，此是如來、應、正等覺，於最末後望廣嚴城。 我今欲往力士生處娑羅雙樹入般涅槃。 不復重來，所以迴顧望此城邑。」</td>
    <td id='p139'>139. The Buddha told Ānanda, “I didn’t just now turn to the right and look back for no reason, as you say. Ānanda, this will be the Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One’s last look at Vaiśālī. Now, I am going to a pair of sal trees in the homeland of warriors to enter <em>parinirvāṇa</em>. I will not come here again, which is why I turned around to look back at the city.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.388c29'>時有苾芻聞佛語已，說伽他曰：</td>
    <td id='p140'>140. After hearing what the Buddha said, there was a monk who spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.389a1'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「最後迴顧望嚴城，<br/>
    正覺不復還來此。<br/>
    今欲詣彼雙林處，<br/>
    壯士生地證無餘。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“Turning for a last look at Vaiśālī,<br/>
    The Awakened One would not return there again.<br/>
    Now he is going to that place between two sal trees<br/>
    In the homeland of warriors to realize no remainder.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389a3'>世尊既至重患村已，住升攝波林，告諸苾芻： 「汝等當知，此戒、定、慧。 由習戒故，定便久住。 善修定故，淨慧得生。 由有慧故，於欲、瞋、癡而得解脫。 於如是等心解脫處。 聖弟子眾而實了知： 『我生已盡、梵行已立、所作已辦。 不受後有。』」</td>
    <td id='p141'>141. When the Buddha had reached the village Kuṣṭha, he stayed in the rosewood grove, where he addressed the monks, “All of you should know, this is moral conduct, <em>samādhi</em>, and wisdom. <em>Samādhi</em> lasts a long time as a result of cultivating the precepts. Pure wisdom can arise because <em>samādhi</em> is cultivated well. One can be freed from desire, anger, and delusion as a result of possessing wisdom. In these ways, the mind dwells in freedom. The assembly of noble disciples truly understands: ‘My births have been ended, the religious practice has been established, and the task has been accomplished. I won’t be subject to a later existence.’”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389a8'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>17. Causes of the Second Earthquake</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389a8'>如是次第經過十餘聚落。 皆為眾生隨機說法，至受用城北林而住。 于時大地悉皆振動，四維上下烟焰洞然。 日月無光，流星墮落，於虛空界天鼓自鳴。</td>
    <td id='p142'>142. In this way, he passed through ten other villages in succession. He inspired sentient beings with his Dharma teachings until he reached the city Bhoga and stayed in a grove to its north.<sup id="ref35"><a href="#n35">35</a></sup> At that point, there was a great earthquake that shook the whole Earth, and smoke and fire blazed in the four intercardinals, up, and down. There was no light from the sun and moon, meteors fell, and the heavenly drums sounded in the sky.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389a11'>時阿難陀於日晡時，從宴坐起往至佛所。 頂禮雙足，在一面立，合掌白言： 「大德世尊，何因緣故大地振動？」</td>
    <td id='p143'>143. That afternoon, Ānanda rose from his seat of repose and went to the Buddha. He bowed his head at the Buddha’s feet, stood to one side, and then said with his palms together, “Venerable Bhagavān, what was the cause and condition for that great earthquake?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389a13'>佛告阿難陀： 「三因緣故大地振動。 云何為三？ 而此大地依水而住、水依風住、風依空住。 空中風擊水即波生，水若波浪地即振動。 阿難陀，此是初因緣大地振動。</td>
    <td id='p144'>144. The Buddha told Ānanda, “This great earthquake had three causes and conditions. What were the three? This Earth rests on water, that water rests on wind, and that wind rests on space. When a gale from that space hits the water, it produces waves, and the Earth is shaken if there are waves in the water. Ānanda, this is the first cause and condition for this great earthquake.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389a18'>「復次阿難陀，若苾芻有大威德具大功用，以神通力令此大地為小塵想作無邊水想，能使大地悉皆振動。 若苾芻尼及諸天大威德者，令大地動亦皆振動。 阿難陀，此是第二因緣大地振動⋯如前廣說⋯</td>
    <td id='p145'>145. “Furthermore, Ānanda, if a monk with great dignity and great merit to use could cause the Earth to shake using his miraculous abilities by imagining this Earth to be a small particle formed in limitless water. A nun or the gods with great dignity could also cause the whole Earth to shake. Ānanda, this is the second cause and condition for this great earthquake … <em>as was explained before</em> …</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389a23'>「復次阿難陀，若如來不久入般涅槃，即大地動⋯如前廣說⋯ 阿難陀，此是第三因緣大地振動。」</td>
    <td id='p146'>146. “Furthermore, Ānanda, if it isn’t long before a <em>tathāgata</em> will enter <em>parinirvāṇa</em>, then there is a great earthquake … <em>as was explained before</em> … Ānanda, this is the third cause and condition for a great earthquake.”<sup id="ref36"><a href="#n36">36</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389a25'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>18. The Extraordinary Feats of the Buddha</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389a25'>時阿難陀白言： 「世尊，希有，大德乃能成就如是不思議事！ 如來、應、正等覺不久將欲入大涅槃。 由斯義故大地振動，現希有相。」</td>
    <td id='p147'>147. Ānanda then said, “Bhagavān, it’s extraordinary that great virtue can accomplish such inconceivable things! It won’t be long before the Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One will enter the great <em>nirvāṇa</em>. It was for this reason there was a great earthquake that manifests such amazing signs.”<sup id="ref37"><a href="#n37">37</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389a27'>如前廣說⋯佛言： 「如是，如是！ 如汝所說，如來、應、正等覺，實能成就如是希有之法。 阿難陀，我昔曾於無量百千剎帝利眾令彼瞻覩，我於爾時隨其形量長短分齊，我即與彼形相共同。顏色、音聲亦皆相似。 彼所說義我亦同說。 其不了者我為說之，以勝上法示教利喜令開悟已我便隱沒。 彼亦不知我何所在。 作如是語： 『彼何處去？ 為天？為人？ 非我境界。』</td>
    <td id='p148'>148. <em>As was said before in detail</em> … The Buddha said, “So it is, so it is! As you’ve said, the Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One really is able to accomplish such amazing things. Ānanda, there was a time once when I made an assembly of measureless hundreds of thousands of warriors look up to me, and I followed the measure of their forms in terms their length so that my body and theirs were alike. My countenance and voice were also resembled theirs. The subjects that they discussed were the same subjects that I discussed. I discussed for them what they did not understand, teaching them the higher Dharma plainly, which benefited and gladdened them, and then I disappeared once they realized it. They did not know where I went, either. They said, ‘Where did he go? Was he a god? Was he a man? He was not from our realm.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389b6'>「阿難陀，我能成就如是無量希有之法。 如剎帝利眾、沙門、婆羅門、長者、居士眾中，悉皆如是。 欲界、色界乃至色究竟天，我皆往彼隨其形量長短分齊」⋯廣如上說，乃至⋯「阿難陀，我能成就如是無量希有之法。」</td>
    <td id='p149'>149. “Ānanda, I’m able to accomplish such measureless and amazing things. As with that community of warriors, I did the same among communities of ascetics, brahmins, prominent men, and householders. In the desire realm or form realm up to the Akaniṣṭha Heaven, I followed the measure of their forms in terms of their length  … <em>and so on as described above up to</em> … Ānanda, I’m able to accomplish such measureless and amazing things.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389b12'>根本說一切有部毘奈耶 雜事 卷第三十六</td>
    <td class="fasc_heading"></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389b15'>根本說一切有部毘奈耶 雜事 卷第三十七</td>
    <td class="fasc_heading"></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389b18'>三藏法師義淨奉　制譯</td>
    <td class="taisho_byline"></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389b19'>第八門第十子攝頌之餘</td>
    <td class="ch_heading"></td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389b19'>(說四黑四白法，四種沙門，次出廣嚴城，向涅槃處)</td>
  <td class='subheading'></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389b21'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>19. The Four Dark Teachings</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389b21'>爾時，世尊告阿難陀曰： 「如是應知，教有真偽。 始從今日，當依經教、不依於人。 云何依教、不依於人？ 若苾芻來作如是語： 『具壽，我從如來親聞是語。 聞已憶持。 說斯經典、說此律教，真是佛語。』 此苾芻聞彼說時，不應勸讚亦勿毀、訾。 應聽其語善持文句，當歸住處檢閱經文及以律教。 若彼所說與經律相違者，應告彼言： 『具壽，汝所說者非是佛語。 是汝惡取，不依經律。 當須捨棄。』</td>
    <td id='p151'>151. It was then that the Bhagavān told Ānanda, “You should know in this way, there are true teachings and fake ones.<sup id="ref38"><a href="#n38">38</a></sup> Starting today, you must rely on the <em>sūtra</em> teachings and not rely on a person. How will you rely on the teaching and not rely on a person? Suppose a monk comes and says this: ‘Elder, I personally have heard these words by the Tathāgata. Having heard it, I memorized it. I recite this <em>sūtra</em> and recite this <em>vinaya</em> teaching, which are truly the Buddha’s words.’ When this monk is heard saying this, you should not praise or criticize him. You should listen to what he says is the well remembered text, and then you must go back to your dwelling and review the text of the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em> teaching. If what that other monk said contradicts the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em>, you should tell him, ‘Elder, what you have said is not the Buddha’s words. You have grasped this badly, for it does not rely on the <em>sūtra</em>s and <em>vinaya</em>. You need to discard it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389c1'>「復次阿難陀，若苾芻來作如是語： 『具壽，我於某住處見有大眾，多是耆宿善明律藏。 我於彼處親聞是語。 聞已憶持。 皆依經律，真是佛語。』 此苾芻聞彼說時，不應勸讚亦不毀訾。 應聽其語善持文句，當歸住處檢閱經文及以律教。 若彼所說與經律相違者，應告彼言： 『具壽，汝所說者非是佛語。 是汝惡取，不依經律。 當須捨棄。』</td>
    <td id='p152'>152. “Furthermore, Ānanda, suppose a monk comes and says this: ‘Elder, I’ve been living in a certain place where there is a large community with many elders who elucidate the <cite>Vinaya Piṭaka</cite> well. I have personally learned this saying from that place. Having heard it, I memorized it. I rely on this <em>sūtra</em> and <em>vinaya</em>, which are truly the Buddha’s words.’ When this monk is heard saying this, you should not praise or criticize him. You should listen to what he says is the well remembered text, and then you must go back to your dwelling and review the text of the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em> teaching. If what that other monk said contradicts the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em>, you should tell him, ‘Elder, what you have said is not the Buddha’s words. You have grasped this badly, for it does not rely on the <em>sūtra</em>s and <em>vinaya</em>. You need to discard it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389c9'>「復次阿難陀，若苾芻來作如是語： 『具壽，我於某住處見有眾多苾芻，皆持經、持律、持母經。 我於彼處親聞是語。 聞已憶持。 皆依經律，真是佛語。』 此苾芻聞彼說時，不應勸讚亦不毀訾。 應聽其語善持文句，當歸住處檢閱經文及以律教。 若彼所說與經律相違者，應告彼言： 『具壽，汝所說者非是佛語。 是汝惡取，不依經律。 當須捨棄。』</td>
    <td id='p153'>153. “Furthermore, Ānanda, suppose a monk comes and says this: ‘Elder, I’ve been living in a certain place where there is a community of many monks who remember the <em>sūtra</em>s, remember the <em>vinaya</em>, and remember the matrix <em>sūtra</em>s. I have personally learned this saying from that place. Having heard it, I memorized it. I rely on this <em>sūtra</em> and <em>vinaya</em>, which is truly the Buddha’s words.’ When this monk is heard saying this, you should not praise or criticize him. You should listen to what he says is the well remembered text, and then you must go back to your dwelling and review the text of the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em> teaching. If what that other monk said contradicts the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em>, you should tell him, ‘Elder, what you have said is not the Buddha’s words. You have grasped this badly, for it does not rely on the <em>sūtra</em>s and <em>vinaya</em>. You need to discard it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389c17'>「復次阿難陀，若苾芻來作如是語： 『具壽，我於某住處見一苾芻，是尊宿智者。 我於彼處親聞是語。 聞已憶持。 皆依經律，真是佛語。』 此苾芻聞彼說時，不應勸讚亦不毀訾。 應聽其語善持文句，當歸住處檢閱經文及以律教。 若彼所說與經律相違者，應告彼言： 『具壽，汝所說者非是佛說。 是汝惡取，不依經律。 當須棄捨。』</td>
    <td id='p154'>154. “Furthermore, Ānanda, suppose a monk comes and says this: ‘Elder, I’ve been living in a certain place where I see a monk who is venerable, senior, and knowledgeable. I have personally learned this saying from that place. Having heard it, I memorized it. I rely on this <em>sūtra</em> and <em>vinaya</em>, which is truly the Buddha’s words.’ When this monk is heard saying this, you should not praise or criticize him. You should listen to what he says is the well remembered text, and then you must go back to your dwelling and review the text of the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em> teaching. If what that other monk said contradicts the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em>, you should tell him, ‘Elder, what you have said is not the Buddha’s words. You have grasped this badly, for it does not rely on the <em>sūtra</em>s and <em>vinaya</em>. You need to discard it.’</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389c25'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>20. The Four Light Teachings</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.389c25'>「復次阿難陀，若苾芻來作如是語： 『具壽，我從如來親聞是語。 聞已憶持。 說斯經典、說此律教⋯』 此苾芻聞彼說時，不應勸讚亦勿毀、訾。 應聽其語善持文句，當歸住處檢閱經文及以律教。 若彼所說與經律不相違者，應告彼言： 『具壽，汝所說者真是佛語。 是汝善取，依經律教。 當可受持。』</td>
    <td id='p155'>155. “Furthermore, Ānanda, suppose a monk comes and says this: ‘Elder, I personally have heard these words by the Tathāgata. Having heard it, I memorized it. I recite this <em>sūtra</em> and recite this <em>vinaya</em> teaching[, which are truly the Buddha’s words].’ When this monk is heard saying this, you should not praise or criticize him. You should listen to what he says is the well remembered text, and then you must go back to your dwelling and review the text of the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em> teaching.  If what that other monk said does not contradict the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em>, you should tell him, ‘Elder, what you have said truly is the Buddha’s words. You have grasped this well, for it relies on the <em>sūtra</em>s and <em>vinaya</em> teaching. You need to keep it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390a3'>「復次阿難陀，若苾芻來作如是語： 『具壽，我於某住處見有大眾，多是耆宿善明律藏。 我於彼處親聞是語。 聞已憶持。 皆依經律，真是佛語。』 時此苾芻聞彼說時，不應勸讚亦勿毀訾。 應聽其語善持文句，當歸住處檢閱經文及以律教。 若彼所說與經律不相違者，應告彼言： 『具壽，汝所說者真是佛語。 是汝善取，依經律教。 當可受持。』</td>
    <td id='p156'>156. “Furthermore, Ānanda, suppose a monk comes and says this: ‘Elder, I’ve been living in a certain place where there is a large community with many elders who elucidate the <cite>Vinaya Piṭaka</cite> well. I have personally learned this saying from that place. Having heard it, I memorized it. I rely on this <em>sūtra</em> and <em>vinaya</em>, which are truly the Buddha’s words.’ When this monk is heard saying this, you should not praise or criticize him. You should listen to what he says is the well remembered text, and then you must go back to your dwelling and review the text of the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em> teaching. If what that other monk said does not contradict the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em>, you should tell him, ‘Elder, what you have said truly is the Buddha’s words. You have grasped this well, for it relies on the <em>sūtra</em> and <em>vinaya</em> teaching. You need to keep it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390a11'>「復次阿難陀，若苾芻來作如是語： 『具壽，我於某住處見有眾多苾芻皆持經、律、持母經。 我於彼處親聞是語。 聞已憶持。 皆依經律，真是佛語。』 此苾芻聞彼說時，不應勸讚亦勿毀訾。 應聽其語善持文句，當歸住處檢閱經文及以律教。 若彼所說與經律不相違者，應告彼言： 『具壽，汝所說者真是佛語。 是汝善取，依經律教。 當可受持。』</td>
    <td id='p157'>157. “Furthermore, Ānanda, suppose a monk comes and says this: ‘Elder, I’ve been living in a certain place where there is a community of many monks who remember the <em>sūtra</em>s, remember the <em>vinaya</em>, and remember the matrix <em>sūtra</em>s. I have personally learned this saying from that place. Having heard it, I memorized it. I rely on this <em>sūtra</em> and <em>vinaya</em>, which is truly the Buddha’s words.’ When this monk is heard saying this, you should not praise or criticize him. You should listen to what he says is the well remembered text, and then you must go back to your dwelling and review the text of the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em> teaching. If what that other monk said does not contradict the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em>, you should tell him, ‘Elder, what you have said truly is the Buddha’s words. You have grasped this well, for it relies on the <em>sūtra</em>s and <em>vinaya</em> teaching. You need to keep it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390a19'>「復次阿難陀，若苾芻來作如是語： 『具壽，我於某住處見一苾芻，是尊宿智者。 我於彼處親聞是語。 聞已憶持。 皆依經律，是真佛語。』 此苾芻聞彼說時，不應勸讚亦勿毀訾。 應聽其語善持文句，當歸住處撿閱經文及以律教。 若彼所說與經律不相違者，應告彼言： 『具壽，汝所說者真是佛語。 是汝善取，依經律教。 當可受持。』</td>
    <td id='p158'>158. “Furthermore, Ānanda, suppose a monk comes and says this: ‘Elder, I’ve been living in a certain place where I see a monk who is venerable, senior, and knowledgeable. I have personally learned this saying from that place. Having heard it, I memorized it. I rely on this <em>sūtra</em> and <em>vinaya</em>, which is truly the Buddha’s words.’ When this monk is heard saying this, you should not praise or criticize him. You should listen to what he says is the well remembered text, and then you must go back to your dwelling and review the text of the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em> teaching. If what that other monk said does not contradict the <em>sūtra</em>s and the <em>vinaya</em>, you should tell him, ‘Elder, what you have said truly is the Buddha’s words. You have grasped this well, for it relies on the <em>sūtra</em>s and <em>vinaya</em> teaching. You need to keep it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390a27'>「復次阿難陀，初之四種名大黑說。 汝等苾芻應可善思，至極觀察，深知是惡： 『此非是經、此非是律、非是佛教。 當須捨棄。』</td>
    <td id='p159'>159. “Furthermore, Ānanda, the first four of these are called the great dark teachings. All of you monks should think about them well, examine them to the utmost, and deeply understand them as bad: ‘This is not a <em>sūtra</em>, this is not the <em>vinaya</em>, and this is not the Buddha’s teaching. It needs to be discarded.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390a29'>「後之四種名大白說。 汝等苾芻應可善思，至極觀察深知是善： 『此實是經、此實是律、真是佛教，當善受持。』 阿難陀，是謂苾芻依於經教，不依於人。 如是應學。 若異此者非我所說。」</td>
    <td id='p160'>160. “The last four of these are called the great light teachings. All of you monks should think about them well, examine them to the utmost, and deeply understand them as good: ‘This really is a <em>sūtra</em>, this really is the <em>vinaya</em>, and this really is the Buddha’s teaching. It needs to be maintained well.’ Ānanda, this is how a monk relies on the teaching in the <em>sūtra</em>s and not relying on a person. They should train in this way. If they do otherwise, it’s not something taught by me.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390b5'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>21. At Pāpāgrāmaka</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390b5'>爾時，世尊告阿難陀曰： 「我今欲往波波聚落。」 (波波此云罪惡)</td>
    <td id='p161'>161. It was then that the Bhagavān told Ānanda, “I’m going to the village of Pāpā now.” (<em>Pāpā is the word for evil.</em>)</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390b6'>答曰：「如是。」 世尊是時欲往俱尸那城壯士生地。 漸至波波邑，依折鹿迦林而住。 諸人聞已，眾議同行，出波波邑，往詣佛所。 到已，禮足，在一面坐。 佛為說法示教利喜。</td>
    <td id='p162'>162. He answered, “Yes.” The Bhagavān then headed for Kuśinagarī in the homeland of warriors. They eventually reached the village of Pāpā and stayed in Jalūkā Grove. When people heard about this, they walked together from the village of Pāpā to where the Buddha was staying. When they arrived, they bowed at his feet and sat to one side. The Buddha then taught them the Dharma plainly, benefiting and gladdening them.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390b9'>時此眾中有鍛師之子名曰准陀，亦坐聽法。 時諸大眾既聞法已，辭佛而去。 准陀即便從座而起，整衣服合掌向佛白言： 「世尊，唯願如來與諸聖眾，明日就宅受我微供。」 佛默然受。</td>
    <td id='p163'>163. There was a smith’s son among this assembly named Cunda who also sat down to listen to the Dharma. Once that large crowd had heard the Dharma, they paid their respects to the Buddha and departed. Cunda then rose from his seat, adjusted his robe, and said to the Buddha with his palms together: “Bhagavān, may the Tathāgata and the noble assembly please accept my meager offerings at my house tomorrow!” The Buddha silently accepted.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390b13'>知佛受已，生大歡喜，奉辭而去。 即辦種種上妙香美飲食，敷設座席置清淨水土屑齒木已，遣使白佛： 「飲食已辦。 願佛知時。」</td>
    <td id='p164'>164. Once he knew that the Buddha had accepted, Cunda felt great joy, paid his respects, and departed. After he had prepared a various of wonderful and delicious drinks and foods, arranged seating, and put out clean water and chewing sticks, he sent a messenger to tell the Buddha, “The meals are prepared. The Buddha may come when he decides it’s time.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390b17'>世尊即於日初分時，著衣持鉢，與諸大眾赴其食處。 佛及僧眾就座而坐。 既見坐定，准陀自手持諸供養奉佛聖眾。 于時有一罪惡苾芻，遂竊銅椀藏著腋下。 佛神力故不令人見。 唯佛、准陀見此非法。</td>
    <td id='p165'>165. During the first part of the day, the Bhagavān put on his robe, took his bowl, and went to the place to eat with the great assembly. The Buddha and the assembly of monks prepared their seats and sat down. Seeing that they were seated and settled, Cunda presented his offerings to the Buddha and noble assembly with his own hands. At that moment, there was an evil monk who hid a copper cup under his arm. The Buddha used his miraculous powers to ensure that other people did not see this. Only the Buddha and Cunda witnessed this unlawful thing happen.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390b21'>准陀知佛及僧悉飽滿已，即行淨水豆屑齒木。 屏鉢器澡漱已，是時准陀便持小席在佛前坐。 即以伽他請世尊曰：</td>
    <td id='p166'>166. Once Cunda knew that the Buddha and the assembly had all eaten their fill, he walked around with clean water, soap, fine earth, and chewing sticks. Once their bowls had been washed, Cunda brought a small stool to sit in front of the Buddha. He made this request of the Bhagavān in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390b24'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「我聞牟尼一切智，<br/>
    已超彼岸無疑惑。<br/>
    最勝導師調御士。<br/>
    願說世有幾沙門。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“I heard that the sage who is omniscient,<br/>
    Who crossed to the other shore, has no doubts.<br/>
    He’s the best teacher and trainer of men.<br/>
    Tell me, how many ascetics are in the world.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390b26'>世尊亦以伽他答准陀曰：</td>
    <td id='p167'>167. The Bhagavān answered Cunda in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390b27'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「有四沙門無第五。<br/>
    我今為汝說次第。<br/>
    應知勝道、及示道，<br/>
    淨道、活命并污道。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“There are four ascetics; there is no fifth.<br/>
    Now I will tell you their order.<br/>
    You should know the best path, the clear path,<br/>
    The pure path, and the path of defiled livelihood.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390b29'>准陀復請曰：</td>
    <td id='p168'>168. Cunda made another request:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390c1'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「世尊說何為勝道，<br/>
    云何名為示道者？<br/>
    何者名為淨活命？<br/>
    并污道者願宣揚。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“Bhagavān, tell me, what is the best path?<br/>
    What is called the clear path?<br/>
    Which is called pure livelihood?<br/>
    And please expound on the defiled path!”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390c3'>世尊答曰：</td>
    <td id='p169'>169. The Buddha answered:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390c4'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「能除疑箭斷諸惑。<br/>
    唯希圓寂非餘處。<br/>
    是謂天人之導師，<br/>
    諸佛說斯為勝道。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“Pulling out the arrow of doubt ends all confusion.<br/>
    Rare, indeed, is the place of perfect peace that has no remainder.<br/>
    He is called the teacher to gods and humans,<br/>
    And this is the best path taught by <em>buddha</em>s.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390c6'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>善解第一最勝義，<br/>
    方便顯了微妙法，<br/>
    牟尼能破諸疑網。<br/>
    是名第二示道師。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>To understand the best and highest aim<br/>
    And make plain the sublime Dharma with skill,<br/>
    The sage destroys the net of doubts.<br/>
    This is called the second teacher who shows the way.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390c8'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>若於法句善宣說，<br/>
    依法少欲而活命，<br/>
    於無罪法善能修。<br/>
    是名第三正道活。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>If Dharma verses are well explained,<br/>
    Relying on Dharma and few desires for one’s livelihood,<br/>
    One can cultivate blamelessness.<br/>
    This is called the third correct way of livelihood.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390c10'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>身著沙門解脫衣，<br/>
    常為污家不羞恥，<br/>
    虛誑恒為不實語。<br/>
    是名第四污道人。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Clothing oneself in the ascetic’s robe of liberation,<br/>
    Not ashamed of often being a defiled householder,<br/>
    The deceiver never speaks truthfully.<br/>
    This is called the fourth person of the defiled path.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390c12'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>於大聲聞真法眾，<br/>
    諸在家人當善察。<br/>
    非我弟子悉皆然，<br/>
    是故當須起深信。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Among the community of the great disciple’s genuine Dharma,<br/>
    People living at home will investigate it well.<br/>
    Not actually being my disciples,<br/>
    They therefore will need to produce deep faith.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390c14'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>云何無罪共罪居，<br/>
    淨與不淨同處住？<br/>
    由彼愚人為惡行，<br/>
    令於善士悉生疑。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>What is a blameless and blameworthy abode,<br/>
    Where the pure and impure live equally?<br/>
    That is the result of those foolish people doing evil<br/>
    And causing good men to become doubtful.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390c16'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>勿以色相信前人，<br/>
    少時同聚便委付。<br/>
    麁險人多詐形貌，<br/>
    誑惑常行於世間。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Don’t trust the first person because of their appearance,<br/>
    Nor those in the same community a short time.<br/>
    Crude and dangerous people often fake their appearance,<br/>
    And the mad are always traveling in the world.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390c18'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>如以少金飾耳璫。<br/>
    體即是銅無所直。<br/>
    內假外實如真相，<br/>
    多攝門徒亂善人。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Like an earring painted with a little gold.<br/>
    Being made of copper, nothing’s genuine about it.<br/>
    Fake on the inside, their outside looks genuine,<br/>
    Which confuses good people as they frequent their doors.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390c20'>爾時世尊見鍛師子設供養已，為說隨喜福頌伽他曰：</td>
    <td id='p170'>170. After seeing the smith provide his support, the Bhagavān then spoke this verse to celebrate his merit:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390c22'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「若施福增長，<br/>
    冤讎皆止息。<br/>
    由善能除惡。<br/>
    惑盡證涅槃。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“As a gift’s merit grows larger,<br/>
    One’s misfortunes, they are all stopped.<br/>
    Evil can be removed because of the good.<br/>
    When confusion ends, one realizes <em>nirvāṇa</em>.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390c24'>佛為說法，示教利喜。 作利益已，從座而去。</td>
    <td id='p171'>171. The Buddha then taught him Dharma plainly, which benefited and gladdened him. After bestowing these blessings, he rose from his seat and departed.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390c25'>內攝頌曰：</td>
    <td>The inner summary verse says:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.390c26'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>佛出廣嚴西，<br/>
    迴顧望城郭，<br/>
    經遊十聚落，<br/>
    最後至波波。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li><em>The Buddha left Vaiśālī headed west,<br/>
    Turned around to look back at the city,<br/>
    Passed through ten villages,<br/>
    And finally arrived at Pāpā.</em></li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390c28'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>22. The Buddha Asks for Water</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390c28'>爾時，世尊告阿難陀： 「我今欲往拘尸那城。」 時阿難陀聞佛告已，即隨佛後漸向波波邑。</td>
    <td id='p172'>172. It was then that the Bhagavān told Ānanda, “I’m going to go to Kuśinagarī now.” Having heard what the Buddha told him, Ānanda then followed the Buddha as they made their way from the village of Pāpā.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.390c29'>未到金河，於此中間路邊暫住。 告阿難陀： 「我今背痛。 汝可以我嗢呾羅僧伽疊為四重。 我欲偃臥以自消息。」</td>
    <td id='p173'>173. Before they reached the Golden River,<sup id="ref40"><a href="#n40">40</a></sup> the Buddha stopped to rest on the side of the road on the way. He told Ānanda, “My back is aching now. You can fold my outer robe four times. I’m going to lie down and give myself some rest.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a3'>時阿難陀聞佛教已，即疾疊衣，白言： 「已作，願佛知時。」</td>
    <td id='p174'>174. Once he had heard the Buddha’s instruction, Ānanda quickly folded the robe and said, “It’s done. The Buddha may do so when he decides.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a4'>于時，世尊自疊僧伽胝枕頭，右脇而臥，兩足相重。 作光明想，正念安住，念當速起。 如是作意，復告阿難陀曰：「汝可速往脚俱多河取滿鉢水，吾欲須飲并灑身體。」</td>
    <td id='p175'>175. Thereupon, the Bhagavān used his folded robe as a pillow to rest his head and lay on his right side with both feet on top of each other. Making the perception of light, he rested with right mindfulness, remembering to rise again soon. Having attended to this, he also told Ānanda, “You can go quickly to the Kakustā River and get a bowl full of water.<sup id="ref41"><a href="#n41">41</a></sup> I need it to drink and clean myself.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a8'>時阿難陀聞已持鉢詣彼河邊。 時有五百乘車纔新渡河，水皆渾濁。 便盛滿鉢來至佛所。 白言： 「大德，有五百乘車新渡此河，水皆渾濁， 唯願世尊，將洗手足，不堪飲用。 金河不遠，清水可求。」 佛即受水，洗足拭面。 身稍安隱，即起加趺。 正念現前端身而住。</td>
    <td id='p176'>176. Having heard this, Ānanda took his bowl to the river bank. At the time, five hundred carts had recently crossed the river there, and the water was all muddy. He filled the bowl and came back to the Buddha. He said, “Venerable, five hundred carts recently forded the river, and the water is all muddy. Please, Bhagavān, use this to wash your hands and feet, but don’t use it as drinking water. The Golden River is not far away, and its water is clean and potable.” The Buddha accepted the water, washed his feet and wiped his face with it. When he felt better, he got up and sat cross-legged. He remained there with proper mindfulness of what was in front of him and upright body.<sup id="ref42"><a href="#n42">42</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a14'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>23. Pūrṇa the Malla Prime Minister</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a14'>爾時，有一壯士大臣名曰圓滿。 從此而過，見佛世尊在樹下坐。 容儀端正，眾所樂見。 身心寂靜、極善調柔，如妙金幢光明赫奕。 見已，就禮世尊雙足，在一面坐。 佛問彼曰： 「汝今愛樂沙門清淨法耶？為樂婆羅門法耶？」</td>
    <td id='p177'>177. At the time, the Malla prime minister named Pūrṇa.<sup id="ref43"><a href="#n43">43</a></sup> As he passed by, he saw the Buddha Bhagavān sitting under a tree. The Buddha’s countenance was handsome and delightful to everyone who saw him. His body and mind were tranquil and skillfully flexible, like a fine gold flag shining brilliantly. Having seen this, he bowed at the Bhagavān’s two feet and sat to one side. The Buddha asked him, “Do you like the ascetic’s way of purification now,<sup id="ref44"><a href="#n44">44</a></sup> or do you like the way of the brahmins?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a18'>大臣答言： 「大德，我樂迦羅摩淨法。」</td>
    <td>The prime minister replied, “Venerable, I like Kālāma’s way of purification.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a19'>佛告大臣：「汝復何緣樂彼淨法？」</td>
    <td id='p178'>178. The Buddha asked the prime minister, “Why do you like his way of purification?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a20'>答言：「大德，其迦羅摩曾隨路行，住一樹下。 時有五百乘車於此而過。 經少時間，餘有人來，問彼言曰： 『向見五百乘車於此過不？』</td>
    <td id='p179'>179. He replied, “Venerable, that Kālāma once was traveling on the road and stood under a tree. Five hundred carts passed by him then. A short time after they passed him, another man came along and asked him, ‘Did you see five hundred carts pass by here?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a23'>「答言： 『不見。』</td>
    <td>“He answered, ‘I didn’t see them.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a23'>「又問： 『聞聲不？』</td>
    <td id='p180'>180. “The man also asked, ‘Did you hear them?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a23'>「答言： 『不聞。』</td>
    <td>“He replied, ‘I didn’t hear them.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a23'>「又問： 『仁，豈睡耶？』</td>
    <td id='p181'>181. “The man also asked, ‘Were you asleep, sir?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a24'>「答言： 『不睡。』</td>
    <td>“He replied, ‘I wasn’t asleep.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a24'>「『若不睡者，五百乘車於此而過，何不見聞？』</td>
    <td id='p182'>182. “‘If you weren’t asleep, how did you not see or hear those five hundred carts pass by?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a25'>「答言： 『我不眠睡，心常覺悟，而不見聞，由定力故。』</td>
    <td id='p183'>183. “He replied, ‘I wasn’t asleep, and my mind was awake the whole time, but I didn’t see or hear them because of the strength of my <em>samādhi</em>.’<sup id="ref45"><a href="#n45">45</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a26'>「彼聞是說，便作是念： 『希有上人！ 澄心寂慮乃能如是。』 又，車行震響，塵坌驚飛。 蒙彼身衣而不聞見。 故我於彼發淨信心愛樂其法。」</td>
    <td id='p184'>184. “When he heard him say this, the man thought, ‘What an extraordinary man! His settled mind is so tranquil that he’s capable of this.’ The noise of the carts traveling was thunderous, and he was covered by the dust that they kicked up. His body and robes were covered in it, but he didn’t hear or see them. Therefore, I have pure faith in him and love his Dharma.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391a29'>佛告大臣： 「汝意云何？ 五百乘車所發音響，比虛空中雷震霹靂何者為大？」</td>
    <td id='p185'>185. The Buddha asked the prime minister, “What do you think? Was the sound made by those five hundred carts as loud as thunder and lightning?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391b2'>白言： 「大德，非但五百乘，假令百千萬車作大音響，豈能大於雷震之聲。」</td>
    <td id='p186'>186. He said, “Venerable, not just five hundred carts but even the great noise of hundreds of thousands of ten thousands of carts wouldn’t be as loud as the sound of a thunderclap.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391b3'>「大臣當知，我於先時，在此聚落，住重閣內。 於小食時，執持衣鉢，入村乞食。 食已，收衣鉢洗足竟，於重閣中宴坐而住。 忽然雷震，降大霹靂。 于時，四牛及二耕夫并有長者兄弟二人，聞此大聲，因斯怖懼俱時喪命。 城中，人民高聲大叫。</td>
    <td id='p187'>187. “Prime Minister, you should know, there was a time before when I was staying in the second-floor hall of a village. When it was time for a small meal, I took my robe and bowl and went into the village to solicit alms. After I had eaten, put away my robe and bowl, and washed my feet, I stayed in that second-floor hall and sat in repose. Suddenly, there was a thunderstorm and a great thunderclap. Thereupon, four oxen and two farmers who were brothers of a prominent man heard that loud noise and died as a result of being terrified by it. In the city, people screamed loudly.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391b8'>「我於爾時，從宴坐起，出閣經行。 時有一人從城出外，來詣我所。 頂禮我足，隨我經行。 我便告曰： 『何故城中共出大聲、有大喧鬧？』</td>
    <td id='p188'>188. “At that point, I rose from my seat of repose and went out for a walk. There was then someone who came out of the city and came to visit me. They bowed their head at my feet and walked along with me. I asked them, ‘Why was there a loud noise and then a great uproar in the city?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391b11'>「彼白我言： 『城中向來天忽雷震，降大霹靂。 四牛及二耕夫，并長者兄弟二人，因斯怖懼俱時喪命。 因此城內共出大聲。』 彼問我言： 『大德，豈可不聞此大震聲？』</td>
    <td id='p189'>189. “They said to me, ‘There was a sudden thunderstorm that came and caused a great thunderclap in the city. Four oxen and two farmers who were brothers of a prominent man died as a result of being terrified by it. That was why there was a great uproar inside the city.’ They then asked me, ‘Venerable, didn’t you hear the sound of that great thunderclap?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391b15'>「我報： 『不聞。』</td>
    <td>“I replied, ‘I didn’t hear it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391b15'>「彼復白言： 『世尊睡耶？』</td>
    <td id='p190'>190. “They again asked, ‘Was the Bhagavān asleep?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391b15'>「報言： 『不睡，我雖內覺，而不外聞。』</td>
    <td id='p191'>191. “I replied, ‘I wasn’t asleep, but I was inwardly aware and not listening to what was outside.’<sup id="ref46"><a href="#n46">46</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391b16'>「彼作是念： 『希有！如來、應、正等覺寂靜而住，大雷震吼而不聞聲。』 即於我所發淨信心。」</td>
    <td id='p192'>192. “They thought, ‘Extraordinary! The Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One was peacefully abiding and didn’t hear the sound of that great thunderstorm’s roar!’ They then had pure faith in me.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391b18'>圓滿聞已，白言： 「大德，豈有於佛不生敬信？ 我今於佛深起淨心。」 是時圓滿告使者曰： 「汝可將我上新細縷黃金色疊奉覆世尊。」 使者持來，圓滿白佛言： 「世尊，此是上新細縷黃金色疊。 唯願哀愍，為我納受。」 世尊欲令彼獲勝利，即便為受。 圓滿復言： 「大德世尊，我當更欲供養佛僧。 願見聽許。」</td>
    <td id='p193'>193. After he heard this, Pūrṇa said, “Venerable, how could anyone not have respect and faith in the Buddha? Now, I’ve had a pure thought about the Buddha occur to me deeply.” Then Pūrṇa told a messenger, “Go and bring me a newly and finely woven golden cloth to present to the Bhagavān.” When the messenger returned with it, Pūrṇa said to the Buddha, “Bhagavān, this is a newly and finely woven golden cloth. Please take pity on me and accept it.” The Bhagavān wanted him to obtain an excellent blessing, so he accepted it. Pūrṇa again said, “Venerable Bhagavān, I would like to give support to the Buddha and the community. Please give your consent to this.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391b25'>佛言： 「斯為善事。」 見佛受已，歡喜踊躍。 頂禮佛足，奉辭而去。</td>
    <td id='p194'>194. The Buddha said, “This is a good thing.” Once he saw the Buddha had accepted, Pūrṇa was overjoyed and celebrated. He bowed his head at the Buddha’s feet, paid his respects, and departed.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391b26'>佛告具壽阿難陀： 「此金色黃疊以刀截縷。 我今欲著。」 時，阿難陀聞佛教已，即便以刀截去縷𦆠，持奉世尊。 佛即為著，佛身威光令衣金色無復光彩。</td>
    <td id='p195'>195. The Buddha told the elder Ānanda, “Cut this golden cloth into strips. I want to wear it.” Having heard the Buddha’s instruction, Ānanda used a knife to cut the cloth into strips and presented them to the Bhagavān. The Buddha put them on, and his body glowed with a golden splendor as never before.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391b29'>時，阿難陀白言： 「大德世尊，我隨佛後二十餘年，未曾覩佛如是顏容威光赫奕。 何因緣故現斯光明非常昞著？」</td>
    <td id='p196'>196. Ānanda then said, “Venerable Bhagavān, after following the Buddha for some twenty years, the Buddha’s countenance has never glowed so brilliantly. What is the cause and condition for his radiance to appear so unusual and remarkable?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391c4'>佛告阿難陀： 「有二因緣現其光相異於常日。 云何為二？ 一者、若菩薩即於此夜證阿耨多羅三藐三菩提。 二者、如來即於此夜入無餘依大涅槃界。 於此二時現斯勝相。 又阿難陀，我往金河。」</td>
    <td id='p197'>197. The Buddha told Ānanda, “There are two causes and conditions for my sign of light to appear different from regular sunlight. What are the two? The first is if it’s the night that a <em>bodhisattva</em> realizes the unsurpassed, correct, and complete awakening. The second is on the night that a <em>tathāgata</em> will enter the great realm of <em>nirvāṇa</em> that has no remaining basis. This supreme sign appears at these two times. Now, Ānanda, I’m going to the Golden River.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391c8'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>24. Cunda’s Reward and Chandaka’s Punishment</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391c8'>阿難陀聞佛教已，即隨佛後，至彼河所。 佛即脫衣，置於岸上。 唯著洗衣，入河洗浴。 出已，拭身，告阿難陀曰： 「准陀必當生追悔心。 汝可安慰報言： 『准陀，汝今多獲善利。 能為最後供養，大師受斯施已入無餘涅槃者，甚為難遇。』</td>
    <td id='p198'>198. Having heard the Buddha’s instruction, Ānanda then followed the Buddha to that river. The Buddha took off his robe and put it on the riverbank. He entered the river to wash himself wearing only his bathing cloth. After he came out, he dried himself and told Ānanda, “Cunda must be having regrets. You could console him by saying, ‘Cunda, you have obtained many good blessings now. It’s a very rare thing to be the last to give support prior to the great teacher entering the [realm of] <em>nirvāṇa</em> without remainder.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391c13'>「應知，准陀有二種因心生追悔。 應為開解作如是語： 『准陀，我自於佛親聞是語： 「有二種施所受果報無與等者： 為菩薩時受其食已，便證無上正等菩提；及以如來受最後食，入無餘依妙涅槃界。」』 阿難陀，此二種施，所獲果報無與等者。 阿難陀應知，准陀為長壽業、為多力業。 美貌、生天、財、食、貴、勝眷屬等業悉皆增長。」</td>
    <td id='p199'>199. “You should know that Cunda will be having regrets for two reasons. You should disclose them to him by telling him, ‘Cunda, I’ve heard this from Buddha himself: “There are two kinds of gifts that will bring unparalleled fruits to someone: When a <em>bodhisattva</em> realizes the unsurpassed, correct, and complete awakening after accepting a meal and when a Tathāgata enters the wonderful realm of <em>nirvāṇa</em> that has no remaining basis after receiving his final meal.”’ Ānanda, these two types of gifts bring unparalleled fruits to someone. Ānanda, you should know that Cunda performed a deed of long life and a deed of many powers. His deeds of having a beautiful countenance, birth in heaven, wealth, food, nobility, and superior friends and family will all grow.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391c20'>爾時具壽阿難陀白言： 「世尊，闡陀苾芻性懷猛惡多瞋造次。 於諸苾芻常出不順麁惡言詞。 佛滅度後云何共住？」</td>
    <td id='p200'>200. Then, the elder Ānanda said, “Bhagavān, the monk Chandaka has a pernicious disposition and often has angry moments. He regularly subjects the monks to disagreeable, crude, and bad criticisms. How shall we share our abode with him after the Buddha’s extinguishment?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391c23'>佛告阿難陀： 「我滅度後，闡陀惡性苾芻應默擯治之。 彼被治時，若生憂悔，起敬仰心，眾知改者，共施歡喜如常共語。」</td>
    <td id='p201'>201. The Buddha told Ānanda, “After my extinguishment, the monks should deal with Chandaka’s bad disposition with silence. If he becomes remorseful and becomes respectful when he is treated this way, the community will know he has changed. They can then share alms, rejoice, and speak with him as usual.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391c26'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>25. The Seven Factors of Awakening</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.391c26'>世尊復告阿難陀： 「我今欲往拘尸那城。」 阿難陀言： 「如世尊教。」 即隨佛後往壯士生地。 既渡金河，去城不遠於路邊住。 告阿難陀曰： 「我今背痛。 汝可以我嗢呾羅僧伽疊為四重。 我欲偃臥以自消息。」</td>
    <td id='p202'>202. The Bhagavān also told Ānanda, “Now, I am going to Kuśinagarī.” Ānanda replied, “As the Bhagavān instructs.” He then followed after the Buddha as he went to the homeland of warriors. After crossing the Golden River, they were not far from the city when they stopped on the side of the road. The Buddha told Ānanda, “My back is aching now. You can fold my outer robe four times. I’m going to lie down and give myself some rest.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392a1'>時阿難陀聞佛教已，即疾疊衣，白言： 「已作，願佛知時。」 于時，世尊自疊僧伽胝枕頭右脇而臥⋯具說如前。</td>
    <td id='p203'>203. Once he had heard the Buddha’s instruction, Ānanda quickly folded his robe and said, “It’s done. The Buddha may do so when he decides.” Thereupon, the Bhagavān used his folded robe as a pillow to rest his head and lay on his right side … <em>and so on as was described before</em>.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392a4'>復告阿難陀：「汝當宣說覺分之法。」</td>
    <td id='p204'>204. Again, he told Ānanda, “You ought to recite the teaching of the factors of awakening.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392a4'>時阿難陀白言： 「大德世尊，於此覺分自證、自覺親，為我說。 依於閑靜、依於離欲、依於寂滅，斷諸緣務，勤修於念、擇法、精進、喜、安、定、捨。 此覺分法，大德世尊自證自覺之所宣說。」</td>
    <td id='p205'>205. Ānanda then said, “The Venerable Bhagavān has taught me the factors of awakening, which he himself has realized and discovered. Relying on seclusion, relying on being free of desires, relying on tranquility, and abandoning worldly duties, a person diligently cultivates mindfulness, analysis of the Dharma, energy, joy, calm, <em>samādhi</em>, and equanimity. These are the factors of awakening that were declared by the Venerable Bhagavān, which he himself has realized and discovered.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392a8'>「阿難陀，汝說如是七覺分法，依閑靜等若多修習勤精進者，當得無上正等菩提。」 說是語已，佛即起坐，正念思惟端身而住。</td>
    <td id='p206'>206. “Ānanda, if someone diligently makes the effort to cultivate these seven factors of awakening relying on seclusion, and so on as you’ve recited, they will attain the unsurpassed, correct, and complete awakening.” After he said this, the Buddha rose from his seat and stood with proper mindfulness and upright body.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392a11'>時有苾芻而說頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p207'>207. There was a monk who spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392a13'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「世尊自勸喻，<br/>
    令宣微妙法。<br/>
    可為諸病人，<br/>
    當說菩提分。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“The Bhagavān encouraged himself<br/>
    To promulgate the subtle Dharma.<br/>
    It was perhaps for ailing people<br/>
    That the factors of awakening were recited.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392a15'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>大師身有疾，<br/>
    并為病苾芻。<br/>
    於覺分法門，<br/>
    敷演令開悟。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The great teacher was physically sick,<br/>
    And his monks were ill, too.<br/>
    The factors are a Dharma entrance<br/>
    That woke them up when explained in detail.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392a17'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>善哉阿難陀！<br/>
    白法皆圓滿。<br/>
    聰明有大智，<br/>
    巧說牟尼法。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Excellent, Ānanda!<br/>
    The white Dharma is completely fulfilled.<br/>
    Astute is the one who with great knowledge<br/>
    Skillfully explains the sage’s Dharma.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392a19'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>於正念擇法，<br/>
    精勤喜覺分。<br/>
    輕安及定捨，<br/>
    善能分別說。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Proper mindfulness, Dharma analysis,<br/>
    Diligence, and joy are factors.<br/>
    Calm, <em>samādhi</em>, and equanimity,<br/>
    He could skillfully discern them.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392a21'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>無上調御師，<br/>
    樂聞覺分法。<br/>
    雖身有疾苦，<br/>
    無辭尚起聽。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The teacher of the unsurpassed training<br/>
    Enjoyed hearing the factors of awakening.<br/>
    Although his body had a painful illness,<br/>
    He rose to listen to it without a word.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392a23'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>佛為法主尊，<br/>
    是能開導者。<br/>
    為法尚殷重，<br/>
    何況所餘人？</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The Buddha is the Dharma lord;<br/>
    He is guide who reveals it.<br/>
    If the Dharma is so respected by him,<br/>
    How much would it be by other people?</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392a25'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>復有諸賢聖，<br/>
    於十力教法。<br/>
    假令遭病苦，<br/>
    起聽不辭勞。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>There are also noble ones<br/>
    Who teach Dharma of the ten powers.<br/>
    Were they to encounter a painful illness,<br/>
    They rise to listen without complaining.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392a27'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>此等善持經，<br/>
    及以明律論。<br/>
    尚樂聞正法，<br/>
    餘人何不聽？</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>They all maintain the <em>sūtra</em>s well<br/>
    And understand the <em>vinaya</em> and commentaries.<br/>
    Even they enjoy hearing the right Dharma,<br/>
    So who else wouldn’t listen to it?</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392a29'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>世尊離染教，<br/>
    聞已如說行。<br/>
    繫念法精勤，<br/>
    當得於喜分。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The Bhagavān’s teaching is free of stain;<br/>
    Practice it just as it was learned.<br/>
    Fixing attention on Dharma is diligence,<br/>
    Then one will attain the factor of joy.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392b2'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>由心有喜故，<br/>
    為此身輕安。<br/>
    由安有樂生，<br/>
    從樂生於定。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>As a result of being joyous,<br/>
    This body of theirs becomes light and calm.<br/>
    Because they are calm, happiness arises.<br/>
    From happiness, <em>samādhi</em> arises.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392b4'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>由有妙定捨，<br/>
    了諸行無常。<br/>
    能離三有生，<br/>
    染著心不起。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Sublime <em>samādhi</em> causes equanimity<br/>
    And understanding the impermanence of actions.<br/>
    Parting from birth in the three existences,<br/>
    Affected thoughts do not arise.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392b6'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>能離諸有苦，<br/>
    不樂於人天。<br/>
    證無上涅槃<br/>
    如薪盡火滅。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Parting with the pain of the existences,<br/>
    One doesn’t enjoy being human or divine.<br/>
    Realizing the unsurpassed <em>nirvāṇa</em><br/>
    Is like fire going out when the wood is gone.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.392b8'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>如是大利益<br/>
    皆從聞法生；<br/>
    是故勸臨終，<br/>
    諦聽於妙法。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Such great benefits as these<br/>
    All arise from hearing the Dharma.<br/>
    Therefore, encourage someone close to death<br/>
    To listen closely to the fine Dharma.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392b10'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>26. Between a Pair of Sal Trees</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392b10'>爾時，世尊告具壽阿難陀： 「今可進詣拘尸那城。」 答言「如是，」即隨佛後至於壯士生地，住娑羅林。</td>
    <td id='p208'>208. It was then that the Bhagavān told the elder Ānanda, “We can continue on to Kuśinagarī now.” Ānanda replied, “Yes,” and followed after the Buddha as he went to the homeland of warriors to stay in a sal tree grove.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392b12'>將欲涅槃，告阿難陀曰： 「汝今為我於雙樹間安置床敷。 我當於彼北首而臥。 今日中夜，必入涅槃。」</td>
    <td id='p209'>209. When he was going to <em>nirvāṇa</em>, the Buddha told Ānanda, “Prepare a bed between a pair of sal trees for me. I will lie with my head to the north. I will surely enter <em>nirvāṇa</em> in the middle of the night tonight.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392b14'>時阿難陀如教作已，詣世尊所，頂禮佛足，在一面立。 合掌白言： 「如佛所教並已安置。」 是時如來即往就床，右脇而臥，兩足相重。 作光明想，繫意正念，觀察而住，為涅槃想。</td>
    <td id='p210'>210. Having heard this instruction, Ānanda went to the Buddha, bowed his head at the Buddha’s feet, and stood to one side. With his palms together, he said, “I will place it as the Buddha has instructed.” He then went and prepared the bed for the Tathāgata, who lay on his right side with his two feet on top of each other. Making the perception of light, he fixed his attention with right mindfulness and observed and abided the sign of <em>nirvāṇa</em>.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392b18'>時阿難陀在佛背後憑床而立，悲啼號哭，出大音聲。 作如是語： 「苦哉！痛哉！何期如來速般涅槃？ 何期善逝速般涅槃？ 何期疾哉世間眼滅？ 每於先時諸方苾芻來詣佛所，佛為說法初中後善，文義巧妙純一圓滿，清淨鮮白梵行之相。 我因得聞甚深妙法。 彼於今日聞佛涅槃不復更來。 遂令如是殊勝妙法隱沒於世。」</td>
    <td id='p211'>211. Ānanda then went behind the Buddha’s bed and stood there crying and lamenting loudly. He said, “The suffering! The pain! How could the time of the Tathāgata’s <em>parinirvāṇa</em> be so soon? How could the time of the Sugata’s <em>parinirvāṇa</em> be so soon? How could the time for the world’s eye to go out be so soon? In the past, monks would often come to the Buddha from other regions, and the Buddha would teach them the Dharma that’s good in the beginning, middle, and end, that is skillful in content and meaning, which is whole and complete, and which is the sign of the pure and clean religious practice. That was the reason I could hear the profound and wonderful Dharma. Today, they’ve heard about the Buddha’s <em>nirvāṇa</em> and will not come anymore. This will lead to the disappearance of this extraordinary and supremely wonderful Dharma from the world.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392b25'>佛告諸苾芻： 「阿難陀今在何處？」</td>
    <td id='p212'>212. The Buddha addressed the monks, “Where is Ānanda now?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392b25'>白言： 「世尊，今在佛後憑床悲慟， 作如是語⋯廣說如前，乃至⋯『殊勝妙法隱沒於世。』」</td>
    <td id='p213'>213. They replied, “Bhagavān, he is behind the Buddha’s bed lamenting. He is saying … <em>repeated in full as before up to</em> … ‘the disappearance of this extraordinary and supremely wonderful Dharma from the world.’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392b27'>佛即告阿難陀曰： 「汝勿憂愁，悲泣懊惱。 何以故？ 汝侍如來，作身慈業獲大利樂。 唯獨一身得無邊福。 作口慈業及意慈業，亦復如是得無邊福。</td>
    <td id='p214'>214. The Buddha then told Ānanda, “Don’t be so grief-stricken, crying and heartbroken. Why is that? You attended to the Tathāgata, performing physical deeds of kindness that will bring great blessings and happiness. You are the only person to obtain limitless merits. You performed deeds of verbal and mental kindness that likewise will bring such limitless merits.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392c1'>「阿難陀，過去如來皆有如是供侍之人，如汝用心供侍於我。 未來諸佛亦有供侍，與汝無異。 阿難陀，世相如是皆不久停。 畢歸磨滅無常住者。 以是義故，汝今不應悲啼涕泣生大苦惱。 不見世間從緣生法常住不壞。 我曾為汝廣說法要。 諸有可愛稱意之事，並歸無常悉皆離別。」</td>
    <td id='p215'>215. “Ānanda, <em>tathāgata</em>s in the past all have had attendants like yourself who put your mind to attending to me. <em>Tathāgata</em>s in the future will also have attendants who are no different than yourself. Ānanda, it’s the nature of the world that nothing lasts long. In the end, we are worn away without any permanence. For this reason, you shouldn’t lament, cry, and be so anguished. Don’t look for dependently arisen worldly things to be everlasting and undestroyed. I have taught you the Dharma’s essentials at length. Our possessions that we cherish and praise in our minds will also return to impermanence and leave us.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392c8'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>27. The Extraordinary Feats of Ānanda</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392c8'>爾時，世尊大悲所熏，為令阿難陀生喜悅故，告諸苾芻： 「轉輪聖王成就四種希有之事。 云何為四？ 謂有剎帝利眾來詣王所，既得見王深生慶悅。 復聞妙法倍加歡喜。 如是，復有婆羅門眾、諸長者眾、雜沙門眾，來詣王所⋯如上所說，乃至⋯倍加歡喜。</td>
    <td id='p216'>216. The Bhagavān was suffused with great compassion then and he told the monks this to cheer up Ānanda: “A noble wheel-turning king accomplishes four extraordinary feats. What are the four? There’s an assembly of warriors who come to the king, and they are happy when they see him. Their joy is redoubled when they hear the wonderful Dharma. In this way, he also has an assembly of brahmins, an assembly of well-to-do people, and an assembly of mixed ascetics who come to the king … <em>as was said before, up to</em> … their joy is redoubled.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392c13'>「汝等當知，如轉輪王四希有事，此阿難陀亦復如是有四希有事。 何等為四？ 謂有四方大苾芻眾，來至其所情生欣慶。 復聞妙法重增歡喜。 如是，苾芻尼眾、鄔波索迦、鄔波斯迦，至阿難陀所，亦復如是倍加歡喜。</td>
    <td id='p217'>217. “All of you should know, this Ānanda also has four extraordinary feats like the noble wheel-turning king’s four extraordinary feats. What are the four? There are great assemblies of monks in the four directions who come and are happy when they see him. Their joy is redoubled when they hear the wonderful Dharma. In this way, assemblies of monks, nuns, laymen, and laywomen go to Ānanda, and they too have their joy redoubled.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392c18'>「汝等苾芻，此阿難陀復有四種希有妙事。 云何為四？ 若阿難陀與苾芻眾說法之時，善能開解無有疑滯。 諸苾芻眾咸作是念： 『善哉！善哉！ 此阿難陀宣說妙法，幸勿默然，莫辭勞倦。 然諸聽眾情無厭足。』 時，阿難陀既說法已默然而住。 或為苾芻尼、近事男、近事女說法亦復如是。」</td>
    <td id='p218'>218. “All of you monks should know, this Ānanda has another four extraordinary feats. What are the four? When Ānanda gives the assembly of monks a Dharma teaching, they understand it well without any doubts. The assembly of monks all think, ‘Good, good! This Ānanda lectures on the wonderful Dharma rather than remaining silent, and he doesn’t say that he’s tired. Indeed, the audience doesn’t feel tired of it at all.’ After Ānanda is done teaching Dharma, he then waits silently. Sometimes he teaches nuns, laymen, or laywomen who are working nearby in the same way.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392c24'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>28. The Legendary City of Kuśivatī</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392c24'>時阿難陀聞是語已，心便喜悅即白佛言： 「世尊，於此地中有六大城，所謂室羅伐城、娑雞多城、占波城、婆羅痆斯城、廣嚴城、王舍城。 何故世尊棄捨如是形勝福地，就斯荒野、磽确、邊隅、卑陋之所而般涅槃？」</td>
    <td id='p219'>219. Once he had heard this said, Ānanda felt happy and then said to the Buddha, “Bhagavān, there are six great cities in this land, which are Śrāvastī, Sāketa, Campā, Bārāṇasī, Vaiśālī, and Rājagṛha. Why has the Bhagavān discarded these places of such excellent merit to <em>parinirvāṇa</em> here in this overgrown, barren, out of the way, and inferior place?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.392c29'>佛告阿難陀： 「勿作是語：拘尸那城是邊鄙、卑陋、不可樂處。 何以故？ 阿難陀，此拘尸那城，乃往古昔，有聖王都城名拘奢伐底。 安隱豐樂人民熾盛。 縱十二踰繕那、廣七踰繕那。 城有七重垣院周匝圍繞，此等皆以四寶所成，謂金、銀、琉璃、水精。 城門亦以四寶合成，門門皆有大華表柱，亦以寶成，舉高七人。</td>
    <td id='p220'>220. The Buddha told Ānanda, “Don’t say that Kuśinagarī is an outlying, inferior, and unenjoyable place. Why? Ānanda, Kuśinagarī here was once upon a time the capital city of a noble king named Kuśivatī.<sup id="ref47"><a href="#n47">47</a></sup> It was peaceful and prosperous, and the people flourished. It was twelve <em>yojana</em>s long and seven <em>yojana</em>s wide. The city has seven walls that encircled it, which were made of four treasures: gold, silver, emerald, and crystal. The city gates were also made of the four treasures, and each gate had pillars imprinted with a large flower, which were also made of treasures and stood seven men high.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393a7'>「城外渠塹深三人半，其渠邊畔砌以寶甎。 於七院中各有多羅樹而為行列，皆四寶成。 金多羅樹以銀為枝葉華果。 銀樹金裝。 琉璃樹水精裝。 水精樹琉璃裝。 此等諸樹風吹動時，出微妙響悅可眾心。 於此樹間皆有浴池，階基砌道亦四寶成。 四邊欄楯亦四寶成，池中多有可愛之華：嗢鉢羅、鉢頭摩、俱物頭、分陀利迦。 極軟華極香，華常生華。 如是諸華人無護者隨其受用。 復於池岸有占博迦華、摩利迦華、美意華。 如是等華隨時開發。</td>
    <td id='p221'>221. “Outside the city was a ditch three and a half men deep, which was lined with precious tiles. There were seven halls, each of which were surrounded by a line of palmyra trees that were made of the four treasures. The gold palmyra trees had silver branches, leaves, flowers, and fruit. The silver trees had gold extremities. The emerald trees had crystal extremities. The crystal trees had emerald extremities. When these trees moved in the wind, they produced a marvelous sound that’s delightful to many minds. Among these trees, there were ponds that had brick walkways also made of the four treasures. They had handrails on four sides made of the four treasures, and many delightful flowers grew in the ponds, which were blue lotuses, red lotuses, white water lilies, and white lotuses. These delicate flowers had amazing fragrances and they were always in bloom. Such flowers had no one protecting them and could be taken and used. Moreover, the shores of the ponds had champak flowers, garland flowers, and jasmine flowers. Such flowers as these bloomed at the appropriate times.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393a16'>「阿難陀，於林樹間多諸美女，服妙瓔珞。 隨意遊從所須飲食皆能給與。 又此城中所有耽著五欲樂者，於此遊觀皆遂其心。 又復常有種種鼓樂，絲竹歌舞出妙音聲，皆悉勸讚修諸福業持齋戒等。</td>
    <td id='p222'>222.  “Ānanda, there were many beautiful women among the trees who wore fine jewelry. They wandered around as they liked providing food and drink as it was needed. There were also people from the city who were deeply attached to delighting in the five desires. They followed their hearts as they went sightseeing. Music was played with a variety of drum, stringed, and wind instruments to which voices sang encouragements and praises for the cultivation of meritorious deeds and the observance of the fasting precepts.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393a21'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>29. The Noble Wheel-Turning King Mahāsudarśana</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393a21'>「又阿難陀，於此城中有王名大善見，七寶具足具四希有。 所謂輪寶、象寶、馬寶、珠寶、女寶、主藏寶、主兵寶。</td>
    <td id='p223'>223. “Also, Ānanda, there was a king in this city named Mahāsudarśana<sup id="ref48"><a href="#n48">48</a></sup> who possessed seven treasures and four extraordinary things. They were the wheel treasure, horse treasure, jewel treasure, woman treasure, treasurer treasure, army general treasure.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393a23'>「四希有者： 所謂王壽命長遠。 初為王子。 次為太子。 次登王位。 後修梵行。 如是四位，皆經八萬四千歲。 是名第一希有。 復次，其王儀容端正世間無比。 是為第二希有。 又復，少病少惱所御飲食，安隱適時。 是為第三希有。 又，諸人眾忠孝事王皆生父想。 王亦愛念猶如赤子。 王出遊時乘車而去，勅馭者曰： 『汝今宜可徐徐引車令眾見我。』 王於人庶常生愍念。 是為第四希有。</td>
    <td id='p224'>224. “The four extraordinary things are as follows: The king’s life span is very long. First, he was the king’s son. Next, he was the crown prince. Next, he ascended to the throne. Finally, he cultivated the religious practice. These four positions were accomplished over the course of 84,000 years. This is the first extraordinary thing. Furthermore, the king’s manner and uprightness had no compare in the world. This was the second extraordinary thing. Moreover, he was seldom ill and seldom troubled by his travels or meals, meeting with good health. This was the third extraordinary thing. Again, the people were devoted and served the king, thinking of him as their father. The king, too, loved them as he would his newborn son. When the king went out touring in his chariot, he would tell his driver, ‘It would be good if you drive slowly so that the masses will see me.’ The king had sympathy for all the people. This was the fourth extraordinary thing.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393b3'>「復次阿難陀，時有國人持諸金銀末尼等寶，來詣王所白言： 『大王，臣有此寶。 謹奉大王，願哀納受。』</td>
    <td id='p225'>225. “Furthermore, Ānanda, there were people who brought gold, silver, and jewels to the king and said, ‘Your Majesty, the minister has these treasures that he would like to present to the king. Please take pity and accept them!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393b5'>「時王告曰： 『卿等當知，如是諸寶我自豐足。 誠無所須。』 諸人如是再三啟請，王竟不受。</td>
    <td id='p226'>226. “The king would tell them, ‘All of you should know, I already have such treasures in great bounties. I honestly have no need for these.’ The people would make their requests three times, and the king in the end wouldn’t accept them.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393b7'>「時彼念曰： 『我持此物本希奉進，王既不受。 將如之何？ 宜置王前各還本處。』 作是念已，置寶而去。</td>
    <td id='p227'>227. “Then they would think, ‘We brought these things from home as exceptional presents, but the king won’t accept them. Now what? Each of us should place them before the king and return home.’ After thinking this, they placed their treasures [in front of him] and departed.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393b9'>「王作是念： 『今此珍寶是依法得，非是枉求。 我今宜用修造法堂。』</td>
    <td id='p228'>228. “The king thought, ‘Now these treasures are obtained in accordance with the Dharma and not pursued corruptly. It would be fitting for me to use them to build a Dharma hall.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393b10'>「時有八萬四千諸城小王聞大王將建法堂。 咸詣王所白言： 『唯願聖王，不煩神慮。 臣等望欲為王營造。』</td>
    <td id='p229'>229. “Then, 84,000 cities with lesser kings who heard that the great king was going to erect a Dharma hall. They all went to the king and said, ‘Please, noble king, take care not to disturb the spirits. The ministers would like to conduct the construction for you.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393b12'>「爾時大王告諸臣曰： 『我足珍財。 無煩卿等。』 諸王如是再三啟請，王不然許。</td>
    <td id='p230'>230. “The great king then told the ministers, ‘I have sufficient wealth. Don’t trouble yourselves with this.’ The kings repeated their request three times, but the king did not accept it.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393b14'>「時諸小王來捧王足。 或執衣襟，合掌啟白： 『願天安住，臣等為造。』 王見慇勤默然而許。 諸王知已各還本處。 各持金銀等寶。</td>
    <td id='p231'>231. “Then the lesser kings came and presented gifts at the king’s feet. Some brought clothing and said with their palms together, ‘Please let the gods to remain in place and let the ministers conduct the construction.’ Seeing their zeal, the king silently agreed. Once they saw this, the kings each returned to their homes. They each carried treasures such as gold and silver.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393b17'>「又復人持一柱，皆以寶成，來詣王所。 白言： 『聖王，諸有所須悉已周備，不知何處可欲興功，其量大小。』</td>
    <td id='p232'>232. “Again, people brought a pillar to the king that was made of treasures. They said, ‘Noble king, having already provided for whatever was needed, we don’t know where we might create merit, or whether their measure be large or small?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393b19'>「王曰： 『於此城東，簡形勝地，縱廣一踰繕那，可於彼作。』 諸王聞已，即就其處，興建法堂如其量數。</td>
    <td id='p233'>233. “The king said, ‘To the east of this city, there is a particularly excellent property that’s a <em>yojana</em> wide where [the hall] could be built.’ After hearing this, the kings prepared that location and constructed a Dharma hall to its dimensions.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393b21'>「阿難陀，其堂所須椽梁枅栱，閣道鉤楯軒廊周匝。 如是諸事皆用金銀琉璃水精等寶之所成就。 其床敷座席氈褥偃枕几案箱篋衣服之流。 皆以眾寶而為莊挍。</td>
    <td id='p234'>234. “Ānanda, that hall required rafters, joists, and supports, and the pavilion’s path had hooks, railings, and covered passageways all around it. Such things were constructed with precious materials like gold, silver, emerald, and crystal. His couch was arranged as a throne with cotton and wool cloth, soft carpets, pillows, side tables, chests, and garments spread on it. These things were all decorated with many jewels.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393b25'>「阿難陀，於堂階下，一一柱間各種一樹。 樹身各列四寶枝葉華果。 互以寶嚴，如前所說。 微風吹動，出和雅音，如奏天樂。 堂內悉以金沙布地，栴檀香水常為灑潤。 金繩界道，寶網四懸。 垂諸寶鈴，盡世嚴飾。</td>
    <td id='p235'>235. “Ānanda, each of the steps below the hall had a pillar between them, which had a tree planted in them. Each trunk of the trees were lined with four treasures, as were the limbs, leaves, flowers, and fruits. They were all ornamented with treasures <em>as described before</em>. When a light breeze moved them, it produced a beautiful sound like they were playing heavenly music. Inside the hall, the floor was covered in gold sand, and sandalwood-scented water was regularly sprinkled around. A gold rope delimits a path, and jeweled netting is hung in four layers. Precious bells dangled from it, all with worldly decorations.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393c1'>「是時八萬四千諸王，同建法堂莊嚴事畢。 於此堂側多造浴池，皆方四十里，所有階砌悉以四寶而為嚴飾。 於其池中有四種華，池外復有諸陸生華。 並如前說。</td>
    <td id='p236'>236. “At that point, the 84,000 kings finished erecting the Dharma hall and its decorative features. Many bathing pools were built beside the hall in an area of forty <em>li</em> that had steps decorated with the four treasures. The four kinds of flowers grew in those pools, and flowers grew on the ground outside of the pools. <em>They were like those that have been described before.</em></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393c4'>「又於堂前處處行列四寶多羅樹。 枝葉華果皆互嚴飾，風動發聲亦如前說。 所在之地皆布金沙灑以香水。 寶鈴和響在處皆懸。</td>
    <td id='p237'>237. “There were also lines of palmyra trees made of the four treasures all around in front of the hall.
