Their branches, leaves, flowers, and fruits were mutually decorated, and they made sounds when the breeze moved them <em>as was described before</em>. The ground was covered in gold sand and sprinkled with fragrant water. Precious bells were hung in those places, which echoed harmoniously.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393c7'>「是時諸王嚴飾既畢，皆共白王： 『聖主當知，所建法堂及諸林泉，備盡嚴麗。 願親臨幸。』</td>
    <td id='p238'>238. “When the [lesser] kings were done with their decorating, they all said to the [great] king, ‘Noble lord, you should know, we have erected the Dharma hall, as well as its trees and springs, which have been completely decorated. May your kin have good fortune soon.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393c9'>「王聞生念： 『此勝法堂，我今不應先自受用。 宜請一切沙門婆羅門等有德行者，於此堂中備盡所有如法供養。』 即隨所念，設大施會。 皆供給已，復作是念： 『我今不應於此法堂放逸著樂。』</td>
    <td id='p239'>239. “Hearing this made the king think, ‘This is an excellent Dharma hall, but I shouldn’t be the first to make use of it. It would be better to invite all the brahmins and the like who are practitioners of virtue and provide this hall to them as a Dharma offering.’ He then followed that thought and arranged for a large donation event. Once the donations had been made, he again thought, ‘Now, I shouldn’t indulge in being attached to enjoying this Dharma hall.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393c13'>「遂將一人以為執侍，躬自入堂淨修梵行。 遂於金閣銀座之上，結加趺坐正念思惟。 遠離欲界諸不善法，除去尋伺證入初禪。 從金閣起次昇銀閣，坐於金座及琉璃水精，皆悉綺互而為莊飾。 其王於上皆能次第證會深禪除諸障累。</td>
    <td id='p240'>240. “Consequently, he assigned someone to act as the attendant, and the king himself entered the hall to cultivate the religious practice purely. He then sat cross-legged on a silver throne in a gold chamber and contemplated with proper mindfulness. Far away from the desire realm and unskillful things, he stopped searching and investigating<sup id="ref49"><a href="#n49">49</a></sup> to realize the first meditation. He then rose from the gold chamber and ascended to the silver chamber, where he sat on a gold throne that was also decorated with ornaments made of emerald and crystal. That king then ascended from there, successively realizing deep meditation rid of obstructing fetters.<sup id="ref50"><a href="#n50">50</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393c19'>「爾時八萬四千宮人婇女詣寶女所，白言： 『大家，我等諸人承王恩念，久闕侍衛情甚渴仰。 咸願拜謁希垂聽許。』</td>
    <td id='p241'>241. “Then, 84,000 palace staff and harem women went to the treasure woman and said to her, ‘Mistress, we are the ones who are held dear by the king, and we missed attending to him for so long that we feel a deep longing for him. Everyone wants to pay him a visit, and we hope that he will give us his attention and allow it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393c21'>「時大夫人報主兵臣曰： 『汝今應知，我等後宮久不見王，情深戀慕。 將事朝謁，宜時嚴駕。』</td>
    <td id='p242'>242. “The head wife then replied to the commander of the army, ‘Now, you should know that those of us in the inner palace have not seen the king for a long time and feel a deep longing to do so. It would be fitting to prepare horses so that we could go and have an audience with him.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393c23'>「其臣白言：『若如是者，伏請大家，勅諸侍從所有莊嚴，皆為黃色。』</td>
    <td id='p243'>243. “The minister said, ‘If that is so, I will yield to the mistress’ request and order the attendants to have ornaments that are yellow in color.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393c24'>「復更白言： 『然我今者且命八萬四千小國王等誡兵令集。』</td>
    <td id='p244'>244. “He also said, ‘Yes, I will order the 84,000 lesser kings to assemble their armies.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.393c26'>「諸王依命初令象駕，都八十千，以長淨象王而為上首。 次嚴馬駕，以騰雲馬王而為上首。 次嚴車駕，以喜鳴輅車而為上首。 如是二類亦八十千，皆寶莊嚴殊妙第一。 國大夫人乘鳴輅車，所將婇女亦復如是。 其諸營從皆乘象馬，威容嚴肅旗鼓。 曜日駭天震地同往法堂。</td>
    <td id='p245'>245. “Based on his order, the kings first commanded their elephant teams, which were 80,000 altogether led by the elephant king Upoṣatha. Next, they readied their horse teams, which were led by the horse king Vālāhā. Next, they readied their chariot teams, which were led by the chariot Nandighoṣa. In this way, these two groups of 80,000 were all adorned with treasures that were exceptional and supreme. The kingdom’s queen rode the chariot Nandighoṣa, and the harem women were likewise led by her. Their followers all rode elephants and horses that were majestic and solemn with banners and drums. The sun was brilliant, the heavenly [drums] were beaten, and the Earth shook as they went together to the Dharma hall.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394a3'>「時王問曰： 『何因緣故？車馬繁雜出大囂聲。』</td>
    <td id='p246'>246. “The king then asked, ‘What is the cause and condition for these chariots and horses to make such a great commotion?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394a4'>「謁者答曰： 『國大夫人及小王類并諸婇女，悉著黃衣。 華鬘幢蓋盡黃嚴飾。 其數繁廣不可勝言。 同來至此方申拜謁。』</td>
    <td id='p247'>247. “An official answered, ‘The queen, the lesser kings, and the harem women are all dressed in yellow. Their flower garlands, flags, banners all decorated in yellow. They are in such numbers that it overpowers words. They are coming together in this direction to extend their honors and greetings.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394a6'>「王曰： 『汝可於此堂外敷設床座。 吾將往觀。』</td>
    <td id='p248'>248. “The king said, ‘You can arrange seating outside of the hall. I will go out and meet them.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394a7'>「使者奉命。 敷金座已，而白王言： 『敷設已畢。』</td>
    <td id='p249'>249. “His messenger accepted this command. Once the golden throne was prepared, he said to the king, ‘The preparations are finished.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394a9'>「時王從臺安詳而下，次半階路，遙見黃色，儀駐嚴盛。 遂作是念： 『是等威儀甚可愛樂，嚴飾鮮異，何其盛哉！』</td>
    <td id='p250'>250. “The king then descended from his tower’s quietude and went halfway down the steps when he saw from afar their yellow colors as they halted in their full adornments. He then had this thought, ‘Their majesty is so delightful, and how full are their ornaments, so new and exceptional!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394a11'>「王既坐已，國大夫人前致敬訖，却住一面，白言： 『大王，以此八萬四千寶女嚴飾美麗敬奉大王。 願時哀納勿為棄捨。』</td>
    <td id='p251'>251. “Once the king was seated and the queen had gone before him to pay her respects, she withdrew to sit to one side and said, ‘Your Majesty, these 84,000 precious women are adorned beautifully to show their respect for His Majesty. Please let this be a time of taking pity; don’t throw it away!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394a14'>「時小國王八萬四千眾，各以兵寶而為上首。 白言： 『大王，今此象馬車乘及以八萬四千城邑，拘奢跋底城而為上首。 復有八萬四千樓閣，悉皆嚴飾甚為殊妙。 唯願大王哀憐納受而見覆護。』</td>
    <td id='p252'>252. “Then, the lesser kings and their 84,000 assemblies were each led by the army general treasure. He said, ‘Your Majesty, now these elephants, horses, and chariots from 84,000 cities are led by those from Kuśivatī. Again, there are 84,000 towers, all of which are decorated exceptionally. Please let His Majesty take pity and protect them.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394a18'>「王曰： 『姊妹當知，我先與汝極為親密，誰謂今日有若怨家。 以諸非法勸喻於我！』</td>
    <td id='p253'>253. “The king said, ‘Sisters, you should know that I gave you the closest intimacy before, but who would I call an enemy today? It would be those who encourage me with parables that are improper.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394a20'>「時夫人等聞彼大王喚為姊妹，泣而言曰： 『今觀王意似棄我等。』 以衣拭淚重白王言： 『何故大王先於我輩意甚親密，今若怨家？』</td>
    <td id='p254'>254. “When his wives heard the great king call them his sisters, they cried and said, ‘The king is thinking now of discarding us!’ Wiping their tears away with their clothes, they again spoke to the king, ‘Why does Your Majesty think of us now as his enemies when before we were his closest companions?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394a23'>「時王告曰： 『汝等應知，人命短促。 生者皆死。 我及諸人同歸滅壞。 設有婇女無量百千，如怨詐親，必能害己。 雖懷愛染，終當離別。 臣佐車馬樓觀嚴飾，如是妙物無量無邊。 一一皆有八萬四千，終歸無常，不得久住。是故，智者速宜遠離，勤修梵行，勿生染著。』</td>
    <td id='p255'>255. “The king told them, ‘All of you should know, a man’s life is short. Those who are born will all die. I and other men will return equally to our destruction. Even if I have measureless hundreds of thousands of attractive women, they are like enemies pretending to be friends who are sure to hurt me. Though they are affected by love, they will die and part from me. Such fine things as ministers, servants, chariots, horses, observation towers, and decorations are measureless and limitless. Each one of these 84,000 things will perish and return to impermanence, being impossible for them to remain long. Therefore, the wise man is quick to distance himself from them, endeavor in the religious practice, and doesn’t create attachments.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394a28'>「時夫人等聞王此語，知不採納，不稱所願。 時王如法廣勸誡已，復歸金閣於銀座上結加趺坐。 於諸有情起大慈意，遍滿十方布無限量。 普熏修已，端心而住。 從慈定起次發悲心⋯大喜⋯大捨，於諸有情亦復如是周遍十方。 其閣及座綺互眾寶。</td>
    <td id='p256'>256. “Hearing the king say this, his wives knew that he wouldn’t choose to grant their request, so they didn’t make it. The King then encouraged them in accordance with the Dharma, and then he went back up into the golden tower to sit cross-legged on the silver throne. His thought of great kindness for sentient beings, pervaded the ten directions without limit. Having cultivated this universal suffusion [of kindness], his mind was upright and abided. From that <em>samādhi</em> of kindness, next he had a thought of compassion … great joy … great equanimity for sentient beings in the same way throughout the ten directions. His tower and throne were both decorated with many treasures.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394b5'>「時王一一修習四梵住，諸欲皆斷。 壽將盡時，為死所逼情生憂悶，命終之後得生梵天。」</td>
    <td id='p257'>257. Then, the king cultivated each one of the four religious abodes and ended his desires. When his life was about to end, he was oppressed by death and feelings sadness, but he was born in the Brahma Heaven after he died.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394b8'>佛告阿難陀： 「拘尸那城至金河岸、娑羅雙樹、壯士生地、繫冠制底，於此周迴十二踰繕那，如來昔為轉輪王，於此中間六度捨命。 今復於此而般涅槃，是為第七。 又復如來、應、正等覺，於十方界更無第八捨身命處。 何以故？ 我生已盡斷諸惑業。 更不於餘受後有故。」</td>
    <td id='p258'>258. The Buddha told Ānanda, “The twelve <em>yojana</em>s all around Kuśinagarī to this pair of sal trees on the banks of the Golden River and Makuṭabandhana Shrine<sup id="ref51"><a href="#n51">51</a></sup> in the homeland of warriors was where the Tathāgata has formerly discarded his life as a wheel-turning king six times. Now, again, this will be the seventh time, and he will <em>parinirvāṇa</em> here. There will not be the place anywhere in the ten directions where the Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One will give up his life for an eighth time. Why is that? After I was born, my afflicted deeds were completely ended. There won’t be another existence to be experienced.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394b14'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>30. The Monk Upamāna</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394b14'>爾時具壽鄔波摩那在佛前立。 佛告鄔波摩那： 「汝今不應對我前住。」</td>
    <td id='p259'>259. It was then that the elder Upamāna was standing in front of the Buddha. The Buddha told Upamāna, “You should not stand right in front of me now.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394b15'>時此苾芻即離佛前。 時阿難陀白佛言： 「我侍世尊二十餘年。 未曾聞作麁訶責言如鄔波摩那苾芻。」</td>
    <td id='p260'>260. This monk then moved away from being in front of the Buddha. Ānanda then said to the Buddha, “I have been the Bhagavān’s attendant for twenty odd years. I have never before heard him speak so rudely to a monk like Upamāna.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394b17'>佛告阿難陀： 「無量百劫長壽諸天共相嫌議，作如是語： 『世間唯有如來大師，極難出世。 時乃一現如烏曇跋華。 今日中夜，定入無餘妙涅槃界，由此威德苾芻當佛前住，我等無暇親近世尊，供養恭敬。』」</td>
    <td id='p261'>261. The Buddha told Ānanda, “Gods who live as long as measureless hundreds of eons are complaining to each other, saying: ‘The world only has the Tathāgata as its great teacher, who very rarely appears in the world. The time it takes for one to appear is like that of an <em>udumbara</em> flower. He will certainly enter the realm of <em>nirvāṇa</em> without remainder in the middle of the night tonight, but we’ll have no chance to be close to the Bhagavān, to give offerings, or pay our respects to him due to this dignified monk standing in front of the Buddha.’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394b22'>阿難陀白言： 「諸來天眾其數幾何？」</td>
    <td id='p262'>262. Ānanda said, “How many are there in this host of gods who have arrived?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394b23'>佛言：「南自金河至拘尸那城雙林之處，來至繫冠制底。 於此周環十二踰繕那，皆有大威德天排肩而住。 中間無有立杖之地。」</td>
    <td id='p263'>263. The Buddha said, “South of here between these two sal trees by the Golden River to Kuśinagarī, they have come to the Makuṭabandhana Shrine. There are gods of great dignity are standing shoulder to shoulder throughout the twenty <em>yojana</em>s all around. There is not even space for a staff to stand between them.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394b25'>時諸苾芻咸生疑心，請世尊曰： 「具壽鄔波摩那，先作何業有大威德？」</td>
    <td id='p264'>264. All the monks then had this doubt, about which they asked the Bhagavān, “What deeds had the elder Upamāna done in the past that he has such great dignity?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394b27'>佛告諸苾芻： 「鄔波摩那先自作業今還自受⋯ 廣說如餘，乃至說頌⋯</td>
    <td id='p265'>265. The Buddha told the monks, “Upamāna has himself done deeds in the past that today have come back for him to experience … <em>The remainder recited in full up to speaking in verse</em> …</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394b28'>「汝等苾芻，乃往古昔，此賢劫中，人壽二萬歲時。 有佛出世名迦攝波，十號具足。 住婆羅痆斯、施鹿林中、仙人墮處。 時鄔波摩那身為出家。 時諸苾芻著衣持鉢，入城乞食，此人次當守寺。 時有黑風暴雨卒起。 既屬嚴寒，彼作是念： 『諸梵行者遭此寒苦。 衣服皆濕，將欲來至。 我今宜應嚴辦相待！』</td>
    <td id='p266'>266. “All of you monks, there was a time in the past during this fortunate eon when people lived for 20,000 years. A <em>buddha</em> appeared in the world then named Kāśyapa, and he fulfilled the ten epithets. He was staying in the Mṛgadāva Grove of Bārāṇasī at the Place Where the Sages Fell.<sup id="ref52"><a href="#n52">52</a></sup> At the time, an incarnation of Upamāna had left home. When the monks put on their robes and took their bowls into the city to solicit alms, this man would remain behind as the temple custodian. There was once a dark wind and torrential rain that suddenly arose. It became quite cold, and he had the thought, ‘Those religious practitioners would be encountering the hardship of being cold. Their clothes must be wet as they make their way back. I ought to go and get ready to meet them!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394c6'>「作此念已，入浴室中然火煖湯，敷設床席。 於其廊下，繫繩為架，詣寺門首，望諸苾芻。 彼既至已，屈入室中，取其濕衣。 淨浣濯已，安在架上。 別將淨服與苾芻著。 既解勞乏身心溫煖，寒苦皆除歡喜適悅。</td>
    <td id='p267'>267. “Having had that thought, he went into the bath house, lit the fire to make warm water, and prepared beds and sitting mats. He put up clotheslines under the verandas, and then he went to the front gate of the temple to watch for the monks. When they arrived, he went into their rooms to get their wet robes. Once he had washed them with clean water, he put them on the clotheslines. He also gave the monks clean robes to wear. Once they were free from their deprivation and warmed up in body and mind, they were happy and glad to be rid of that hardship of being cold.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394c10'>「其守寺苾芻長跪合掌，向大眾前而發願言： 『我今為諸同梵行者，除苦得樂所生善根。 如迦攝波如來、應、正等覺授摩納婆記，於當來世人壽百歲時，成等正覺號釋迦牟尼。 願我於彼佛法之中而得出家，斷諸煩惱，證阿羅漢果。 然火功德，當願身光。 天莫能近。』</td>
    <td id='p268'>268. “That monk who was the temple custodian knelt in front of the large assembly with his palms together and expressed this aspiration: ‘Now, I have produced roots of goodness by ridding my fellow religious practitioners of their hardships and making them happy. The Tathāgata Kāśyapa, the Arhat, and Completely Awakened One has given his prediction to his students that there will come a time when people live for a hundred years, and the completely awakened one will be called Śākyamuni. I aspire to leave home in that <em>buddha</em>’s Dharma, end the mental afflictions, and realize the fruit of the <em>arhat</em>. I will light the fires of virtue, and I will aspire to have a halo around me. May I not be close to the gods.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394c16'>「汝等當知，由彼願力，於我法中而得出家。 斷諸煩惱，證阿羅漢果，有大威德。 為此諸天莫能逼近。」</td>
    <td id='p269'>269. “All of you should know that because of the power of his aspiration, Upamāna was able to leave home in this Dharma of mine. He has the great dignity of having ended the mental afflictions and realized the fruit of the <em>arhat</em>. This is why these gods will not press forward to get closer to him.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394c19'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>31. How to Inter a Buddha</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394c19'>時具壽阿難陀而白佛言： 「大德世尊般涅槃後，我當云何恭敬供養如來法身？」</td>
    <td id='p270'>270. The elder Ānanda then asked the Buddha, “Venerable, how shall we pay our respects and make offerings to the Tathāgata’s Dharma body after the Bhagavān’s <em>parinirvāṇa</em>?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394c20'>佛告阿難陀： 「汝宜且止！ 汝所問事，當有信心婆羅門長者等自為施設。」</td>
    <td id='p271'>271. The Buddha told Ānanda, “You ought to stop. Regarding this topic you’ve asked about, there will be believing brahmins and prominent men who will arrange for that themselves.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394c22'>復白佛言： 「諸長者等所有施設其事云何？」</td>
    <td id='p272'>272. Ānanda again asked the Buddha, “What will be arranged by those prominent men?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394c23'>佛言： 「一一皆如轉輪王葬法。」</td>
    <td id='p273'>273. The Buddha said, “They each will follow the way to inter a wheel-turning king.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394c24'>又問： 「轉輪王法其事云何？」</td>
    <td id='p274'>274. Ānanda also asked, “What is the way of doing this for a wheel-turning king?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.394c24'>佛言： 「汝今應知，轉輪聖王命終之後，以五百斤上妙疊絮以用纏身上下，各有五百妙衣以為裝飾。 於鐵棺中滿盛香油，舁王置內。 然後蓋棺。 以諸香木焚燒其棺。 次灑香乳以滅炎火，方收王骨安置金瓶。 於四衢道興建大塔，幡幢傘蓋諸妙香華，恭敬供養尊重讚歎設大齋會。 阿難陀，如恭敬供養轉輪聖王，於我滅後，人天供養當倍過此。」</td>
    <td id='p275'>275. The Buddha said, “Now, you should know that after a noble wheel-turning king’s life has ended, his body is wrapped top to bottom with five hundred sheets of fine cotton cloth, and each of those five hundred fine cloths are embroidered. An iron casket is filled with fragrant oil, and the king is carried on shoulders, placed inside. Afterward, the coffin is covered. Fragrant wood is then used to set fire to the coffin. Next, the flames are doused with fragrant milk, and the king’s bones are gathered up and placed in a gold pot. A large shrine is constructed at a crossroad with banners, canopies, and wonderful and fragrant flowers, and people pay their respects, give offerings, honors, praises to him, and arrange large feasts. Ānanda, after I have perished, humans and gods will give twice as many offerings as those respects and offerings given to a noble wheel-turning king.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395a4'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>32. The Mallas of Kuśinagarī</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395a4'>爾時，世尊告阿難陀： 「汝今宜往拘尸那城，宣我言告五百壯士： 『諸人當知，如來大師，必定今日於中夜時入無餘依妙涅槃界。 所應作者宜可速為。 勿招後悔云： 「此境內大師涅槃，我等不知，不為供養。」』」</td>
    <td id='p276'>276. It was then that the Bhagavān told Ānanda, “Now, you ought to go to Kuśinagarī and announce that I have this to say to the five hundred Malla men: ‘Everyone should know, the Tathāgata, the great teacher, will certainly enter the realm of <em>nirvāṇa</em> with no remaining basis in the middle of the night tonight. It would be fitting to act quickly. Don’t regret it later, saying, “The great teacher’s <em>nirvāṇa</em> was in this region, but we didn’t know it and didn’t make offerings to him!”’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395a8'>時具壽阿難陀聞佛教已，持僧伽胝，將一侍者即便往至拘尸那城，眾集堂所。 五百壯士皆至於此共論餘事。 時阿難陀傳世尊命告諸壯士曰： 「汝等既集咸應善聽： 如來大師今日中夜必入無餘大涅槃界。 所應作者皆可作之。 勿招後悔作如是語： 『如來大師於我境內入般涅槃，我等不能少興供養。』」</td>
    <td id='p277'>277. Having heard the Buddha’s instructions, the elder Ānanda took his outer robe and led an attendant to Kuśinagarī, where a crowd had gathered at a hall. Five hundred Malla men had come together to discuss some other matter. Then, Ānanda told those Mallas what the Bhagavān had ordered: “All of you who have gathered here should listen to this well: The Tathāgata, the great teacher, will surely enter the great realm of <em>nirvāṇa</em> without remainder in the middle of the night tonight. It would be fitting to act quickly. Don’t regret it later, saying, ‘The Tathāgata, the great teacher, entered <em>parinirvāṇa</em> in this region, but we couldn’t make even a small offering to him!’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395a15'>時諸壯士既聞是語，各與妻子、眷屬、朋友、僕使之類，共相招引詣娑羅林。 頂禮佛足，退坐一面。</td>
    <td id='p278'>278. Having heard this said, those Mallas each headed out for the pair of sal trees with their families, households, friends, and employees. They bowed their heads at the Buddha’s feet and withdraw to sit to one side.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395a17'>爾時，世尊為說妙法，示教利喜。 時諸壯士從座而起，整衣服偏袒右肩，合掌瞻仰。 而白佛言： 「大德世尊，我某甲等並是拘尸那城尊貴壯士，願盡形壽歸依佛陀、歸依達摩、歸依僧伽。 并受學處。」</td>
    <td id='p279'>279. The Bhagavān then taught them the wonderful Dharma plainly, benefiting and gladdening them. The Mallas then rose from their seats, adjusted their robes to expose their right shoulders, put their palms together, and looked up to him. They said to the Buddha, “Venerable Bhagavān, there are some of us noble Mallas in Kuśinagarī who have vowed to take refuge in the Buddha, the Dharma, and the Saṅgha for their entire lives. Together, they accept the points of training.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395a21'>時阿難陀作如是念： 「彼諸壯士於世尊處，一一別受近事學者。時既淹久，妨廢圓寂。 我今宜請與彼一時，受其學處。」</td>
    <td id='p280'>280. Ānanda then thought, “Each one of these Mallas who dwell in the Bhagavān have given personal service in the Bhagavān’s abode. They have stagnated for a long time and prevented from reaching perfect peace. Now, I ought to invite them to accept the points of training all at once.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395a24'>作是念已，從坐而起，整衣合掌，而白佛言： 「大德世尊，諸壯士等并諸眷屬品類眾多，各有如是別別名號。 欲歸三寶，求五學處。 若各別受時恐淹遲。 唯願大悲一時為受。」</td>
    <td id='p281'>281. Having had that thought, he rose from his seat, adjusted his robe, put his palms together, and said to the Buddha, “Venerable Bhagavān, these Mallas together with their retinues of different sorts of many people have each their individual names. They want to take refuge in the three treasures and seek the five points of training. If they each were to accept them, then they would be anxious and halting. Please have great compassion and confer [the precepts to them] at the same time!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395a28'>時阿難陀對世尊前，一時牒名為受歸戒。 時諸壯士聞佛說法，復受學處。 生大歡喜，頂禮佛足，奉辭而去。</td>
    <td id='p282'>282. Ānanda then stood opposite the Bhagavān and conferred the precepts to all of them by name at the same time. The Mallas then accepted the points of training upon hearing the Buddha teach the Dharma. Feeling great joy, they bowed their heads at the Buddha’s feet, paid their respects, and departed.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395b2'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>33. The Story of Supriya</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395b2'>爾時，世尊為菩薩時在覩史多天。 以五種事觀察世間，六欲天子三淨母腹。現白象相來入母胎。</td>
    <td id='p283'>283. At that point,<sup id="ref53"><a href="#n53">53</a></sup> when the Bhagavān was a <em>bodhisattva</em> watching from the Tuṣita Heaven, he examined the world using five criteria as a young god of the six desire heavens and the three purities of his mother’s belly. He then manifested the form of a white elephant and entered his mother’s womb.<sup id="ref54"><a href="#n54">54</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395b4'>時天帝釋告善愛健闥婆王： 「汝今當知菩薩在覩史多宮。 以其五事觀察世間，六欲天子三淨母腹。 現白象相降神母胎。 我等宜往共為衛護。」</td>
    <td id='p284'>284. Śakra the Lord of Gods then addressed the <em>gandharva</em> king Supriya, “Now, you should know that a <em>bodhisattva</em> was watching from his Tuṣita palace. He examined the world using five criteria as a young god of the six desire heavens and the three purities of his mother’s belly. He then manifested the form of a white elephant and entered his mother’s womb. We ought to go and provide him protection.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395b7'>時健闥婆王白言： 「大天可去，我且於此奏諸音樂。」</td>
    <td id='p285'>285. The <em>gandharva</em> king said, “Your Majesty can go, but I will stay here and play music.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395b8'>是時菩薩出母胎時，其天帝釋復告善愛音樂王曰： 「汝今當知，菩薩從母胎出。 我等宜往而為侍從。」 答乃如前。</td>
    <td id='p286'>286. When the <em>bodhisattva</em> emerged from his mother’s womb, Śakra the Lord of Gods again told the <em>gandharva</em> king Supriya, “Now, you should know that the <em>bodhisattva</em> has emerged from his mother’s womb. We ought to go and attend to him.” He answered as before.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395b11'>與諸童子共遊戲時，其天帝釋復告音樂王曰： 「汝今當知菩薩共諸童子遊戲。 可往侍從。」 答乃如前。</td>
    <td id='p287'>287. When the youths were playing together, Śakra the Lord of Gods again told the <em>gandharva</em> king, “Now, you should know that the <em>bodhisattva</em> is playing with the other youths. Perhaps we could go and attend to him.” He answered as before.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395b13'>菩薩觀知老病死已，情生憂惱。 依託林野修諸苦行。 後食二牧牛女十六轉乳糜，氣力宣通食諸飲食。 沐浴形體塗拭蘇油。 爾時帝釋復命樂神，令其侍衛。 答亦如前。</td>
    <td id='p288'>288. After the <em>bodhisattva</em> witnessed old age, illness, and death, he felt sad and troubled. He went into the wilderness to cultivate the ascetic practice. After eating sixteen turns of milk gruel from a cowherd’s daughter, he regained his strength and ate his meals. He bathed his body and rubbed it with butter oil. Then, Śakra the Lord of Gods again ordered the <em>gandharva</em> to go and guard him. He answered as he did before.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395b16'>世尊降彼三十六億天魔軍眾，成無上智。 梵王來請詣婆羅痆斯，三轉十二行法輪，制諸學處。 凡是有緣所應度者，皆已度訖，詣拘尸那城，最後而臥。 時天帝釋復命樂神廣如前說⋯乃至⋯ 「可往聽法。」 答言：「我且奏諸音樂。」</td>
    <td id='p289'>289. The Bhagavān defeated that army of the god Māra that numbered thirty-six million and achieved the unsurpassed knowledge. King Brahmā came to him at Bārāṇasī and requested that he turn the Dharma three times in twelve steps and establish the points of training. Once the conditions were met for everyone to be liberated who could be liberated, he went to Kuśinagarī and lay down for the last time. Śakra the Lord of Gods then ordered that <em>gandharva</em>, and he answered as he did before … <em>up to</em> … “Perhaps we could go and listen to the Dharma.” He replied, “I will stay here and play music.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395b21'>時天帝釋復告樂神曰： 「汝今當知，大覺世尊最後而臥，必般涅槃。 可興供養。」 答亦同前。</td>
    <td id='p290'>290. Śakra the Lord of Gods again told the <em>gandharva</em>, “Now, you should know that the greatly awakened Bhagavān has laid down for the last time and will surely <em>parinirvāṇa</em>. We can go make offerings to him.” He also answered the same as before.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395b23'>爾時世尊作如是念： 「善賢外道能至我所，而受調伏。 樂神善愛無自來法。」 又復念曰： 「凡是聲聞度者，如來亦度。 應佛度者，餘不能度。 由待勝上善巧方便，我今應可度彼善愛。」</td>
    <td id='p291'>291. At this point, the Bhagavān had this thought, “Virtuous people from other religions can come to me and accept the discipline. This <em>gandharva</em> Supriya never comes himself to the Dharma.” He also thought, “All of the disciples being liberated, the Tathāgata will also be liberated. Should the Buddha be liberated, then he can’t liberate anyone else. Given how superior and talented he is, I should liberate that Supriya now.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395b27'>作是念已，即便入定，由定力故最後臥處化作一身。 又復化作千絃瑠璃箜篌。 於臥處沒自持箜篌，詣三十三天。 至善愛健闥婆王宮門而住。 其時善愛自恃憍慢。 於彈箜篌謂無過者，於自宮中作樂歡戲，情生愛著。</td>
    <td id='p292'>292. Having had that thought, he entered <em>samādhi</em> and conjured a body there where he had laid down for the last time using the power of that <em>samādhi</em>. Moreover, he conjured a thousand-stringed emerald lute. He then disappeared from the place where he lay and went to the Trāyastriṃśa Heaven carrying this lute. He arrived standing at the gate of the <em>gandharva</em> king Supriya’s palace. Supriya was at the time independent and proud. He plucked his lute, which was known as Flawless, being in love with the enjoyment of playing music in his own palace.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395c3'>爾時，世尊告守門者： 「汝可往報善愛王言： 『有健闥婆來至門首，欲求相見。』」</td>
    <td id='p293'>293. It was then that the Bhagavān addressed the guard at the gate, “You can go and tell King Supriya, ‘There is a <em>gandharva</em> at the gate who seeks to meet with you.’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395c5'>時守門者即入具報。 其王高慢報曰： 「除我更有健闥婆耶？」</td>
    <td id='p294'>294. The guard then went inside and told him that. His king arrogantly replied, “There is another <em>gandharva</em> aside from me?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395c6'>答曰： 「更有今在門外。」</td>
    <td>The guard answered, “There is another one outside the gate right now.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395c6'>善愛聞已情懷不忍，即自出門告言： 「丈夫，汝是健闥婆耶？」</td>
    <td id='p295'>295. Having heard this, Supriya felt impatient and went out to the gate himself and said, “Man, are you a <em>gandharva</em>?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395c8'>佛言： 「我今實是健闥婆王。」</td>
    <td>The Buddha said, “I really am a <em>gandharva</em> king now.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395c8'>「若爾，可來對奏音樂。」</td>
    <td id='p296'>296. “If that’s so, you can come and play music with me.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395c9'>報言： 「大仙，甚善！ 我能共作。」</td>
    <td>The Buddha replied, “Great sage, that would be very good! I can do that with you.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395c10'>佛即對彼共彈箜篌。 佛斷一絃，彼亦斷一。 然二音聲並無闕處。 佛又斷二，彼亦斷二。 然其音韻一種相似。 佛又斷三斷四，彼亦如是⋯乃至⋯ 各留一絃然音聲不異。 佛便總斷，彼亦斷之。 佛於空中張手彈擊。 然其雅韻倍勝於常，彼便不能。 情生希有降伏傲慢，知： 「彼音樂超勝於我。」 世尊觀已，即便隱彼健闥婆身復本形相。</td>
    <td id='p297'>297. The Buddha then plucked his harp opposite the king. The Buddha cut one sting, and Supriya cut one, too. Still, the both their sounds would be flawless. The Buddha also cut two strings, and Supriya cut two as well. Still, their sounds were like a single sound. The Buddha also cut three strings or four, and Supriya did the same … <em>up to</em> … Each of the strings still sounded no different. The Buddha then cut them all, and Supriya cut them, too. The Buddha then extended his hand in the air and plucked at it. Still, the elegance of the sound was always twice as great, and Supriya couldn’t do the same. He felt extraordinarily defeated and impudent, knowing, “His music is much better than mine!” Once he saw this, the Bhagavān transformed his <em>gandharva</em> body back to his original form.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395c17'>時彼樂神見佛世尊，身真金色，三十二相、八十種好周匝莊嚴。 赫奕光明超逾千日。 如寶山王觀者忘倦。 見已，欣悅深生敬仰。 禮佛足下坐聽法要。</td>
    <td id='p298'>298. That <em>gandharva</em> then saw the golden color of the Buddha’s, the Bhagavān’s, body adorned all over with the thirty-two signs and eighty excellencies adorning. His blazing glow was brighter than a thousand suns. It was like someone gazing at the king of the jewel mountains and forgetting their fatigue. Seeing it was delightful and he became deeply respectful. He bowed at the Buddha’s feet and sat down to listen to the essentials of the Dharma.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395c20'>爾時世尊觀彼根性，隨機為說四聖諦法，令得開悟。 彼即能以智金剛杵摧二十種身見邪山。 證預流果。</td>
    <td id='p299'>299. The Bhagavān then examined the nature of his faculties and taught him the four noble truths according to his capacity in order to awaken the king. He shattered the mountain of twenty kinds of wrong [views] about personality view with the <em>vajra</em> hammer of knowledge. Supriya then realized the fruit of stream entry.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395c22'>既見諦已，深自慶幸而白佛言： 「大德世尊，我今所得非父、非母、非王、非天、非我眷屬及諸知識、非餘沙門婆羅門等，能為成辦如是勝事。 唯獨世尊慈念哀愍，令我今者枯竭血海，超越骨山。 閉惡趣門，開涅槃路，置人天道。 我今歸依佛、法、僧寶為鄔波索迦。 始從今日乃至盡形，不殺生⋯乃至⋯不飲酒。 受三歸依并五學處。」</td>
    <td id='p300'>300. Once he saw the truths, Supriya was deeply thankful and said to the Buddha, “Venerable Bhagavān, now I find no father, mother, king, god, none of my retinue and friends, nor any other ascetic or brahmin who can achieve such an excellent feat. Only the Bhagavān with his compassionate and sympathetic thought could cause me to dry up the ocean of blood and leap beyond the mountain of bones today. The door to the bad destinies is shut and the road to <em>nirvāṇa</em> is opened, and I’ve been put on the path of humans and gods. Now, I take refuge in the Buddha, Dharma, and Saṅgha treasures as a layman. Starting today until the end of my life, I will not kill beings … <em>up to</em> … will not drink alcohol. I accept these three refuges and five points of training.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.395c29'>爾時，世尊復為說法示教利喜已，即便入定。 天宮處沒，還至雙林，最後臥處。</td>
    <td id='p301'>301. After he had taught Supriya the Dharma plainly, benefited, and gladdened him, the Bhagavān entered <em>samādhi</em>. He then disappeared from that heavenly palace and returned to the place between the two trees where he would lay for the last time.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396a3'>根本說一切有部毘奈耶 雜事 卷第三十七</td>
    <td class="fasc_heading"></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396a6'>根本說一切有部毘奈耶 雜事 卷第三十八</td>
    <td class="fasc_heading"></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396a9'>三藏法師義淨奉　制譯</td>
    <td class="taisho_byline"></td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396a10'>第八門第十子攝頌說涅槃之餘</td>
  <td class='subheading'></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396a11'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>34. The Story of Subhadra</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396a11'>爾時，拘尸那城有出家外道，名曰善賢(梵云蘇跋陀羅)。 年百二十，形容衰朽。 俱尸那城所有壯士，於善賢處，悉生恭敬。 尊重供養如阿羅漢。</td>
    <td id='p302'>302. It was then that there was a man who had left home in another religion named Subhadra. At a hundred and twenty years old, he was quite elderly and frail. He was among the Mallas of Kuśinagarī, all of whom were respectful in the presence of Subhadra. They gave honors and offerings to him like they would to an <em>arhat</em>.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396a13'>去斯不遠，有大華池名曰曼陀枳儞。 於池岸上，有烏曇跋樹，善賢梵志常遊於此。 往昔菩薩在覩史天，作白象狀入母胎時。 彼烏曇樹華始新出。 降誕之始漸有光色，為童子時其華欲發。 厭老病死，遠託山林，其華稍大狀如鵶嘴。 修苦行時，現萎萃相。 捨苦行已氣息疎通噉諸飲食⋯廣如前說，乃至⋯ 成等正覺其華開敷。 梵王來請於婆羅痆斯轉法輪時，其樹及華光色榮盛，妙香芬馥遍諸方界。 然佛大悲普於有緣所在世界。 廣濟度已，詣拘尸那為最後臥，而此華樹形色枯萃。 見者驚歎。 是時善賢觀斯變異而作是念： 「拘尸那城必有凶禍。」</td>
    <td id='p303'>303. Not far away, there was a large lotus pond called Mandākinī. An <em>udumbara</em> tree grew on the shore of that pond where the ascetic Subhadra often went for walks. In the past when the <em>bodhisattva</em> was in the Tuṣita Heaven, he became a white elephant and entered his mother’s womb. That <em>udumbara</em> tree’s flowers had just begun to emerge then. It gradually began to shine when the <em>bodhisattva</em> descended, and its flowers were about to open when he was a boy. When he was disillusioned with old age, illness, and death and went out into the wilderness, its flowers had grown as large as a crows beak. While he was cultivating ascetic practices, it looked withered. After he had cultivated the ascetic practices, he breathed a sigh and ate a meal … <em>as described before up to</em> … the flower bloomed when he achieved the complete awakening. When King Brahmā came to request that he turn the Dharma wheel at Bārāṇasī, that tree and its flowers were brightly colored and flourishing, and the wonderful scent permeated throughout the regions of the realm. Still, the Buddha had great compassion in every circumstance where he was in the world. After he had made the wide ford, he went to Kuśinagarī where he laid down for the last time, and then the form of this flower and tree was dried up and withered. People who saw gasped in shock. When Subhadra saw this extraordinary thing, he thought, “Surely something terrible has happened in Kuśinagarī.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396a26'>爾時護國天神發大音聲告諸人曰：「今日如來於中夜時，必入無餘妙涅槃界。」</td>
    <td id='p304'>304. The heavenly spirits that guarded the country then loudly announced to everyone, “The Tathāgata will surely enter the realm of <em>nirvāṇa</em> without remainder in the middle of the night tonight!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396a27'>善賢梵志聞其說已，作如是念： 「哀哉！苦哉！ 彼大沙門喬答摩氏必於今夜當般涅槃！ 然我每於自所得法有懷疑惑。 常自思惟: 『我於何時因何方便得見彼人，諮啟未悟？』 惜哉！法眼不久將滅。 今宜速往，親自啟問。 若蒙大悲，垂哀為決，於諸猶豫，永得開解。」 作是念已，出拘尸那城，詣雙林所。</td>
    <td id='p305'>305. After he heard them say this, the ascetic Subhadra then thought, “What a pity! What suffering! That great ascetic of the Gautama clan surely will <em>parinirvāṇa</em> tonight! But I often feel doubtful about the Dharma that I have obtained by myself. I’m always thinking to myself, ‘When will be the day and by what method will I come to meet that man and inquire about what I’ve yet to realize?’ So valuable is the Dharma eye that will be put on before long! Now, I ought to go quickly and question him myself. Perhaps he will have great compassion, take pity on me, and settle my doubts so that I will understand them forever.” After thinking this, he left Kuśinagarī and went to that pair of trees in the forest.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396b5'>于時阿難陀見佛日將沒，在寺門外，身心憂慼，露地經行。 善賢見已，近而告曰： 「汝阿難陀，我聞沙門喬答摩具一切智，於諸眾生平等濟拔。 然我每於自所得法有懷猶豫，比常希願聽受未聞，竟不果遂。 今聞天聲遍告我等，如來今夜定入涅槃。 大德，頗能為我諮啟，容我面奉，申述疑情。」</td>
    <td id='p306'>306. Thereupon, Ānanda saw the Buddha sun setting as he walked over open ground outside of the temple gate, and he felt depressed both in mind and body. Once he saw Ānanda, Subhadra came close and told him, “You, Ānanda! I heard that the ascetic Gautama possessed all knowledge and that he saves sentient beings equally. I often feel unsure about the Dharma that I have obtained by myself, and I’ve always had the hope to listen to what I have not heard yet, but it has not born fruit. I heard a heavenly voice announce to us that the Tathāgata will certainly enter <em>nirvāṇa</em> tonight. Venerable, might I ask of you to permit me to see him and settle my feelings of doubt.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396b12'>阿難陀言： 「善賢，汝今不應作如是語，故惱世尊。 然我大師今見背痛未能安隱。」 善賢如是再三諮啟，竟不為白。</td>
    <td id='p307'>307. Ānanda said, “Subhadra, you shouldn’t say such things now, for it will trouble the Bhagavān. My great teacher is suffering from back pain and isn’t comfortable yet.” Subhadra repeated his request three times, but Ānanda would not speak to him.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396b14'>又告曰： 「阿難陀，我昔曾聞古仙梵志耆年有德軌範人說。 諸佛出世如烏曇華，億百萬劫時乃一現。 如來今日定入涅槃，我懷迷惑願見諮問。 唯希大德為我諮白。 我得見佛誠為幸甚。」</td>
    <td id='p308'>308. Again, he said, “Ānanda, I’ve heard in the past that the ancient sages, ascetics, and seniors taught worthy people. The <em>buddha</em>s appear in the world like <em>udumbara</em> flowers, which appear once in a million hundreds of thousands of eons. The Tathāgata will certainly enter <em>nirvāṇa</em> today, and I hope to meet him and inquire about my confusion. I only ask this of the venerable for this reason. I would appreciate it if I might see the Buddha.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396b18'>阿難陀告言： 「善賢，今我大師身有乖違。 甚不安隱，勿故相惱。」 善賢再三如前苦請，尊者不允其志。</td>
    <td id='p309'>309. Ānanda told him, “Subhadra, my great teacher has a physical imbalance. He is really very uncomfortable, so don’t trouble him further.” Subhadra repeated himself three times as before with his pained request, and the venerable one did not satisfy his aim.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396b20'>阿難陀與善賢於寺門外共言論時，佛以清淨耳超越人天一一聞說。 告阿難陀曰： 「汝今不應遮彼善賢任來見我。 隨其請問。 何以故？ 此善賢者即是我於最後為外道說法令生正信。 親命善來為我弟子。」</td>
    <td id='p310'>310. While Ānanda and Subhadra were having this conversation outside the temple gate, the Buddha heard each of their statements with his clear hearing that goes beyond that of humans or gods. He told Ānanda, “Now, you should not stop that Subhadra from coming to see me. Go along with his request. Why is that? This Subhadra will be the very last person from another religion who will become properly faithful after being taught the Dharma. I order you to welcome him as my disciple.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396b25'>于時，善賢聞佛世尊慈悲容許，心生歡喜不勝抃躍。 詣世尊所，共申種種往復言談。 却住一面，白言： 「喬答摩，我欲諮問。 願垂聽許，為我解說。」</td>
    <td id='p311'>311. When Subhadra heard the Buddha, the Bhagavān, mercifully give his permission, he felt joyous and couldn’t overcome the desire to celebrate. He went to the Bhagavān, and they had a conversation about a variety of things. He then withdrew to stand to one side and said, “Gautama, I would like to ask a question. Please permit me to do so and explain it for me.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396b28'>佛告梵志： 「隨汝所問。」</td>
    <td id='p312'>312. The Buddha told the ascetic, “Go ahead and ask your question.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396b28'>彼即問曰： 「喬答摩，我曾遍觀諸外道類。 各別立宗所謂： 晡剌拏迦攝波子、末塞羯利瞿梨子、珊逝移毘剌知子、阿市多雞舍甘跋羅子、脚俱陀迦多演那子、昵揭爛陀慎若低子。 此等諸師各述異宗。 未知誰是？」</td>
    <td id='p313'>313. He then asked, “Gautama, I have observed those of the various other religions everywhere. They each establish their own tradition, which are called the disciples of Pūraṇa Kāśyapa, disciples of Maskarī Gośālin, disciples of Saṃjayin Vairuṭīputra, disciples of Ajita Keśakambala, disciples of Kakuda Kātyāyana, and disciples of Nirgrantha Jñātiputra. The teachers of these groups each explained different traditions. Which of them had yet to understand?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396c4'>爾時世尊即命善賢，為說伽他曰：</td>
    <td id='p314'>314. The Bhagavān then instructed Subhadra by speaking in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.396c6'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「我年二十九，<br/>
    出家求善法。<br/>
    又五十餘年，<br/>
    專行戒定慧。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“At the age of twenty-nine years old,<br/>
    I left home to pursue the good Dharma.<br/>
    For over fifty years since then,<br/>
    I’ve only practiced precepts, <em>samādhi</em>, and wisdom.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.396c8'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>一心無散亂，<br/>
    唯求於正理。<br/>
    除斯真法外，<br/>
    無別有沙門。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>With mind unified without confusion,<br/>
    I sought only the correct principles.<br/>
    Aside from what’s outside this true Dharma,<br/>
    There’re no distinctions between ascetics.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396c10'>爾時世尊說此頌已，復告善賢曰： 「此是諸佛善說八聖道支。 甚為希有難可值遇。 除此已外欲求一、二、三、四沙門道果終無可得。 是故能，於善說法律八聖道支，求沙門果，必定當得。</td>
    <td id='p315'>315. After he had spoken these verses, the Bhagavān also told Subhadra, “This is the noble eightfold path that’s well taught by <em>buddha</em>s. It’s extraordinarily rare to encounter. Aside from it, outsiders who pursue the first, second, third, or fourth fruit of the ascetic will never obtain it. Therefore, someone who can pursue the fruits of the ascetic through this noble eightfold path in the well-taught Dharma and Vinaya will surely obtain those fruits.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396c14'>「復次，善賢，離八聖法，諸有外道婆羅門等各執己見。 或說三世無因無果，所修福善皆空無益。 是故我於沙門婆羅門眾中，大師子吼而作是言： 『凡有修行皆獲果報。』」</td>
    <td id='p316'>316. “Furthermore, Subhadra, apart from the eight noble principles, other religions and brahmins each adhere to their own views. They might claim that the three times have no cause and no effect, or that the cultivation of merits and virtues is empty and profitless. I therefore roar the great lion’s roar in the assemblies of ascetics and brahmins by saying this: ‘Whatever is cultivated, that will obtain its reward.’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396c17'>說此法時，善賢梵志遠塵離垢，得法眼淨。 於諸諦實得不壞信，超越愛河，斷諸疑網。 自然通達諸微妙法，即從座起，整衣合掌，向阿難陀。 作如是語： 「大師尊重事難諮請。 我觀大德獲大善利。 幸得值遇無上法王，於諸師中灌頂最上。 由師力故我亦善證。 我今重希於善說法律而為出家求受近圓，成苾芻性，修沙門行。」</td>
    <td id='p317'>317. When he was taught this Dharma, the ascetic Subhadra became far removed from dust and free of dirt, and his Dharma vision was clarified. He gained an unbroken faith in the truths, leaped over the river of craving, and cut away the net of doubts. He naturally penetrated the subtle teachings, and then he rose from his seat, adjusted his robe, and saluted Ānanda with his palms together. He said this: “It’s difficult to make a request of a great teacher who is honored. I observe that this venerable has won a great and virtuous reward. I was fortunate to encounter the unsurpassed Dharma king who has been anointed as the highest of teachers. This good realization of mine was the result of the powers of this teacher. It would be twice as rare for me to leave home and pursue to near completion the well-taught Dharma and Vinaya and achieve the disposition of a monk who cultivates the ascetic practice.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396c25'>時具壽阿難陀白佛言： 「世尊，今此善賢聞法悟解。 心樂出家⋯廣如前說，乃至⋯ 成苾芻性。 唯願世尊哀愍拔濟。」</td>
    <td id='p318'>318. The elder Ānanda then said to the Buddha, “Bhagavān, Subhadra here has heard the Dharma and realized it. He is happy to leave home … <em>as was recited before up to</em> … achieve a monk’s disposition. Please, Bhagavān, have pity and save him.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396c27'>爾時世尊即告善賢： 「善來苾芻！ 可修梵行。」</td>
    <td>The Bhagavān then told Subhadra, “Welcome, monk! You may cultivate the religious practice.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.396c28'>於佛言下如常威儀，出家近圓成苾芻性。 一心勤勇，不為放逸。 作如是念： 「善男子，何故剃除鬢髮而披法服，正信出家，於無上道而修梵行？ 於現法中得自證悟： 『我生已盡、梵行已立、所作已辦。 不受後有。』」</td>
    <td id='p319'>319. Upon the Buddha saying this, he lowered his usual dignified manner, left home to near completion, and achieved the disposition of a monk. With a unified mind, he was vigorous and not careless. He thought, “Good son, why do you cut off your hair and beard, take the Dharma robes, and leave home with proper faith to cultivate the religious practice on the unsurpassed path? It’s to realize for yourself here in the present: ‘My births have been ended, the religious practice has been established, and the task has been accomplished. I won’t be subject to a later existence.’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397a4'>爾時，善賢起徹到心，即便速證阿羅漢果。 得心解脫，復作是念： 「我今不忍見佛般涅槃。 宜可先去。」 作是念已，詣世尊所，頂禮雙足，退坐一面。 白佛言： 「大德世尊，我願先入涅槃。」</td>
    <td id='p320'>320. Subhadra then produced a thoroughgoing mind and quickly realized the fruit of the <em>arhat</em>. His mind being freed, he again thought, “Now, I cannot stand to see the Buddha <em>parinirvāṇa</em>. I ought to depart before he does.” Having had that thought, he went to the Bhagavān, bowed his head at the Buddha’s two feet, and withdrew to sit to one side. He said to the Buddha, “Venerable Bhagavān, I would like to enter <em>nirvāṇa</em> first.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397a8'>佛告善賢： 「汝於今者入涅槃耶？」 答言：「如是。」 再三顧問，佛言： 「一切諸行皆悉無常。 汝於所作自可知時。 我更何言？」</td>
    <td id='p321'>321. The Buddha told Subhadra, “You are going to enter <em>nirvāṇa</em> now?” He answered, “Yes.” The Buddha asked this three times, and then he said, “All actions are entirely impermanent. You yourself know what you will do. What more can I say about it?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397a11'>善賢將欲入滅而作是念： 「我今應為五種加持方可滅度： 諸來觀者皆見我身，剃除鬢髮，著僧伽胝。 莫令彼見外道儀式。 又諸外道來舁我時，勿令身舉。 同梵行者方能舁去。 又入浴池洗我身時，令諸外道不得其底。 同梵行者能洗我身。 又諸外道入水之時，當令魚鼈擾亂不安，同梵行者即無惱害。 又諸外道不能燒我遺身。 同梵行者方令火著。」 作此五種加持念已，便入涅槃。</td>
    <td id='p322'>322. When Subhadra was about to enter extinguishment, he had this thought, “Now, there are five assisting things that make extinguishment possible: Those who come and look will see my body with a shaven head and beard and wearing that monk’s robe. This will prevent them from seeing me in the manner of other religions. Also, when those of other religions come to carry me, they won’t be made to lift my body. Rather, those of the same religious practice as myself will carry me away. Also, when they bathe my body, those of other religions will not find the bottom [of the river]. Rather, those of the same religious practice as myself will be able to wash my body. Also, when those of other religions go into the water, they will disturb the fishes and turtles, while those of the same religious practice as myself will not bother them. Also, those of other religions will be unable to cremate my remains. Rather, those of the same religious practice as myself will be able to perform the cremation.” Once he had thought of these five assisting things, he then entered <em>nirvāṇa</em>.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397a19'>時諸外道聞善賢梵志已入涅槃。 將諸音樂幢旛傘蓋，詣拘尸那城，於四衢道告諸人曰： 「汝等當知，彼大沙門喬答摩常作此語： 『唯我法中，有八支聖道、四沙門果。 外道中無⋯廣說如前，乃至⋯ 作師子吼。』 然我法中，同梵行者大師善賢亦得涅槃。 與彼何異？」</td>
    <td id='p323'>323. Then, the adherents of the other religion heard that the ascetic Subhadra had entered <em>nirvāṇa</em>. Singing songs and carrying banners and parasols, they went to Kuśinagarī and addressed people in the streets, “All of you should know, the great ascetic Gautama often said this: ‘The noble eightfold path and four fruits of ascetics are only in my Dharma. They are not found in the other religions … <em>recited in full as before up to</em> … he roared the lions roar.’ Yet, the great teacher Subhadra who was an equal religious practitioner in our Dharma also attained <em>nirvāṇa</em>. How was he any different?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397a25'>諸苾芻曰： 「汝等若言『是我徒侶』，任自持去。」 而諸外道多人共舉竟不能動況能持去。 苾芻告曰： 「汝等不能，我等自舉。」</td>
    <td id='p324'>324. The monks said, “If all of you say, ‘He was our follower,’ then take him yourselves and go.” Many of the people of other religions then [attempted to] lift him up, but they could not move him at all, much less take him away. The monks told them, “All of you are unable to do so, but we can lift him ourselves.”<sup id="ref55"><a href="#n55">55</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397a28'>答曰：「可爾。」 諸苾芻即共舉去，外道默然。</td>
    <td id='p325'>325. They answered, “That may be.” The monks then lifted him up together and departed, and adherents of the other religion were silent.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397a29'>又諸外道來至浴池，諸苾芻曰： 「今可為，汝同梵行者，洗浴其身。」 彼入水時，不得其底。 又被魚鼈之所擾惱，苾芻不爾。 苾芻報曰： 「此若是汝同梵行者，宜自焚燒。」 而諸外道以火焚燒竟不能著。 苾芻然火遂便炎熾。</td>
    <td id='p326'>326. Also, the adherents of another religion brought him to a bathing pool, and the monks said, “Now, you could bathe his body, being of the same religious practice [as he was].” When they entered the water, they could not find the bottom [to stand on]. They also disturbed the fish and turtles, while the monks did not. The monks responded, “If you are of the same religious practice, you ought to cremate him yourselves.” But those adherents of another religion could not cremate him in the end. Then, the monks lit the fire and cremated him.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397b4'>時諸人眾共嗤外道，彼各懷慚，低頭而去。</td>
    <td id='p327'>327. A crowd of people laughed at those of the other religion, each of whom felt ashamed as they left with their heads down.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397b5'>時拘尸那城諸壯士等見此希奇於世尊處，倍生敬仰發淨信心。 各懷戀慕作如是語： 「大悲世尊為最後臥。 現身有疾支節不安。 尚能為彼善賢說法，令速證得阿羅漢果。 復令拘尸那城諸壯士等皆獲善利。」</td>
    <td id='p328'>328. Then the Mallas from Kuśinagarī saw this extraordinary place where the Bhagavān was and redoubled their esteem and thoughts of pure faith in him. They each said this with longing: “The Bhagavān of great compassion has laid down for last time. His body looks ill, and his limbs are not at ease. Yet, he caused Subhadra to quickly realize the fruit of the <em>arhat</em> when he begged him for a Dharma teaching. Once again, the Mallas of Kuśinagarī have all gained a good blessing.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397b10'>時諸苾芻咸皆有疑，請世尊曰： 「如來今時現身有疾支節不安。 尚能令彼善賢梵志出生死海，證阿羅漢。 究竟涅槃，盡諸苦際。」</td>
    <td id='p329'>329. Then, all the monks all were doubtful and asked the Bhagavān, “Today, the Tathāgata’s body looks ill, and his limbs are not at ease. Yet, he caused Subhadra to escape the sea of birth and death and realize the fruit of the <em>arhat</em>. He finally entered <em>nirvāṇa</em> and reached the end of suffering.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397b13'>佛告苾芻： 「汝等當知，此未希有。 我今已斷根本三毒，解脫生、老、病、死、愁、憂、苦、惱。 具一切智，於諸境界得大自在。 令彼善賢出生死海，得最後邊，住涅槃處。 不足為難。 我於往昔在生死中，具貪、瞋、癡，未斷生、老、病、死、憂、悲、苦、惱。 無有智慧能善思量。 在傍生內，尚能為彼善賢梵志及拘尸那城諸壯士等，自捨身命。 我為汝說。 宜應諦聽。</td>
    <td id='p330'>330. The Buddha addressed the monks, “All of you should know that this is something unprecedented. Now, I’ve cut the three root poisons and am liberated from birth, old age, illness, death, sorrow, lamentation, pain, and trouble. I possess all knowledge and have great sovereignty over all the realms. I caused Subhadra to escape the sea of birth and death, reach the final end, and abide in the abode of <em>nirvāṇa</em>. This is not enough to be difficult. In the past, when I was in the sea of birth and death and possessed greed, anger, and delusion, I had yet to end birth, old age, illness, death, sorrow, lamentation, pain, and trouble. I didn’t have the wisdom to be able to think things through with skill. Yet, when I was an animal, I could discard my life for that ascetic Subhadra and the Mallas of Kuśinagarī. I will explain this for you. You ought to listen closely.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397b21'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>35. The Story of the Deer King</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397b21'>「乃往昔時，於大山澤，有一鹿王，千鹿圍繞，依林而住。 有大智慧、預識、機宜。 於所居處獵者來見，而往告王。 時王以兵周遍圍繞，鹿王作念： 『我若不能救濟眾鹿，必被獵人之所屠害。』</td>
    <td id='p331'>331. “There was a time in the past when a deer king lived surrounded by a thousand deer in a forest on a great lush mountain.<sup id="ref56"><a href="#n56">56</a></sup> He had great wisdom, awareness, and good timing. There was a hunter who came and saw where they lived and went back to tell the king. The king was completely surrounded them with an army, and the deer king thought, ‘If I cannot save the herd of deer, surely they will be slaughtered by the hunter!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397b25'>「爾時，鹿王四顧瞻望而作是念： 『我今作何方便，能令群鹿免斯苦厄？』</td>
    <td id='p332'>332. “The deer king looked around hopefully and thought, ‘Now, what means do I have to make an escape for the deer herd from this calamity?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397b26'>「遂見深山下有㵎水駛流出谷。 諸鹿羸弱不能浮趒，鹿王入㵎橫流而住，作大音聲普告群鹿： 『汝等速來！ 可從此岸擲上我背，趒於彼岸。 必得存活！ 若不爾者，當遭屠害。』</td>
    <td id='p333'>333. “He saw a river rapids flowing from a valley deep in the mountains. The deer were too weak to float down it, but the deer king stood in that broad mountain stream and called loudly to the herd: ‘All of you, come quick! You can walk across my back from this side to the other. Surely then you will survive! If you don’t do this, then you’ll be slaughtered!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397c1'>「於是群鹿次第悉踏大鹿王脊。 皆越駛河得離危難。 由諸群鹿蹄甲踐蹋，鹿王皮穿血肉皆盡唯餘脊骨。 雖極苦痛，心無退轉。 悉令群鹿安隱得渡，仍懷顧戀誰未渡者。 於群鹿中有一鹿兒不能趒渡。 爾時，鹿王雖受極苦，尚懷哀念不顧自身。 從水而出遂取鹿兒，置於脊上渡至彼岸。</td>
    <td id='p334'>334. “The deer herd took turns walking over that great deer king’s back. They crossed over the river rapids and were freed from danger. As a result of the deer herd’s hooves treading on his back, the deer king’s skin was punctured and bloodied until only his backbone remained. Although it was very painful, there was no turning back in his mind. The entire deer herd safely crossed over, and his only concern was who had yet to cross over. There was a calf among the herd who was unable to cross the river. The deer king then did not look at his own body out of pity despite it being very painful. He got out of the river and carried the calf across the river to the other shore on his back.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397c7'>「鹿王遍觀知渡盡已，氣力將竭臨命終時而發誓願： 『我救群鹿及此鹿兒。 救濟死厄不惜身命。 願我當來得成無上正等覺時，令彼得渡生死羅網。 置最後邊妙涅槃處。』」</td>
    <td id='p335'>335. “Once he saw that they had all crossed the river, he made this vow when the last of his strength was used up and he was about to die: ‘I saved the deer herd and this baby deer. I saved them from mortal danger, and I didn’t begrudge my own life. I vow that in the future when I achieve the unsurpassed, correct, and complete awakening, I’ll make them cross over the trap of birth and death. I’ll bring them to the final end, that wonderful place of <em>nirvāṇa</em>.’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397c11'>佛告諸苾芻： 「汝意云何？ 勿生異念。 往時鹿王者即我身是。 其群鹿者拘尸那城諸壯士是。 其鹿兒者即善賢是。</td>
    <td id='p336'>336. The Buddha told the monks, “What do you think? Don’t have a different thought [that this]. When he departed, that deer king was an incarnation of myself. His herd of deer were the Mallas of Kuśinagarī. That deer calf was Subhadra.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397c14'>「又諸苾芻，如我無智在傍生內。 喘息不安受諸苦毒。 皮肉支節分解之時，救濟善賢令至無畏。</td>
    <td id='p337'>337. “Also, monks, I had no knowledge while I was inside an animal. My breathing was not calm and I felt terrible pains. When my hide, flesh, limbs, and joints were separated, I saved Subhadra and made him become fearless.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397c16'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>36. The Story of King Brahmadatta’s Horse</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397c16'>「汝等善聽！ 乃往古昔婆羅痆斯時有國王名曰梵授，以法化世⋯廣如經說⋯ 王有智馬預知前事，隣國敬畏。 悉來朝貢。 馬既命終時，諸小王令使報曰： 『汝梵授王今可輸稅分與我等。 若不爾者，不得出城。 如見違者，我等同來，破滅其國。』</td>
    <td id='p338'>338. “All of you, listen well! There once in the past a king of Bārāṇasī named Brahmadatta who educated the world with the Dharma … <em>recited in full as in the</em> sūtra … That king had an intelligent horse who had knowledge of events before they happened, which neighboring countries respected and feared. They all came to give tribute. When the horse died, the lesser kings sent messengers to say, ‘King Brahmadatta, you may pay us tribute now. If you do not, you cannot leave the city. We will all come and destroy your kingdom just as though we saw an enemy!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397c21'>「王告使曰： 『我不送稅亦不出城。』 遂於國內訪求智馬，後於異處遂便獲得。 時屬春序，卉木敷榮，群鳥和鳴甚可愛樂。 王乘智馬，將諸婇女，遊適芳園，歡娛受樂。</td>
    <td id='p339'>339. “The king told the messenger, ‘I will not pay you tribute, nor will I leave the city.’ He went into the country in search of an intelligent horse, and he caught one after chasing it in a different place. As was typical during spring, the plants and trees were growing luxuriantly and flocks of birds were singing delightfully. The king rode the intelligent horse, leading his harem women during tours of his garden, which made them happy and delighted.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397c25'>「時諸小王聞梵授王與諸臣佐及宮婇女在外遊戲，情無所懼。 未即入城相與謀計，各嚴四兵至城門首。</td>
    <td id='p340'>340. “The lesser kings heard that King Brahmadatta, his ministers, and harem women were outside touring without any anxiety. Before they returned to the city, the kings devised a plan, and each of them prepared fourfold armies to march on the city gates.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397c27'>「大臣白王： 『諸小國王不恭朝命。敢興逆亂，來扣城門。 願見警備。』</td>
    <td id='p341'>341. “The prime minister said to the king, ‘The lesser kings did not pay tribute when ordered to do so. Daring to rebel against you, they have come knocking on the city gates. Please prepare yourself!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397c28'>「王既聞已，勅索智馬： 『速嚴四兵。 我自討擊。』</td>
    <td id='p342'>342. “Once he heard that, the king ordered his intelligent horse, ‘Quickly, ready the fourfold army. I will lead the attack myself!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.397c29'>「時王乘馬嚴兵，誓眾共彼鬪戰。 王恃威力，獨處先鋒。 遂被賊軍以槊中馬，腸胃皆出受諸楚毒。 眾苦難堪，形命無幾。 仍作是念： 『王遭困厄。 我若不救是所不應。 宜忍苦楚，令王免厄。 得至城門，到無畏處。』</td>
    <td id='p343'>343. “The king then mounted his horse, equipped himself for battle, and vowed that he would go fight them. The king relied on his majestic strength, standing alone at the vanguard. Consequently, his horse was struck by a barbed arrow from the rebel army, and it was seriously wounded in its abdomen. The many pains were difficult to bear, and it would not live much longer. Thereupon, the horse thought, ‘Misfortune has befallen the king. It would be irresponsible if I didn’t save him. I ought to endure these torments to allow the king to escape this disaster. We can reach the city gate and go to a place where there’s nothing to fear.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398a5'>「作是念已周迴顧望無入城路。 然此城外有大浴池名曰妙梵，近王宮闕。 於其池中有四蓮華，青、黃、赤、白，皆悉遍滿。 于時，智馬不顧身命，騰躍池中踐荷葉上。 負王渡難直入宮中。時王纔下，馬便命絕。</td>
    <td id='p344'>344. “Having had this thought, the horse circled around looking carefully, but there was no road going into the city. There was, however, a large lake called Wondrous Brahma near the royal palace entrance outside of the city. Four kinds of lotus flowers, which were blue, yellow, red, and white, pervaded that lake. Thereupon, the intelligent horse leapt over the lake without regard for its own life and walked on the lotus pads. It carried the king across with difficulty, going straight into the palace. The horse then died when the king dismounted.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398a9'>「時諸小王競入園林，處處尋覓，竟不能得。 迴軍劫掠，各還本居。 時梵授王既免危厄，得存性命。 告婆羅痆斯諸大臣等及眾人曰： 『若有能救剎帝利灌頂大王命者，如何恩賞？』</td>
    <td id='p345'>345. “The lesser kings entered the garden park then, searching everywhere but not finding him in the end. They turned their armies to plunder, and then each returned to their home. King Brahmadatta had escaped that danger and survived with his life. He addressed the prime ministers of Bārāṇasī and a crowd of people, ‘If someone saves the life of a great head-anointed warrior king, how should that favor be rewarded?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398a13'>「諸臣白王： 『可分半國。』</td>
    <td>“The ministers said to the king, ‘You could give them half of the kingdom.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398a14'>「王曰： 『此之智馬能全我命，馬今既死。 欲何以報？』</td>
    <td id='p346'>346. “The king asked, ‘This intelligent horse was able to preserve my life, but now the horse has died. What should be its reward?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398a15'>「諸臣答言： 『應為智馬於城四門宜作非時白蓮華會。 廣行惠施，盛修福業以資魂路。』</td>
    <td id='p347'>347. “The ministers replied, ‘It should be appropriate to conduct an out of season white lotus flowers convention at the four gates of the city for the intelligent horse. Then travel broadly focused on generosity and cultivate meritorious deeds that will assist its spirit on the road.’ </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398a17'>「王言： 『甚善！宜時疾作。』</td>
    <td>“The king said, ‘Very good! It would be a good time to do this quickly.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398a17'>「時王即令太子、中宮、婇女、臣、佐吏、民莊嚴衢路。 布列香華、幡、蓋、明燈，在處懸設無不充滿。 如歡喜園甚可愛樂。 王令擊鼓宣告遠近： 『我於明日欲為智馬於城四門營建非時白蓮華會。 宜可告知，集法場所，受我供養。』 時至雲集，隨須給與。 普令稱意。</td>
    <td id='p348'>348. “The king then bade his princes, concubines, ministers, servants, and people in the middle palace to decorate the street intersections. They put out rows of fragrant flowers, banners, parasols, and bright lamps, hanging them so that no place was not filled with them. The roads were as delightful as a pleasure park. The king then bade them beat a drum and announce to everyone far and near: ‘Tomorrow, I will convene an out of season white lotus flower convention at the four gates for that intelligent horse. It would be good to tell those you know to gather at the Dharma platform to receive my offerings!’ Then, people arrived and gathered like clouds and were given whatever they needed. Everyone was bade to tell him what they had in mind.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398a23'>「汝等苾芻於意云何？ 彼時智馬即我身是。 我為彼王受諸苦楚，身形分解不顧身命。 尚能救濟令離危厄。」</td>
    <td id='p349'>349. “What do you monks think? At that time, that intelligent horse was me. I had accepted that suffering for that king, and I had no regard for my own life when my body was taken apart. Still, I was able to save him and keep him away from misfortune.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398a26'>時諸苾芻又復有疑，請世尊曰： 「大德，具壽善賢先作何業，今為大師最後弟子？」</td>
    <td id='p350'>350. Then, the monks had another doubt and asked the Bhagavān, “Venerable, what deeds had the elder Subhadra done to be the great teacher’s last disciple today?”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398a27'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>37. The Story of the Monk Aśoka</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398a27'>佛告諸苾芻： 「汝等當知，自所作業今還自受⋯ 廣如餘處乃至說頌⋯ 汝等苾芻，乃往古昔此賢劫中，人壽二萬歲時，有佛出世名迦攝波。 十號具足，在婆羅痆斯仙人墮處施鹿林中。 時彼如來、應、正等覺，有外孫子名曰無憂，求解脫故而為出家。 謂解脫果自然可得，於八正道而不勤修。 經歷多時竟無果證。 遊行人間隨處作夏。 時彼如來有緣皆度，所作已辦。 如薪盡火滅，於其中夜將入涅槃。</td>
    <td id='p351'>351. The Buddha told the monks, “All of you should know, the deeds that he has done have now returned for him to experience … <em>and so on, the remainder is told in full up to speaking in verse</em> … All of you monks, there was a Buddha, a Bhagavān, named Kāśyapa in ancient times during this fortunate eon when people lived for 20,000 years. He had accomplished the ten epithets and was at the Place Where Sages Fell in the Deer Preserve near Bārāṇasī. At the time, that Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One had a matrilineal grandson named Aśoka who left home to pursue liberation. He called it the fruit of liberation that’s naturally obtained without endeavoring on the eightfold path. After many seasons had passed, he had realized no fruit in the end. He wandered among the people whenever it was summer there. That Tathāgata was liberated from being conditioned and had accomplished the task. Like a fire that goes out when its wood runs out, he entered <em>nirvāṇa</em> in the middle of the night.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398b7'>「時彼苾芻在無憂樹下，而此樹神聞迦攝波如來當般涅槃。 悲泣雨淚，霑無憂身。 苾芻仰觀問其神曰： 『有何所以如是悲啼？』</td>
    <td id='p352'>352. “Then, that monk was under an ashoka tree, and the spirit of that tree heard that Tathāgata Kāśyapa was going to enter <em>parinirvāṇa</em>. Its tears rained down as it cried, which made Aśoka wet. The monk looked up and asked the spirit, ‘What is there to be crying about?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398b10'>「樹神對曰： 『今日中夜，迦攝波佛將入涅槃。』</td>
    <td>“The tree spirit replied, ‘The Buddha Kāśyapa will enter <em>nirvāṇa</em> in the middle of the night tonight!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398b11'>「時彼苾芻聞如是語，情懷痛切如箭入心，悲啼號哭，發聲大喚。 樹神問曰： 『何故悲啼？』</td>
    <td id='p353'>353. “Hearing this said, that monk felt a pain like an arrow had pierced his heart. He lamented, cried out, and raised his voice loudly. The tree spirit asked him, ‘Why are you crying?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398b13'>「對曰： 『迦攝波如來、應、正等覺是我親舅！ 我雖依附而不勤修。 去此既遠。 難申禮敬。 我是凡夫，無力速往。 是以悲哭。』</td>
    <td id='p354'>354. “He replied, ‘Tathāgata Kāśyapa the Arhat and Completely Awakened One is my maternal grandfather! I have not made the effort to cultivate even though I relied on him. Now he is departing, and he’s too far away. It would be difficult to pay my respects. I am an ordinary man without the power to go there quickly. This is why I am crying.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398b15'>「樹神報曰： 『然我有力令仁疾至。 不知見佛得有益不？』</td>
    <td id='p355'>355. “The tree spirit replied, ‘But I have the power to make you go there swiftly. Do you not know that seeing a <em>buddha</em> gains blessings?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398b16'>「苾芻報曰： 『我極勇猛。 若見佛者，必能依行，證獲果利。』</td>
    <td id='p356'>356. “The monks replied, ‘I am very courageous. Someone who sees a <em>buddha</em> surely can realize fruitful benefits as a result of doing so!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398b18'>「是時，樹神以神通力，將此苾芻疾至佛所。 既見佛已，發清淨心，起廣大願。 時彼如來隨其根性，為說妙法，證阿羅漢果。 不忍見佛入般涅槃，是故於先而取滅度。</td>
    <td id='p357'>357. “The tree spirit then used its miraculous power to bring this monk swiftly to where the Buddha was. Upon seeing the Buddha, he made a pure resolve and made a grand vow. That Tathāgata then taught him the marvelous Dharma according to his faculties and disposition, and he realize the fruit of an <em>arhat</em>. He could not bear to see the Buddha enter <em>parinirvāṇa</em>, so he chose to be extinguished before him.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398b21'>「時彼樹神既見世尊及苾芻涅槃已，情懷戀慕，作如是念： 『今此具壽所獲勝利皆由我得。 以此功德，願我來世迦攝波佛所授摩納婆記。 人壽百歲得成正覺號釋迦牟尼。 彼涅槃時，我得聲聞無學果已在先滅度。』」</td>
    <td id='p358'>358. “Having seen the Bhagavān and the monk <em>nirvāṇa</em>, that tree spirit longed for them and thought, ‘Now, this elder has obtained an excellent blessing because of me. Please let this merit make me a trusted student of a future Buddha Kāśyapa. When people live for a hundred years, the one who obtains complete awakening will be called Śākyamuni. At the time of his <em>nirvāṇa</em>, I will be extinguished before him after I become his disciple and obtain the fruit of one with nothing more to learn.’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398b26'>佛告諸苾芻： 「於汝意云何？ 時天神者今善賢是。 由是義故，於一切時，遠離惡友，近善知識，應如是學。」</td>
    <td id='p359'>359. The Buddha told the monks, “What do you think? At the time, that heavenly spirit was Subhadra in the present. From this meaning, you should train yourselves by remaining far away from bad friends and close to good associates at all times.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398b29'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>38. Good Friends Are the Whole of the Religious Practice</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398b29'>時阿難陀白佛言： 「世尊，我於靜處作如是念： 『善知識者是半梵行，諸修行者，由善友力方能成辦。 得善友故，遠離惡友。 以是義故，方知善友是半梵行。』」</td>
    <td id='p360'>360. Ānanda then said to the Buddha, “Bhagavān, while I was in a quiet place, I had this thought: ‘Good friends are half the religious practice, for those who cultivate themselves are able to accomplish that due to the strength and means of their good friends. One stays far away from bad friends when they find good friends. By this meaning, we have the means to know that good friends are half of the religious practice.’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398c3'>佛言： 「阿難陀勿作是語： 『善知識者是半梵行。』 何以故？ 善知識者是全梵行。 由此便能離惡知識，不造諸惡常修眾善。 純一、清白、具足、圓滿梵行之相。 由是因緣若得善伴與其同住⋯乃至⋯涅槃事無不辦。 故名全梵行。 何以故？ 阿難陀，我由善知識故，令諸有情於生、老、病、死、憂、悲、苦、惱皆得解脫。 若離善友無如是事。 阿難陀，於我所說應勤修學。」</td>
    <td id='p361'>361. The Buddha said, “Ānanda, don’t say, ‘Good friends are half the religious practice.’ And why? Good friends are the whole of the religious practice. It’s because of good friends that one can part with bad friends, not do evil things, and always cultivate the many virtues. These are signs of the unadulterated, clean, perfect, and fulfilled religious practice. Because of these causes and conditions, one who finds good associates to share a common abode … <em>up to</em> … <em>nirvāṇa</em>, there’s nothing one cannot accomplish. Therefore, they are called the whole of the religious practice. And why? Ānanda, it was because of good friends that I could liberate sentient beings from birth, old age, illness, death, sorrow, lamentation, pain, and trouble. If it weren’t for good friends, there would be no such thing [as freedom from suffering]. Ānanda, you should endeavor to cultivate and train in what I have taught.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398c12'>爾時佛告諸苾芻曰： 「由是義故，從今已去，不應輒度外道出家，并受近圓，除釋迦種、及事火留髻外道。 若披外道服來求出家及受近圓者，問無障法，此人應與。 何以故？ 此是我親有機緣故。 其事火人說有業用，有因有緣，有策勵果故，此等不勞共住。 即與出家并受近圓。 若是自餘外道之類，來求出家及近圓者，其親教師應與衣服、食，僧常食四月共住。 若觀其人，性行調柔，堪濟度者。 應與出家并近圓事。 如是應知。</td>
    <td id='p362'>362. The Buddha then addressed the monks,<sup id="ref57"><a href="#n57">57</a></sup> “Because of this meaning, up until now, you would not immediately give the going forth to a renunciate of another religion or fully ordain them except for a member of the Śākya clan, a fire tender, or a long-haired ascetic of another religion. If they put on the robes of another religion and come seeking to leave home and be fully ordained, or they ask about the unobstructed Dharma, these people should be permitted. And why? Because they possess the circumstances to be our friends. Because those fire tenders teach that there are deeds that are operative, that there are causes and conditions, and there is the instigation of results, they do not toil for a living. Therefore, they are allowed to leave home and be fully ordained. If anyone from other types of religions come wanting to leave home and be fully ordained, a friendly teaching and teacher should permit clothing and meals, and the assembly should regularly eat together during the fourth month. If a person is investigated and found to behave in a disciplined and flexible way, they are capable of being liberated. They should be permitted to leave home and be fully ordained. They should be known in this way.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.398c22'>「復次汝等苾芻，若法能於現在及未來世生長利樂者，汝等應當受、持、讀、誦、為他演說，勿使廢忘。 欲令梵行得久住世，安樂人天利樂饒益諸眾生故。 此法是何？ 所謂契經、應頌、記別、諷頌、自說、因緣、本事、本生、方廣、希有、譬喻、論議。 此十二分教，若能受持讀誦如說行者，能於現未生長利樂⋯乃至⋯ 慈愍群生佛法久住。 汝等苾芻，我涅槃後，作如是念： 『我於今日無有大師。』 汝等不應起如是見，我令汝等每於半月說波羅底木叉。 當知此則是汝大師、是汝依處。 若我住世無有異也。</td>
    <td id='p363'>363. “Furthermore, all of you monks, if a Dharma can produce blessings and happiness for a long time in present and future lives, then you monks ought to accept, maintain, read, recite, and expound it for others without causing any of it to be forgotten. This will cause the religious practice to remain in the world for a long time, bring solace to humans and gods and beneficial happiness and blessings to sentient beings. What is this Dharma? It is the old <em>sūtra</em>s, responding verses, elaborations, songs, lectures, histories, legendary events, legendary births, expansions, wonders, parables, and commentaries. If these twelve divisions of the teaching can be accepted, maintained, read, recited, and put into practice, it can produce blessings and happiness for a long time in present and future lives … <em>up to</em> … Compassion for all living things will make the Buddha’s teachings last a long time. After my <em>nirvāṇa</em>, all of you monks will think, ‘Today, we have no great teacher!’ But you should not produce such a view, for I will have you recite the Pratimokṣa every half-month. You should know this to be your great teaching, that this is your place of refuge. It will be no different than if I were still in the world.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.399a3'>「又始從今日，小下苾芻於長宿處不應喚其氏族姓字。 應喚『大德』，或云『具壽』。 老大苾芻應喚小者為『具壽』。 然大苾芻於小者處應可存情哀憐覆護，生慈念心。 或以衣、鉢、鉢絡、腰絛，共相濟給，勿令闕事。 或復教授讀誦禪思，使有日益。 如是能令我法增長。 若不爾者，法當速滅。</td>
    <td id='p364'>364. “Also beginning today, the junior monks should called seniors by their family or clan names in their presence. They should address them as ‘venerable’ or ‘elder.’ The old senior monks should call the juniors ‘elder.’ Still, the senior monks should preserve their sympathy and protectiveness for the juniors by having thoughts of kindness. If they distribute robes, bowls, bowl bags, and waist cords, they should do so equally and not hide them away. Or they will make them improve daily by teaching, reading, reciting, and meditating. They can make my Dharma grow in this way. If they don’t do so, the Dharma will quickly cease.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.399a9'>「又汝等苾芻，此地方所有其四處，若有淨信男子女人⋯乃至⋯盡形常應繫念生恭敬心。 云何為四？ 一謂佛生處。 二、成正覺處。 三、轉法輪處。 四、入大涅槃處。 若能於此四處或自親禮，或遙致敬企念虔誠，生清淨信，常繫心者，命終之後必，得生天。」</td>
    <td id='p365'>365. “Also, all of you monks, there are four places where men and women of pure faith … <em>up to</em> … should regularly tie their thoughts to reverence for their entire lives. What are the four? The first is the Buddha’s birthplace. Second is the place where he was completely awakened. Third is the place where he turned the Dharma wheel. Fourth is the place where he entered the great <em>nirvāṇa</em>. If they can personally venerate these four places or pay their respects from a distance with hope and sincerity and they constantly tie their minds to producing pure faith, then they will surely be born in heaven after they die.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.399a15'>(比於西方，親見如來一代五十餘年居止之處，有其八所： 一、本生處。 二、成道處。 三、轉法輪處。 四、鷲峯山處。 五、廣嚴城處。 六、從天下處。 七、祇樹園處。 八、雙林涅槃處。 四是定處，餘皆不定。</td>
    <td>(<em>Compare this to the West,<sup id="ref58"><a href="#n58">58</a></sup> where the places Tathāgata personally lived for a period of over fifty years are eight locations: First was his birthplace. Second was the place where he realized his awakening. Third was the place he turned the Dharma wheel. Fourth was the place at Mount Gṛdhrakūṭa. Fifth was the place at the city of Vaiśālī. Sixth was the place where he descended from heaven. Seventh was the park in Jeta’s Grove. Eighth was the place were he <em>nirvāṇa</em>-d between a pair of trees. Four of these places are certain, but the others are uncertain.</em></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.399a16'>總攝頌曰：</td>
    <td>The general summary verse says:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1451.24.399a16'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「生、成、法、鷲、<br/>
    廣、下、祇、林：<br/>
    虔誠一想，<br/>
    福勝千金。」)</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li><em>“Birth, achievement, Dharma, Gṛdhrakūṭa,<br/>
    Vaiśālī, descent, Jeta, and the forest:<br/>
    Perceived once with sincerity,<br/>
    Their fortune will be more than a thousand gold.”</em>)</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.399a18'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>39. The Buddha Enters Parinirvāṇa</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.399a18'>復次，佛告諸苾芻： 「汝等有疑今悉應問，若於佛法、僧、寶，苦、集、滅、道四聖諦處。 有疑問者我當為答。」</td>
    <td id='p366'>366. Furthermore, the Buddha told the monks, “All of you who have doubts should ask questions now, whether it’s about the Buddha, Dharma, or Saṅgha, or points about the four noble truths of suffering, its formation, its cessation, or the path. I will answer questions by those with doubts.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.399a21'>時具壽阿難陀白佛言： 「世尊，如我今者解佛所說，命諸苾芻有疑當問。 然此眾中，竟無一人，於佛、法、僧寶，苦、集、滅、道諦，有懷疑惑，更須問者。」</td>
    <td id='p367'>367. The elder Ānanda then said to the Buddha, “Bhagavān, as I understand what the Buddha has said, he has ordered the monks who have doubts to ask questions. But there isn’t a single person in this assembly who has any doubts about the Buddha, Dharma, and Saṅgha treasures or the four noble truths of suffering, formation, cessation, and the path about which they need to ask questions.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1451.24.399a24'>佛言： 「善哉，善哉！ 阿難陀，汝能如實通達作如是語。 於此眾內我以智觀，於諦、寶中實無疑者。 此是如來最後所作。」</td>
    <td id='p368'>368. The Buddha replied, “Good, good! Ānanda, this penetrating statement you’ve made is true. I have observed those in this assembly with wisdom, and there are none who have doubts about these treasures or truths. This is the final task of a <em>tathāgata</em>.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
